# Droplet Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Build Droplet, a SwiftUI iOS app with a Supabase backend. It rounds up settled Plaid Sandbox purchases to the next dollar and, at a threshold, records a simulated investment into a crypto asset at the live True Markets price.

**Architecture:**
- **Backend:** Supabase Postgres holds per-user data behind RLS. Two `security definer` SQL functions do the money-moving work atomically: `apply_transactions` and `sweep_drops`. Deno Edge Functions talk to Plaid Sandbox and to True Markets' public price endpoint, and are the only writers.
- **iOS app:** reads its own rows through supabase-swift and calls Edge Functions for everything else. A `DropletAPI` protocol separates the UI from Supabase, so previews and tests run on an in-memory fake.

**Tech Stack:**
- **iOS:** Swift 6, SwiftUI (iOS 26 SDK, Liquid Glass), Swift Charts, Swift Testing, XcodeGen; supabase-swift **2.55.4** and Plaid LinkKit **7.2.3** (pinned exactly).
- **Backend:** Supabase CLI 2.122 (Postgres 17, pgTAP, pg_cron, pg_net, Vault), Deno 2 Edge Functions, `npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2.118.0`, `npm:jose@6.2.13` (pinned inline; `jsr:@std/assert@1.0.19` in tests).

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-24-droplet-design.md`

## Global Constraints

- iOS deployment target **26.0**. Build with Xcode 27.1, `SWIFT_VERSION = 6.0`. Test destination `platform=iOS Simulator,name=iPhone 17`.
- No default-actor-isolation build setting. Mark UI state classes `@MainActor @Observable final class`. Model structs are `Codable, Sendable`.
- SPM dependencies, pinned with `exactVersion` in `ios/project.yml`: `https://github.com/supabase/supabase-swift` **2.55.4** (product `Supabase`) and `https://github.com/plaid/plaid-link-ios-spm` **7.2.3** (product `LinkKit`). `Package.resolved` lives inside the gitignored `.xcodeproj`, so the pins are what keep fresh clones reproducible. The test target must **not** link `Supabase` a second time: tests import it through the host app.
- **Money:** always integer **cents** (`Int` in Swift, `bigint`/`int` in SQL, `number` integers in TS). Asset price and quantity are `numeric(38,18)` in SQL and **strings** in every JSON payload.
- **JSON keys:** `snake_case` on the wire. Swift uses `JSONDecoder.droplet` / `JSONEncoder.droplet` (snake-case conversion) everywhere.
- **Round-up rule:** `roundup = amount_cents <= 0 ? 0 : (100 - amount_cents % 100) % 100`. Only settled (`pending == false`) USD purchases count, and only those dated on or after `plaid_items.linked_on`. Excluded categories: `TRANSFER_IN`, `TRANSFER_OUT`, `LOAN_PAYMENTS`, `BANK_FEES`, `INCOME`.
- **Threshold options:** `200 | 500 | 1000` cents; default `500`. Default asset `SOL`. Curated assets: `SOL, ETH, CBBTC, AAVE, JUP, RENDER`.
- **Trades are simulated only.** `investments.mode = 'simulated'`. No True Markets authenticated call, key or signing code anywhere in this build.
- **Secrets** (Plaid client id and secret, `CRON_SECRET`, service role key) live only in `supabase/functions/.env` (local, gitignored) or in Supabase function secrets (remote). The app gets only the Supabase URL and the publishable/anon key, through the gitignored `ios/Droplet/Resources/Config.xcconfig`.
- **Price source:** `GET https://api.truemarkets.co/v1/defi/market/prices?symbol=<SYMBOL>`. Take the candle where `interval == "30s"` and use its `closePrice` (string). Timeout 5 s.
- **Plaid base URL** comes from `PLAID_ENV`: `sandbox` → `https://sandbox.plaid.com`, `production` → `https://production.plaid.com`. `simulate-purchase` refuses unless `PLAID_ENV == 'sandbox'`.
- **"Today"** for Plaid dates and `linked_on` is the `America/New_York` calendar date, formatted `YYYY-MM-DD`.
- **UI copy:**
  - Tabs: "Portfolio", "Spending", "Profile".
  - Accumulated money is "drops". Pending total: "$3.21 of $5.00", with the caption "until your next drop".
  - Investments show a "Simulated" capsule label.
- **Design rules:**
  - Springs only: `Theme.spring = .spring(response: 0.4, dampingFraction: 1.0)`; sheets use `.spring(response: 0.3, dampingFraction: 0.8)`.
  - Press scale 0.97.
  - Glass only on chrome (tab bar, accessory, floating buttons), never on content cards.
  - Accent `#2F7BF6` light / `#5A9BFF` dark.
  - Hero numbers: 56 pt semibold, tracking −1.1, `.monospacedDigit()`.
  - Haptics only for success (an investment), error and selection.
- **Commits:** `git commit` once per task on branch `feat/droplet-app`. End every message with the trailer line `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`. Before each commit, run `git diff --cached | grep -iE "secret|access-sandbox|access-production"` and make sure no secret values appear.

## Shared Contracts (every task must match these exactly; C6 lists the verified additive members)

### C1. Edge Function HTTP contracts

All functions take `POST` with a JSON body and return JSON. Errors always have this shape:
`{"error":{"code":"<CODE>","message":"<human text>"}}`

| Code | HTTP | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| `UNAUTHORIZED` | 401 | missing or invalid user JWT / cron secret / webhook signature |
| `BAD_REQUEST` | 400 | invalid body |
| `NO_BANK` | 404 | the user has no `plaid_items` row |
| `ITEM_LOGIN_REQUIRED` | 409 | Plaid says the user must re-link |
| `NOT_SANDBOX` | 403 | `simulate-purchase` when `PLAID_ENV != 'sandbox'` |
| `PLAID_ERROR` | 502 | any other Plaid API error (message = Plaid `error_message`) |
| `INTERNAL` | 500 | anything else |

| Function | Caller auth | Request body | 200 response body |
|---|---|---|---|
| `plaid-link-token` | user JWT | `{}` | `{"link_token":"link-sandbox-…","expiration":"2026-10-24T20:00:00Z"}` |
| `plaid-exchange` | user JWT | `{"public_token":"public-sandbox-…","institution_name":"First Platypus Bank"}` (`institution_name` may be `null`) | `SyncResult` |
| `plaid-sync` | user JWT | `{}` | `SyncResult` |
| `simulate-purchase` | user JWT | `{"merchant":"Blue Bottle","amount_cents":567}` (`merchant` 1–60 chars, `amount_cents` 1–100000) | `SyncResult` |
| `plaid-webhook` | Plaid `Plaid-Verification` JWT | Plaid webhook JSON | `{"ok":true}` |
| `sync-all` | header `x-cron-secret: <CRON_SECRET>` | `{}` | `{"items":3,"failed":0}` |

`SyncResult` and `Investment` (JSON):
```json
{"added":2,"modified":0,"removed":0,
 "investment":{"id":"6f1c…","asset_symbol":"SOL","amount_cents":528,
   "price_usd":"99.366035688829","quantity":"0.053137…","mode":"simulated",
   "created_at":"2026-10-24T16:03:11.482Z"}}
```
`investment` is `null` when the sync did not trigger a sweep.

### C2. Database objects (created in Task 2; the SQL in Task 2 is authoritative)
- **Tables:** `public.assets`, `public.profiles`, `public.plaid_items`, `public.bank_connections`, `public.investments`, `public.transactions`.
- **Views** (security_invoker):
  - `public.drop_summary(user_id, pending_cents, threshold_cents, lifetime_roundup_cents, invested_cents)`
  - `public.portfolio_by_asset(user_id, asset_symbol, asset_name, icon_url, invested_cents, quantity text, last_invested_at)`
- **RPCs** (service_role only):
  - `public.apply_transactions(p_user uuid, p_rows jsonb, p_removed text[]) returns void`
  - `public.sweep_drops(p_user uuid, p_asset text, p_price numeric) returns table(id uuid, asset_symbol text, amount_cents bigint, price_usd text, quantity text, mode text, created_at timestamptz)`
- **`p_rows` element shape** (produced by `toTransactionRow` in TS):
  `{"plaid_transaction_id":"…","merchant_name":"…","amount_cents":567,"iso_currency_code":"USD","date":"2026-10-24","pending":false,"category":"FOOD_AND_DRINK"|null,"roundup_cents":33,"roundup_status":"pending"|"skipped"}`

### C3. TypeScript module exports (`supabase/functions/_shared`)
```ts
// http.ts
export class HttpError extends Error { constructor(public status: number, public code: string, message: string) }
export function json(body: unknown, status?: number): Response
export function errorResponse(err: unknown): Response          // HttpError → its status/code; anything else → 500 INTERNAL
export async function readJson<T>(req: Request): Promise<T>     // throws HttpError(400,'BAD_REQUEST')
// env.ts
export function requireEnv(name: string): string                // throws Error if missing
export function plaidBaseUrl(env?: string): string
export function todayInNewYork(now?: Date): string              // 'YYYY-MM-DD'
// db.ts
export function adminClient(): SupabaseClient                   // service role, no session persistence
export async function requireUser(req: Request, admin: SupabaseClient): Promise<string>  // returns user id or throws HttpError(401,'UNAUTHORIZED')
// roundup.ts
export const EXCLUDED_CATEGORIES: ReadonlySet<string>
export function roundupCents(amountCents: number): number
export interface PlaidTransaction { transaction_id: string; amount: number; iso_currency_code: string | null; date: string; pending: boolean; merchant_name?: string | null; name?: string | null; personal_finance_category?: { primary?: string | null } | null }
export interface TransactionRow { plaid_transaction_id: string; merchant_name: string; amount_cents: number; iso_currency_code: string; date: string; pending: boolean; category: string | null; roundup_cents: number; roundup_status: 'pending' | 'skipped' }
export function toTransactionRow(t: PlaidTransaction, linkedOn: string): TransactionRow | null   // null when t.date < linkedOn
// prices.ts
export async function fetchPrice(symbol: string, fetchFn?: typeof fetch): Promise<string>   // decimal string; throws on failure
// plaid.ts
export class PlaidApiError extends Error { constructor(public errorCode: string, public errorType: string, message: string, public status: number) }
export class PlaidClient {
  constructor(opts: { clientId: string; secret: string; baseUrl: string; fetchFn?: typeof fetch })
  static fromEnv(fetchFn?: typeof fetch): PlaidClient
  linkTokenCreate(args: { userId: string; webhook?: string }): Promise<{ link_token: string; expiration: string }>
  exchangePublicToken(publicToken: string): Promise<{ access_token: string; item_id: string }>
  transactionsSync(accessToken: string, cursor: string | null): Promise<{ added: PlaidTransaction[]; modified: PlaidTransaction[]; removed: { transaction_id: string }[]; next_cursor: string; has_more: boolean }>
  sandboxTransactionsCreate(accessToken: string, txns: { date_transacted: string; date_posted: string; amount: number; description: string }[]): Promise<void>
  webhookVerificationKey(keyId: string): Promise<JsonWebKey>
}
// sync.ts
export interface SyncResult { added: number; modified: number; removed: number; investment: InvestmentJson | null }
export interface InvestmentJson { id: string; asset_symbol: string; amount_cents: number; price_usd: string; quantity: string; mode: string; created_at: string }
export interface PlaidItemRecord { id: string; user_id: string; item_id: string; access_token: string; cursor: string | null; linked_on: string; status: string }
export interface SyncStore {
  applyTransactions(userId: string, rows: TransactionRow[], removedIds: string[]): Promise<void>
  saveCursor(itemRowId: string, userId: string, cursor: string): Promise<void>    // also bank_connections.last_synced_at = now(), status 'active'
  setStatus(itemRowId: string, userId: string, status: 'active' | 'login_required' | 'error'): Promise<void>
  pendingAndThreshold(userId: string): Promise<{ pendingCents: number; thresholdCents: number; targetAsset: string; paused: boolean }>
  sweep(userId: string, asset: string, price: string): Promise<InvestmentJson | null>
}
export interface SyncDeps { plaid: Pick<PlaidClient, 'transactionsSync'>; store: SyncStore; price: (symbol: string) => Promise<string> }
export async function syncItem(deps: SyncDeps, item: PlaidItemRecord): Promise<SyncResult>
export function supabaseSyncStore(admin: SupabaseClient): SyncStore
export async function loadItemForUser(admin: SupabaseClient, userId: string): Promise<PlaidItemRecord>  // throws HttpError(404,'NO_BANK')
// sweep.ts
export async function sweepIfReady(store: SyncStore, price: (s: string) => Promise<string>, userId: string): Promise<InvestmentJson | null>   // never throws on price failure (returns null)
// webhook.ts
export async function verifyPlaidWebhook(rawBody: string, verificationHeader: string | null, getKey: (kid: string) => Promise<JsonWebKey>, nowSeconds?: number): Promise<boolean>
```

### C4. Swift types (Core/, produced by Tasks 10–12, consumed by every UI task)
```swift
// Models.swift
struct Asset: Codable, Sendable, Hashable, Identifiable { let symbol: String; let name: String; let chain: String; let iconUrl: URL?; let sortOrder: Int; var id: String { symbol } }
struct Profile: Codable, Sendable, Equatable { let id: UUID; var displayName: String; var thresholdCents: Int; var targetAsset: String; var roundupsPaused: Bool }
struct ProfilePatch: Encodable, Sendable, Equatable { var displayName: String? = nil; var thresholdCents: Int? = nil; var targetAsset: String? = nil; var roundupsPaused: Bool? = nil }
enum BankStatus: String, Codable, Sendable { case active; case loginRequired = "login_required"; case error }
struct BankConnection: Codable, Sendable, Equatable { let institutionName: String?; let status: BankStatus; let lastSyncedAt: Date? }
enum RoundupStatus: String, Codable, Sendable { case pending, invested, skipped, reversed }
struct Transaction: Codable, Sendable, Identifiable, Equatable {
  let id: UUID; let merchantName: String; let amountCents: Int; let date: String /* yyyy-MM-dd */
  let pending: Bool; let roundupCents: Int; let roundupStatus: RoundupStatus; let createdAt: Date
  var day: Date { get }   // date parsed in Calendar.current at local midnight; falls back to createdAt
}
struct DropSummary: Codable, Sendable, Equatable {
  let pendingCents: Int; let thresholdCents: Int; let lifetimeRoundupCents: Int; let investedCents: Int
  var progress: Double { get }        // min(1, pending / max(threshold,1))
  var remainingCents: Int { get }     // max(0, threshold - pending)
  static let empty: DropSummary       // (0, 500, 0, 0)
}
struct Holding: Codable, Sendable, Identifiable, Equatable { let assetSymbol: String; let assetName: String; let iconUrl: URL?; let investedCents: Int; let quantity: String; let lastInvestedAt: Date?; var id: String { assetSymbol } }
struct Investment: Codable, Sendable, Equatable, Identifiable { let id: UUID; let assetSymbol: String; let amountCents: Int; let priceUsd: String; let quantity: String; let mode: String; let createdAt: Date }
struct SyncResult: Codable, Sendable, Equatable { let added: Int; let modified: Int; let removed: Int; let investment: Investment? }
// Coding.swift
extension JSONDecoder { static var droplet: JSONDecoder { get } }   // .convertFromSnakeCase; dates: ISO-8601 with/without fractional seconds, "yyyy-MM-dd"
extension JSONEncoder { static var droplet: JSONEncoder { get } }   // .convertToSnakeCase; ISO-8601 dates
// Money.swift
enum Money {
  static func format(cents: Int) -> String                 // 567 -> "$5.67", 0 -> "$0.00", -567 -> "-$5.67"
  static func formatSigned(cents: Int) -> String           // 33 -> "+$0.33"
  static func roundupCents(forAmountCents: Int) -> Int     // mirrors server rule
  static func formatQuantity(_ decimal: String, maxFractionDigits: Int = 6) -> String  // "0.053137000000000000" -> "0.053137"
}
// UserFacingError.swift
struct UserFacingError: Error, Equatable, Identifiable, Sendable {
  let id: UUID; let message: String; let code: String?
  init(message: String, code: String? = nil)
  static func from(_ error: any Error) -> UserFacingError
  // FunctionsError.httpError(code:data:) → decode C1 error body → message/code; URLError .notConnectedToInternet/.networkConnectionLost/.timedOut → "You're offline. Check your connection and try again."; otherwise error.localizedDescription
}
// DropletAPI.swift
enum SessionState: Sendable, Equatable { case signedOut, signedIn(UUID) }
protocol DropletAPI: Sendable {
  func sessionStates() -> AsyncStream<SessionState>        // yields the current state first, then changes
  func currentEmail() async -> String?
  func signUp(email: String, password: String, name: String) async throws
  func signIn(email: String, password: String) async throws
  func signOut() async throws
  func profile() async throws -> Profile
  func updateProfile(_ patch: ProfilePatch) async throws -> Profile
  func assets() async throws -> [Asset]
  func bankConnection() async throws -> BankConnection?
  func createLinkToken() async throws -> String
  func exchange(publicToken: String, institutionName: String?) async throws -> SyncResult
  func sync() async throws -> SyncResult
  func simulatePurchase(merchant: String, amountCents: Int) async throws -> SyncResult
  func transactions(limit: Int) async throws -> [Transaction]
  func dropSummary() async throws -> DropSummary
  func portfolio() async throws -> [Holding]
}
// LiveDropletAPI.swift
final class LiveDropletAPI: DropletAPI { init(client: SupabaseClient); convenience init(config: AppConfig) }
// PreviewDropletAPI.swift
actor PreviewDropletAPI: DropletAPI {
  init(signedIn: Bool = true, hasBank: Bool = true, seed: PreviewSeed = .demo)
  func fail(next error: (any Error)?)                       // the next throwing call throws this, then it clears
  var calls: [String] { get }                               // method names, in call order (for tests)
}
struct PreviewSeed: Sendable { var profile: Profile; var assets: [Asset]; var transactions: [Transaction]; var holdings: [Holding]; var summary: DropSummary; static let demo: PreviewSeed; static let empty: PreviewSeed }
// AppConfig.swift
struct AppConfig: Sendable { let supabaseURL: URL; let supabaseKey: String; static func fromBundle(_ bundle: Bundle = .main) -> AppConfig }
```

### C5. Swift UI state (App/ and Features/*Model.swift, produced by Task 12, consumed by Tasks 13–17)
```swift
@MainActor @Observable final class AppModel {
  enum Route: Equatable { case launching, auth, connectBank, main }
  private(set) var route: Route            // starts .launching
  let api: any DropletAPI
  init(api: any DropletAPI)
  func start() async                       // for await state in api.sessionStates(): signedOut → .auth; signedIn → bankConnection() == nil ? .connectBank : .main
  func bankLinked()                        // route = .main
}
@MainActor @Observable final class DropletStore {
  init(api: any DropletAPI)
  private(set) var profile: Profile?
  private(set) var email: String?
  private(set) var assets: [Asset]                 // sorted by sortOrder
  private(set) var bank: BankConnection?
  private(set) var summary: DropSummary            // starts .empty
  private(set) var transactions: [Transaction]     // newest first
  private(set) var holdings: [Holding]             // largest investedCents first
  private(set) var isLoading: Bool
  private(set) var isSyncing: Bool
  private(set) var lastInvestment: Investment?     // set when sync/simulatePurchase returns a non-nil investment
  var error: UserFacingError?
  var totalInvestedCents: Int { get }
  var targetAsset: Asset? { get }                  // assets.first { $0.symbol == profile?.targetAsset }
  var transactionsByDay: [DaySection] { get }      // grouped by Transaction.day, newest day first
  func load() async                                // profile, email, assets, bank, summary, transactions(limit: 200), portfolio, fetched concurrently
  func sync() async                                // api.sync() then load()
  func simulatePurchase(merchant: String, amountCents: Int) async -> Bool   // true on success
  func setThreshold(_ cents: Int) async
  func setTargetAsset(_ symbol: String) async
  func setPaused(_ paused: Bool) async
  func signOut() async
}
struct DaySection: Identifiable, Equatable, Sendable { let day: Date; let items: [Transaction]; var id: Date { day } }
@MainActor @Observable final class AuthModel {
  enum Mode: Equatable { case signIn, signUp }
  var mode: Mode = .signUp; var name = ""; var email = ""; var password = ""
  private(set) var isSubmitting = false
  var error: UserFacingError?
  var canSubmit: Bool { get }              // email contains "@" && password.count >= 6 && (mode == .signIn || !name.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces).isEmpty) && !isSubmitting
  init(api: any DropletAPI)
  func submit() async                      // signUp or signIn; AppModel re-routes from sessionStates
}
@MainActor @Observable final class ConnectBankModel {
  enum Phase: Equatable { case idle, fetchingToken, ready(linkToken: String), exchanging, done }
  private(set) var phase: Phase = .idle
  var error: UserFacingError?
  init(api: any DropletAPI)
  func prepare() async                                              // .fetchingToken → .ready(token) | .idle + error
  func handleSuccess(publicToken: String, institutionName: String?) async   // .exchanging → .done | .idle + error
  func handleExit(errorMessage: String?)                            // phase = .idle; error = message.map { UserFacingError(message: $0) }
}
```

### C6. Additive members introduced by the verified tasks (allowed; nothing in C1–C5 changed)
- **SQL (Task 2):** `drop_summary` and `portfolio_by_asset` are read-only for `authenticated`. Their default privileges are revoked, and anon cannot read them.
- **TS `_shared` (Tasks 3–8):**
  - `db.ts` re-exports `type SupabaseClient`.
  - `http.ts` exports `constantTimeEqual(a: string, b: string): boolean`, used by `sync-all` and `webhook.ts`.
  - `plaid.ts` exports `plaidHttpError(err): HttpError`, `rethrowAsHttp(err): never`, `type TransactionsSyncPage` and `type SandboxTransaction`.
  - `sync.ts` exports `liveSyncDeps(admin, plaid?)`, `ITEM_COLUMNS`, `findItemByPlaidId(admin, itemId)` and `listActiveItems(admin)`.
  - `SyncStore.saveCursor` also sets `plaid_items.status = 'active'`.
  - `toTransactionRow` stores `roundup_cents = 0` for every skipped row, and stores a null Plaid currency as `'XXX'`, which is never eligible.
- **Swift Core (Tasks 10–12):**
  - Edge Function body/response types: `EmptyBody`, `ExchangeBody(publicToken:institutionName:)`, `SimulatePurchaseBody(merchant:amountCents:)` and `LinkTokenResponse(linkToken, expiration)`.
  - `enum DropletDates { parse(_:), localMidnight(fromDay:), dayString(_:) }`.
  - `JSONEncoder.droplet` also uses `.sortedKeys`.
  - `UserFacingError.offlineMessage`. A non-envelope HTTP body maps to "Something went wrong (HTTP <status>). Please try again.", and a relay error maps to "Couldn't reach Droplet's servers. Please try again."
  - `AppConfig.from(infoDictionary:) -> AppConfig?`.
  - `PreviewDropletAPI.setBankConnection(_:)` and `PreviewDropletAPI.prices`.
  - `PreviewSeed.userID` and `PreviewSeed.curatedAssets`.
  - `AppModel.start()` dedupes repeated session states, and routes to `.main` if `bankConnection()` throws.
  - `DropletStore.signOut()` always clears local state, even if the network sign-out fails.
- **Swift UI (Tasks 13–17):**
  - `AuthView(api:)` and `ConnectBankView(api:onLinked: @escaping @MainActor () -> Void)`: each view owns its C5 model.
  - `DropletApp` injects both `AppModel` and `DropletStore` with `.environment(_:)`.
  - `ErrorBanner(error:retry:dismiss:)` and `View.errorBanner(_:retry:)`.
  - `NextDropPill(summary:celebrating:)`.
  - Theme tokens: `Theme.accent`, `spring`, `sheetSpring`, `cardCornerRadius`, `heroTracking`, `Theme.Spacing`, and `View.heroNumber()`.
  - Inside the module, the model `Transaction` shadows `SwiftUI.Transaction`.

---

## Foundation (Tasks 1–2)

### Task 1: Local backend toolchain and Supabase scaffold

**Files:**
- Create: `supabase/config.toml` (generated by `supabase init`, then edited)
- Create: `supabase/functions/.env.example`
- Create: `supabase/functions/.env` (gitignored; never committed)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the Plaid client id from `~/Downloads/keystufflol.json` (`.plaid.client_id`) and the Plaid **Sandbox** secret, which the user adds to that file as `.plaid.sandbox_secret`. The existing `.plaid.secret` is a *production* secret: `sandbox.plaid.com` rejects it with `INVALID_API_KEYS`.
- Produces: a running local Supabase stack (API `http://127.0.0.1:54321`, DB `postgresql://postgres:postgres@127.0.0.1:54322/postgres`), plus env vars `PLAID_CLIENT_ID`, `PLAID_SECRET`, `PLAID_ENV`, `PLAID_WEBHOOK_URL` and `CRON_SECRET` for the Edge Functions.

- [ ] **Step 1: Make sure Docker is running and no other local Supabase stack holds the ports**

Run:
```bash
open -a Docker; until docker info >/dev/null 2>&1; do sleep 2; done; supabase stop --all
```
Expected: no error output. `supabase stop --all` prints `Stopped supabase local development setup.`, or nothing if no stack was running.

- [ ] **Step 2: Initialise Supabase in the repo**

Run:
```bash
supabase init
```
Expected: `Finished supabase init.` and a new `supabase/config.toml`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Set the project id and confirm email confirmations are off**

In `supabase/config.toml`, change the first setting line to:
```toml
project_id = "droplet"
```
Then run:
```bash
grep -n '^project_id = "droplet"' supabase/config.toml && sed -n '/^\[auth.email\]/,/^\[/p' supabase/config.toml | grep -n 'enable_confirmations = false'
```
Expected: two matching lines. Email sign-up works without confirmation, as the spec's demo requires.

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the committed env template**

Create `supabase/functions/.env.example`:
```dotenv
# Copy to supabase/functions/.env (gitignored) and fill in.
# Plaid Dashboard -> Developers -> Keys. Use the SANDBOX secret.
PLAID_CLIENT_ID=
PLAID_SECRET=
PLAID_ENV=sandbox
# Empty locally. Remotely: https://<project-ref>.supabase.co/functions/v1/plaid-webhook
PLAID_WEBHOOK_URL=
# Long random string. Must equal the Vault secret droplet_cron_secret (Task 19).
CRON_SECRET=
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Create the real (gitignored) env file from the key file**

Run:
```bash
KEYS="$HOME/Downloads/keystufflol.json"
SANDBOX_SECRET=$(jq -r '.plaid.sandbox_secret // empty' "$KEYS")
test -n "$SANDBOX_SECRET" || { echo "Add the Plaid SANDBOX secret to $KEYS as .plaid.sandbox_secret, then re-run."; exit 1; }
umask 077
{
  echo "PLAID_CLIENT_ID=$(jq -r .plaid.client_id "$KEYS")"
  echo "PLAID_SECRET=$SANDBOX_SECRET"
  echo "PLAID_ENV=sandbox"
  echo "PLAID_WEBHOOK_URL="
  echo "CRON_SECRET=$(openssl rand -hex 32)"
} > supabase/functions/.env
git check-ignore -q supabase/functions/.env && echo "env file is gitignored"
```
Expected: `env file is gitignored`. If the script stops with the "Add the Plaid SANDBOX secret" message, stop and ask the user for the Sandbox secret.

- [ ] **Step 6: Prove the Sandbox credentials work (no secret is printed)**

Run:
```bash
set -a; . supabase/functions/.env; set +a
curl -s https://sandbox.plaid.com/link/token/create -H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
  -d "{\"client_id\":\"$PLAID_CLIENT_ID\",\"secret\":\"$PLAID_SECRET\",\"client_name\":\"Droplet\",\"language\":\"en\",\"country_codes\":[\"US\"],\"user\":{\"client_user_id\":\"setup-check\"},\"products\":[\"transactions\"]}" \
  | jq -r '.link_token // .error_code' | cut -c1-13
```
Expected: `link-sandbox-`. If it prints `INVALID_API_KEYS`, the secret is not a Sandbox secret: stop and ask the user.

- [ ] **Step 7: Start the local stack**

Run:
```bash
supabase start
```
Expected: the stack starts and prints `API URL: http://127.0.0.1:54321`, `DB URL: postgresql://postgres:postgres@127.0.0.1:54322/postgres` and the local publishable/anon and secret keys. These are local-only demo keys.

- [ ] **Step 8: Commit**

```bash
git add supabase/config.toml supabase/functions/.env.example
git diff --cached | grep -iE "PLAID_SECRET=.+|CRON_SECRET=.+" && echo "STOP: secret staged" || echo "clean"
git commit -m "chore: scaffold Supabase project and env template

Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>"
```
Expected: `clean`, then a commit.

### Task 2: Database schema, RLS and atomic round-up RPCs

**Files:**
- Create: `supabase/migrations/20261024000001_schema.sql`
- Create: `supabase/migrations/20261024000002_rls.sql`
- Create: `supabase/migrations/20261024000003_logic.sql`
- Create: `supabase/migrations/20261024000004_cron.sql`
- Test: `supabase/tests/database/sweep.test.sql`
- Test: `supabase/tests/database/rls.test.sql`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the running local stack from Task 1.
- Produces: every database object in contract **C2**:
  - Tables: `assets` (6 seeded rows), `profiles` (auto-created on signup from `raw_user_meta_data.display_name`), `plaid_items`, `bank_connections`, `investments`, `transactions`.
  - Views: `drop_summary`, `portfolio_by_asset`.
  - Service-role-only RPCs: `apply_transactions(p_user uuid, p_rows jsonb, p_removed text[])` and `sweep_drops(p_user uuid, p_asset text, p_price numeric)`.
  - A pg_cron job, `droplet-sync-all`, that only fires once the Vault secrets `droplet_functions_url` and `droplet_cron_secret` exist.

```sql
  'portfolio_by_asset aggregates invested cents and quantity per asset');

select * from finish();
rollback;
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the schema migration**

Create `supabase/migrations/20261024000001_schema.sql`:
```sql
-- Droplet schema: tables in foreign-key order, plus the curated asset seed.
-- Money is integer cents; asset price/quantity are numeric(38,18).

create table public.assets (
  symbol      text primary key,
  name        text not null,
  chain       text not null,
  tm_asset_id uuid not null,
  icon_url    text,
  sort_order  int  not null default 0,
  enabled     boolean not null default true
);

insert into public.assets (symbol, name, chain, tm_asset_id, icon_url, sort_order) values
  ('SOL',    'Solana',          'solana', '495e07ac-fa3e-4179-85b7-b1e8cece3dc4', 'https://coin-images.coingecko.com/coins/images/4128/large/solana.png?1718769756', 1),
  ('ETH',    'Ethereum',        'base',   '28e9d809-26ec-4269-ba55-b1cd1e052cd8', 'https://coin-images.coingecko.com/coins/images/279/large/ethereum.png?1696501628', 2),
  ('CBBTC',  'Bitcoin (cbBTC)', 'base',   'f227d00e-1665-47e0-b598-959387a19373', 'https://coin-images.coingecko.com/coins/images/40143/large/cbbtc.webp?1726136727', 3),
  ('AAVE',   'Aave',            'base',   '64925b1c-338b-487f-bba0-3484764b4856', 'https://coin-images.coingecko.com/coins/images/12645/large/aave-token-round.png?1720472354', 4),
  ('JUP',    'Jupiter',         'solana', 'f7fc05d1-f30a-4765-b527-8b12aaa649dc', 'https://coin-images.coingecko.com/coins/images/34188/large/jup.png?1704266489', 5),
  ('RENDER', 'Render',          'solana', 'bbed4a7b-491b-4e44-a04b-9991c1612a97', 'https://coin-images.coingecko.com/coins/images/11636/large/rndr.png?1696511529', 6);

create table public.profiles (
  id              uuid primary key references auth.users (id) on delete cascade,
  display_name    text not null default '',
  threshold_cents int  not null default 500 check (threshold_cents in (200, 500, 1000)),
  target_asset    text not null default 'SOL' references public.assets (symbol),
  roundups_paused boolean not null default false,
  created_at      timestamptz not null default now()
);

-- Holds the Plaid access token. RLS on with no policies and no client grants (see rls migration),
-- so only the service role (Edge Functions) can read it.
create table public.plaid_items (
  id               uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id          uuid not null unique references auth.users (id) on delete cascade,
  item_id          text not null unique,
  access_token     text not null,
  cursor           text,
  institution_name text,
  status           text not null default 'active' check (status in ('active', 'login_required', 'error')),
  linked_on        date not null,
  created_at       timestamptz not null default now(),
  updated_at       timestamptz not null default now()
);

-- Client-safe mirror of plaid_items (no token).
create table public.bank_connections (
  user_id          uuid primary key references auth.users (id) on delete cascade,
  institution_name text,
  status           text not null default 'active' check (status in ('active', 'login_required', 'error')),
  last_synced_at   timestamptz
);

create table public.investments (
  id           uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id      uuid not null references auth.users (id) on delete cascade,
  asset_symbol text not null references public.assets (symbol),
  amount_cents bigint not null check (amount_cents > 0),
  price_usd    numeric(38, 18) not null check (price_usd > 0),
  quantity     numeric(38, 18) not null check (quantity > 0),
  mode         text not null default 'simulated' check (mode in ('simulated', 'live')),
  status       text not null default 'filled' check (status in ('filled', 'failed')),
  tm_order_id  text,
  created_at   timestamptz not null default now()
);
create index investments_user_created_idx on public.investments (user_id, created_at desc);

create table public.transactions (
  id                   uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  user_id              uuid not null references auth.users (id) on delete cascade,
  plaid_transaction_id text not null unique,
  merchant_name        text not null,
  amount_cents         bigint not null,
  iso_currency_code    text not null default 'USD',
  date                 date not null,
  pending              boolean not null default false,
  category             text,
  roundup_cents        int not null default 0 check (roundup_cents between 0 and 99),
  roundup_status       text not null check (roundup_status in ('pending', 'invested', 'skipped', 'reversed')),
  investment_id        uuid references public.investments (id),
  created_at           timestamptz not null default now()
);
create index transactions_user_date_idx on public.transactions (user_id, date desc, created_at desc);
create index transactions_user_pending_idx on public.transactions (user_id) where roundup_status = 'pending';
create index transactions_investment_idx on public.transactions (investment_id);
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the RLS migration**

Create `supabase/migrations/20261024000002_rls.sql`:
```sql
-- Row level security: clients read only their own rows; only Edge Functions (service_role) write
-- transactions, investments, plaid_items and bank_connections.

alter table public.assets           enable row level security;
alter table public.profiles         enable row level security;
alter table public.plaid_items      enable row level security;
alter table public.bank_connections enable row level security;
alter table public.investments      enable row level security;
alter table public.transactions     enable row level security;

revoke all on table public.assets, public.profiles, public.plaid_items,
  public.bank_connections, public.investments, public.transactions
  from anon, authenticated;

grant select on table public.assets to authenticated;
create policy "assets are readable" on public.assets
  for select to authenticated using (enabled);

grant select on table public.profiles to authenticated;
grant update (display_name, threshold_cents, target_asset, roundups_paused) on table public.profiles to authenticated;
create policy "read own profile" on public.profiles
  for select to authenticated using ((select auth.uid()) = id);
create policy "update own profile" on public.profiles
  for update to authenticated
  using ((select auth.uid()) = id)
  with check ((select auth.uid()) = id);

grant select on table public.bank_connections to authenticated;
create policy "read own bank connection" on public.bank_connections
  for select to authenticated using ((select auth.uid()) = user_id);

grant select on table public.investments to authenticated;
create policy "read own investments" on public.investments
  for select to authenticated using ((select auth.uid()) = user_id);

grant select on table public.transactions to authenticated;
create policy "read own transactions" on public.transactions
  for select to authenticated using ((select auth.uid()) = user_id);

-- public.plaid_items: intentionally no grants and no policies for anon/authenticated.
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write the logic migration (trigger, views, RPCs)**

Create `supabase/migrations/20261024000003_logic.sql`:
```sql
-- New-user trigger, read views, and the two atomic RPCs used by Edge Functions.

create function public.handle_new_user()
returns trigger
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = ''
as $$
begin
  insert into public.profiles (id, display_name)
  values (new.id, coalesce(new.raw_user_meta_data ->> 'display_name', ''));
  return new;
end;
$$;

create trigger on_auth_user_created
  after insert on auth.users
  for each row execute function public.handle_new_user();

create view public.drop_summary with (security_invoker = true) as
select
  p.id                                          as user_id,
  coalesce(t.pending_cents, 0)::bigint          as pending_cents,
  p.threshold_cents                             as threshold_cents,
  coalesce(t.lifetime_roundup_cents, 0)::bigint as lifetime_roundup_cents,
  coalesce(i.invested_cents, 0)::bigint         as invested_cents
from public.profiles p
left join lateral (
  select
    sum(x.roundup_cents) filter (where x.roundup_status = 'pending')               as pending_cents,
    sum(x.roundup_cents) filter (where x.roundup_status in ('pending', 'invested')) as lifetime_roundup_cents
  from public.transactions x
  where x.user_id = p.id
) t on true
left join lateral (
  select sum(y.amount_cents) as invested_cents
  from public.investments y
  where y.user_id = p.id and y.status = 'filled'
) i on true;

create view public.portfolio_by_asset with (security_invoker = true) as
select
  i.user_id,
  i.asset_symbol,
  a.name                            as asset_name,
  a.icon_url,
  sum(i.amount_cents)::bigint       as invested_cents,
  trim_scale(sum(i.quantity))::text as quantity,
  max(i.created_at)                 as last_invested_at
from public.investments i
join public.assets a on a.symbol = i.asset_symbol
where i.status = 'filled'
group by i.user_id, i.asset_symbol, a.name, a.icon_url;

-- Views inherit Supabase's default privileges (ALL to anon/authenticated); narrow them to read-only.
revoke all on public.drop_summary, public.portfolio_by_asset from anon, authenticated;
grant select on public.drop_summary, public.portfolio_by_asset to authenticated;

-- Upserts mapped Plaid rows and reverses removed ones. Never touches rows already invested.
create function public.apply_transactions(p_user uuid, p_rows jsonb, p_removed text[])
returns void
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = ''
as $$
begin
  insert into public.transactions as t (
    user_id, plaid_transaction_id, merchant_name, amount_cents, iso_currency_code,
    date, pending, category, roundup_cents, roundup_status
  )
  select
    p_user, r.plaid_transaction_id, r.merchant_name, r.amount_cents, coalesce(r.iso_currency_code, 'USD'),
    r.date, r.pending, r.category, r.roundup_cents, r.roundup_status
  from jsonb_to_recordset(coalesce(p_rows, '[]'::jsonb)) as r (
    plaid_transaction_id text, merchant_name text, amount_cents bigint, iso_currency_code text,
    date date, pending boolean, category text, roundup_cents int, roundup_status text
  )
  on conflict (plaid_transaction_id) do update set
    merchant_name     = excluded.merchant_name,
    amount_cents      = excluded.amount_cents,
    iso_currency_code = excluded.iso_currency_code,
    date              = excluded.date,
    pending           = excluded.pending,
    category          = excluded.category,
    roundup_cents     = excluded.roundup_cents,
    roundup_status    = excluded.roundup_status
  where t.user_id = p_user and t.roundup_status <> 'invested';

  update public.transactions
  set roundup_status = 'reversed', roundup_cents = 0
  where user_id = p_user
    and plaid_transaction_id = any (coalesce(p_removed, '{}'::text[]))
    and roundup_status in ('pending', 'skipped');
end;
$$;

-- Invests every pending drop for a user once the total reaches their threshold.
-- Locks the profile row (serialises concurrent sweeps) and the pending rows it consumes.
create function public.sweep_drops(p_user uuid, p_asset text, p_price numeric)
returns table (
  id uuid, asset_symbol text, amount_cents bigint, price_usd text,
  quantity text, mode text, created_at timestamptz
)
language plpgsql
security definer
set search_path = ''
as $$
#variable_conflict use_column
declare
  v_profile public.profiles;
  v_ids     uuid[];
  v_pending bigint;
  v_inv     public.investments;
begin
  if p_price is null or p_price <= 0 then
    raise exception 'sweep_drops: price must be positive';
  end if;

  select * into v_profile from public.profiles where public.profiles.id = p_user for update;
  if not found or v_profile.roundups_paused then
    return;
  end if;

  select coalesce(array_agg(s.id), '{}'::uuid[]), coalesce(sum(s.roundup_cents), 0)
    into v_ids, v_pending
  from (
    select x.id, x.roundup_cents
    from public.transactions x
    where x.user_id = p_user and x.roundup_status = 'pending'
    for update
  ) s;

  if v_pending < v_profile.threshold_cents then
    return;
  end if;

  insert into public.investments (user_id, asset_symbol, amount_cents, price_usd, quantity, mode)
  values (p_user, p_asset, v_pending, p_price, round((v_pending::numeric / 100) / p_price, 18), 'simulated')
  returning * into v_inv;

  update public.transactions
  set roundup_status = 'invested', investment_id = v_inv.id
  where public.transactions.id = any (v_ids);

  return query select
    v_inv.id, v_inv.asset_symbol, v_inv.amount_cents,
    trim_scale(v_inv.price_usd)::text, trim_scale(v_inv.quantity)::text,
    v_inv.mode, v_inv.created_at;
end;
$$;

revoke execute on function public.apply_transactions(uuid, jsonb, text[]) from public, anon, authenticated;
revoke execute on function public.sweep_drops(uuid, text, numeric) from public, anon, authenticated;
grant execute on function public.apply_transactions(uuid, jsonb, text[]) to service_role;
grant execute on function public.sweep_drops(uuid, text, numeric) to service_role;
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Write the cron migration**

Create `supabase/migrations/20261024000004_cron.sql`:
```sql
-- Safety-net sync every 15 minutes. Reads the target URL and shared secret from Vault, so the job
-- is a no-op until Task 19 stores `droplet_functions_url` and `droplet_cron_secret` for this project.

create extension if not exists pg_cron;
create extension if not exists pg_net with schema extensions;

select cron.schedule(
  'droplet-sync-all',
  '*/15 * * * *',
  $$
  select net.http_post(
    url     := (select decrypted_secret from vault.decrypted_secrets where name = 'droplet_functions_url') || '/sync-all',
    headers := jsonb_build_object(
      'Content-Type', 'application/json',
      'x-cron-secret', (select decrypted_secret from vault.decrypted_secrets where name = 'droplet_cron_secret')
    ),
    body    := '{}'::jsonb
  )
  where exists (select 1 from vault.decrypted_secrets where name = 'droplet_functions_url')
    and exists (select 1 from vault.decrypted_secrets where name = 'droplet_cron_secret');
  $$
);
```

Run:
```bash
C=$(docker ps --format '{{.Names}}' | grep -m1 supabase_db_droplet)
P() { docker exec -i "$C" psql -U postgres -d postgres -v ON_ERROR_STOP=1 -qtA "$@"; }
P <<'SQL'
insert into auth.users (id, email) values ('33333333-3333-3333-3333-333333333333','race@test.dev');
select public.apply_transactions('33333333-3333-3333-3333-333333333333',
 (select jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('plaid_transaction_id','race'||g,'merchant_name','M','amount_cents',101,'iso_currency_code','USD','date','2026-10-24','pending',false,'category',null,'roundup_cents',99,'roundup_status','pending')) from generate_series(1,6) g), '{}');
SQL
for i in 1 2 3 4; do ( P -c "begin; select count(*) from public.sweep_drops('33333333-3333-3333-3333-333333333333','SOL',100); select pg_sleep(0.5); commit;" >/dev/null ) & done; wait
P -c "select 'investments='||count(*)||' cents='||sum(amount_cents) from public.investments where user_id='33333333-3333-3333-3333-333333333333';"
P -c "delete from auth.users where id='33333333-3333-3333-3333-333333333333';"
```
Expected: `investments=1 cents=594`. Four racing sweeps produce a single investment.

## Backend: Edge Functions (Tasks 3–8)

Conventions for Tasks 3–8:
- There is no `deno.json` at the repo root, so every Deno command passes `--config supabase/functions/deno.json`. `deno task --config supabase/functions/deno.json test` runs with `supabase/functions` as its working directory.
- `"lock": false` keeps Deno 2.9 from writing a `deno.lock` into `supabase/functions`. The Supabase Edge Runtime (1.77.8, Deno 2.1.4 compatible) bundles that directory, and a newer-format lockfile has no use there.
- Imports are pinned inline (`npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2.118.0`, `npm:jose@6.2.13`), so each deployed function is self-contained. `jsr:@std/assert@1.0.19` appears only in tests.
- Test doubles are plain objects and fake `fetch` functions. Nothing in `deno task … test` touches the network. The one database test (Task 6) is ignored unless `DROPLET_DB_TESTS=1`.
- The pre-commit secret check (`git diff --cached | grep -iE "secret|access-sandbox|access-production"`) will match identifier names such as `PLAID_SECRET`, `CRON_SECRET`, the `secret` request field, and the fake test strings `secret-abc`, `env-secret` and `a-shared-secret-that-is-32-bytes!`. Those are expected. Anything else is a real value and must not be committed.

### Task 3: Edge Function shared helpers and the round-up rule

**Files:**
- Create: `supabase/functions/deno.json`
- Create: `supabase/functions/_shared/http.ts`
- Create: `supabase/functions/_shared/env.ts`
- Create: `supabase/functions/_shared/db.ts`
- Create: `supabase/functions/_shared/roundup.ts`
- Test: `supabase/functions/tests/http.test.ts`
- Test: `supabase/functions/tests/env.test.ts`
- Test: `supabase/functions/tests/db.test.ts`
- Test: `supabase/functions/tests/roundup.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - The C2 `p_rows` element shape, which `public.apply_transactions` reads (Task 2).
  - Env vars `SUPABASE_URL` and `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY` (injected by Supabase), and `PLAID_ENV` (from `supabase/functions/.env`, Task 1).
- Produces, exactly as in C3:
  - `http.ts`: `HttpError`, `json()`, `errorResponse()`, `readJson<T>()`
  - `env.ts`: `requireEnv()`, `plaidBaseUrl(env?)`, `todayInNewYork(now?)`
  - `db.ts`: `adminClient()`, `requireUser(req, admin)`
  - `roundup.ts`: `EXCLUDED_CATEGORIES`, `roundupCents()`, `PlaidTransaction`, `TransactionRow`, `toTransactionRow(t, linkedOn)`
- Also produces (additive):
  - `db.ts` re-exports `type SupabaseClient`, so the supabase-js version is pinned in one place.
  - `http.ts` exports `constantTimeEqual(a, b)`, used by `webhook.ts` (Task 7) and `sync-all` (Task 8).
  - The `test` task in `supabase/functions/deno.json`.

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing tests**

`supabase/functions/tests/roundup.test.ts`:
```ts
import { assertEquals } from 'jsr:@std/assert@1.0.19'
import {
  EXCLUDED_CATEGORIES,
  type PlaidTransaction,
  roundupCents,
  toTransactionRow,
} from '../_shared/roundup.ts'

const LINKED_ON = '2026-10-24'

function plaidTxn(overrides: Partial<PlaidTransaction> = {}): PlaidTransaction {
  return {
    transaction_id: 'txn-1',
    amount: 5.67,
    iso_currency_code: 'USD',
    date: '2026-10-24',
    pending: false,
    merchant_name: 'Blue Bottle',
    name: 'BLUE BOTTLE COFFEE #42',
    personal_finance_category: { primary: 'FOOD_AND_DRINK' },
    ...overrides,
  }
}

Deno.test('roundupCents rounds up to the next dollar', () => {
  assertEquals(roundupCents(567), 33)
  assertEquals(roundupCents(1342), 58)
  assertEquals(roundupCents(2318), 82)
  assertEquals(roundupCents(1), 99)
})

Deno.test('roundupCents is 0 for whole dollars, zero and refunds', () => {
  assertEquals(roundupCents(400), 0)
  assertEquals(roundupCents(0), 0)
  assertEquals(roundupCents(-1200), 0)
})

Deno.test('EXCLUDED_CATEGORIES lists the five non-purchase categories', () => {
  assertEquals([...EXCLUDED_CATEGORIES].sort(), [
    'BANK_FEES',
    'INCOME',
    'LOAN_PAYMENTS',
    'TRANSFER_IN',
    'TRANSFER_OUT',
  ])
})

Deno.test('toTransactionRow maps a settled USD purchase to a pending round-up', () => {
  assertEquals(toTransactionRow(plaidTxn(), LINKED_ON), {
    plaid_transaction_id: 'txn-1',
    merchant_name: 'Blue Bottle',
    amount_cents: 567,
    iso_currency_code: 'USD',
    date: '2026-10-24',
    pending: false,
    category: 'FOOD_AND_DRINK',
    roundup_cents: 33,
    roundup_status: 'pending',
  })
})

Deno.test('toTransactionRow converts dollars to cents with Math.round', () => {
  assertEquals(toTransactionRow(plaidTxn({ amount: 5.67 }), LINKED_ON)?.amount_cents, 567)
  assertEquals(toTransactionRow(plaidTxn({ amount: 0.07 }), LINKED_ON)?.amount_cents, 7)
  assertEquals(toTransactionRow(plaidTxn({ amount: 19.99 }), LINKED_ON)?.amount_cents, 1999)
})

Deno.test('toTransactionRow skips pending transactions', () => {
  const row = toTransactionRow(plaidTxn({ pending: true }), LINKED_ON)
  assertEquals(row?.pending, true)
  assertEquals(row?.roundup_cents, 0)
  assertEquals(row?.roundup_status, 'skipped')
})

Deno.test('toTransactionRow skips non-USD and unknown currencies', () => {
  const cad = toTransactionRow(plaidTxn({ iso_currency_code: 'CAD' }), LINKED_ON)
  assertEquals(cad?.iso_currency_code, 'CAD')
  assertEquals(cad?.roundup_cents, 0)
  assertEquals(cad?.roundup_status, 'skipped')
  const unknown = toTransactionRow(plaidTxn({ iso_currency_code: null }), LINKED_ON)
  assertEquals(unknown?.iso_currency_code, 'XXX')
  assertEquals(unknown?.roundup_status, 'skipped')
})

Deno.test('toTransactionRow skips excluded categories', () => {
  for (const primary of EXCLUDED_CATEGORIES) {
    const row = toTransactionRow(plaidTxn({ personal_finance_category: { primary } }), LINKED_ON)
    assertEquals(row?.category, primary)
    assertEquals(row?.roundup_cents, 0)
    assertEquals(row?.roundup_status, 'skipped')
  }
})

Deno.test('toTransactionRow skips whole-dollar purchases and refunds', () => {
  const whole = toTransactionRow(plaidTxn({ amount: 4 }), LINKED_ON)
  assertEquals(whole?.roundup_cents, 0)
  assertEquals(whole?.roundup_status, 'skipped')
  const refund = toTransactionRow(plaidTxn({ amount: -12 }), LINKED_ON)
  assertEquals(refund?.amount_cents, -1200)
  assertEquals(refund?.roundup_status, 'skipped')
})

Deno.test('toTransactionRow returns null before linked_on and keeps the linked_on day', () => {
  assertEquals(toTransactionRow(plaidTxn({ date: '2026-10-23' }), LINKED_ON), null)
  assertEquals(toTransactionRow(plaidTxn({ date: '2026-10-24' }), LINKED_ON)?.date, '2026-10-24')
})

Deno.test('toTransactionRow falls back from merchant_name to name to "Purchase"', () => {
  assertEquals(
    toTransactionRow(plaidTxn({ merchant_name: null }), LINKED_ON)?.merchant_name,
    'BLUE BOTTLE COFFEE #42',
  )
  assertEquals(
    toTransactionRow(plaidTxn({ merchant_name: '', name: undefined }), LINKED_ON)?.merchant_name,
    'Purchase',
  )
  assertEquals(
    toTransactionRow(plaidTxn({ merchant_name: undefined, name: null }), LINKED_ON)?.merchant_name,
    'Purchase',
  )
})

Deno.test('toTransactionRow stores a null category when Plaid sends none', () => {
  const row = toTransactionRow(plaidTxn({ personal_finance_category: null }), LINKED_ON)
  assertEquals(row?.category, null)
  assertEquals(row?.roundup_status, 'pending')
})
```

`supabase/functions/tests/env.test.ts`:
```ts
import { assertEquals, assertThrows } from 'jsr:@std/assert@1.0.19'
import { plaidBaseUrl, requireEnv, todayInNewYork } from '../_shared/env.ts'

function withEnv(name: string, value: string | undefined, fn: () => void): void {
  const previous = Deno.env.get(name)
  if (value === undefined) Deno.env.delete(name)
  else Deno.env.set(name, value)
  try {
    fn()
  } finally {
    if (previous === undefined) Deno.env.delete(name)
    else Deno.env.set(name, previous)
  }
}

Deno.test('requireEnv returns the value when set', () => {
  withEnv('DROPLET_TEST_VAR', 'hello', () => {
    assertEquals(requireEnv('DROPLET_TEST_VAR'), 'hello')
  })
})

Deno.test('requireEnv throws when missing or empty', () => {
  withEnv('DROPLET_TEST_VAR', undefined, () => {
    assertThrows(() => requireEnv('DROPLET_TEST_VAR'), Error, 'DROPLET_TEST_VAR')
  })
  withEnv('DROPLET_TEST_VAR', '', () => {
    assertThrows(() => requireEnv('DROPLET_TEST_VAR'), Error, 'DROPLET_TEST_VAR')
  })
})

Deno.test('plaidBaseUrl maps sandbox and production', () => {
  assertEquals(plaidBaseUrl('sandbox'), 'https://sandbox.plaid.com')
  assertEquals(plaidBaseUrl('production'), 'https://production.plaid.com')
})

Deno.test('plaidBaseUrl rejects other environments', () => {
  assertThrows(() => plaidBaseUrl('development'), Error, 'PLAID_ENV')
})

Deno.test('plaidBaseUrl reads PLAID_ENV by default', () => {
  withEnv('PLAID_ENV', 'sandbox', () => {
    assertEquals(plaidBaseUrl(), 'https://sandbox.plaid.com')
  })
  withEnv('PLAID_ENV', undefined, () => {
    assertThrows(() => plaidBaseUrl(), Error, 'PLAID_ENV')
  })
})

Deno.test('todayInNewYork uses the America/New_York calendar day', () => {
  // 23:30 EDT on Oct 24 is already Oct 25 in UTC.
  assertEquals(todayInNewYork(new Date('2026-10-25T03:30:00Z')), '2026-10-24')
  assertEquals(todayInNewYork(new Date('2026-10-24T04:30:00Z')), '2026-10-24')
  // 23:59 EST on Dec 31.
  assertEquals(todayInNewYork(new Date('2026-01-01T04:59:00Z')), '2025-12-31')
  assertEquals(todayInNewYork(new Date('2026-03-05T17:00:00Z')), '2026-03-05')
})
```

`supabase/functions/tests/http.test.ts`:
```ts
import { assertEquals, assertInstanceOf, assertRejects } from 'jsr:@std/assert@1.0.19'
import { constantTimeEqual, errorResponse, HttpError, json, readJson } from '../_shared/http.ts'

function post(body: string): Request {
  return new Request('http://localhost/fn', { method: 'POST', body })
}

Deno.test('HttpError carries status, code and message', () => {
  const err = new HttpError(404, 'NO_BANK', 'Connect a bank first.')
  assertInstanceOf(err, Error)
  assertEquals(err.status, 404)
  assertEquals(err.code, 'NO_BANK')
  assertEquals(err.message, 'Connect a bank first.')
  assertEquals(err.name, 'HttpError')
})

Deno.test('json returns a JSON response, 200 by default', async () => {
  const res = json({ ok: true })
  assertEquals(res.status, 200)
  assertEquals(res.headers.get('Content-Type'), 'application/json')
  assertEquals(await res.json(), { ok: true })
  assertEquals(json({}, 201).status, 201)
})

Deno.test('errorResponse maps HttpError to its status and code', async () => {
  const res = errorResponse(new HttpError(409, 'ITEM_LOGIN_REQUIRED', 'Reconnect your bank.'))
  assertEquals(res.status, 409)
  assertEquals(await res.json(), {
    error: { code: 'ITEM_LOGIN_REQUIRED', message: 'Reconnect your bank.' },
  })
})

Deno.test('errorResponse hides unexpected errors behind 500 INTERNAL', async () => {
  for (const err of [new Error('db password is hunter2'), 'boom', null]) {
    const res = errorResponse(err)
    assertEquals(res.status, 500)
    assertEquals(await res.json(), {
      error: { code: 'INTERNAL', message: 'Something went wrong. Try again.' },
    })
  }
})

Deno.test('readJson parses a JSON object', async () => {
  assertEquals(await readJson<{ a: number }>(post('{"a":1}')), { a: 1 })
})

Deno.test('readJson rejects invalid JSON and non-objects with 400 BAD_REQUEST', async () => {
  for (const body of ['not json', '', '[]', 'null', '42']) {
    const err = await assertRejects(() => readJson(post(body)), HttpError)
    assertEquals(err.status, 400)
    assertEquals(err.code, 'BAD_REQUEST')
  }
})

Deno.test('constantTimeEqual is true only for identical strings', () => {
  assertEquals(constantTimeEqual('k7Qz-41', 'k7Qz-41'), true)
  assertEquals(constantTimeEqual('k7Qz-41', 'k7Qz-42'), false)
  assertEquals(constantTimeEqual('k7Qz-41', 'k7Qz-410'), false)
  assertEquals(constantTimeEqual('', 'k7Qz-41'), false)
  assertEquals(constantTimeEqual('', ''), true)
})
```

`supabase/functions/tests/db.test.ts`:
```ts
import { assertEquals, assertRejects, assertThrows } from 'jsr:@std/assert@1.0.19'
import { adminClient, requireUser, type SupabaseClient } from '../_shared/db.ts'
import { HttpError } from '../_shared/http.ts'

type GetUserResult = { data: { user: { id: string } | null }; error: { message: string } | null }

function fakeAdmin(result: GetUserResult, seen: string[] = []): SupabaseClient {
  const auth = {
    getUser: (jwt: string): Promise<GetUserResult> => {
      seen.push(jwt)
      return Promise.resolve(result)
    },
  }
  return { auth } as unknown as SupabaseClient
}

function request(headers: Record<string, string> = {}): Request {
  return new Request('http://localhost/fn', { method: 'POST', headers, body: '{}' })
}

const USER_ID = '7d0f8a52-5c6e-4b8e-9a51-2f1d3c4b5a69'

Deno.test('requireUser returns the user id for a valid bearer token', async () => {
  const seen: string[] = []
  const admin = fakeAdmin({ data: { user: { id: USER_ID } }, error: null }, seen)
  assertEquals(await requireUser(request({ Authorization: 'Bearer user-jwt' }), admin), USER_ID)
  assertEquals(seen, ['user-jwt'])
})

Deno.test('requireUser rejects a missing or malformed Authorization header', async () => {
  const seen: string[] = []
  const admin = fakeAdmin({ data: { user: { id: USER_ID } }, error: null }, seen)
  const cases: Record<string, string>[] = [
    {},
    { Authorization: 'Basic abc' },
    { Authorization: 'Bearer ' },
  ]
  for (const headers of cases) {
    const err = await assertRejects(() => requireUser(request(headers), admin), HttpError)
    assertEquals(err.status, 401)
    assertEquals(err.code, 'UNAUTHORIZED')
  }
  assertEquals(seen, [])
})

Deno.test('requireUser rejects a token that Supabase Auth does not accept', async () => {
  const admin = fakeAdmin({ data: { user: null }, error: { message: 'invalid JWT' } })
  const err = await assertRejects(
    () => requireUser(request({ Authorization: 'Bearer anon-key' }), admin),
    HttpError,
  )
  assertEquals(err.status, 401)
  assertEquals(err.code, 'UNAUTHORIZED')
})

Deno.test('adminClient needs SUPABASE_URL and SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY', () => {
  const url = Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_URL')
  const key = Deno.env.get('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY')
  try {
    Deno.env.delete('SUPABASE_URL')
    Deno.env.delete('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY')
    assertThrows(() => adminClient(), Error, 'SUPABASE_URL')
    Deno.env.set('SUPABASE_URL', 'http://127.0.0.1:54321')
    Deno.env.set('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY', 'test-service-role-key')
    assertEquals(typeof adminClient().from, 'function')
  } finally {
    if (url === undefined) Deno.env.delete('SUPABASE_URL')
    else Deno.env.set('SUPABASE_URL', url)
    if (key === undefined) Deno.env.delete('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY')
    else Deno.env.set('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY', key)
  }
})
```

`supabase/functions/_shared/http.ts`:
```ts
// JSON responses and the error shape from contract C1: {"error":{"code","message"}}.

export class HttpError extends Error {
  constructor(public status: number, public code: string, message: string) {
    super(message)
    this.name = 'HttpError'
  }
}

export function json(body: unknown, status = 200): Response {
  return new Response(JSON.stringify(body), {
    status,
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
  })
}

/** HttpError → its status and code. Anything else → 500 INTERNAL, logged but not exposed. */
export function errorResponse(err: unknown): Response {
  if (err instanceof HttpError) {
    if (err.status >= 500) console.error(`${err.code}: ${err.message}`)
    return json({ error: { code: err.code, message: err.message } }, err.status)
  }
  console.error('Unhandled error:', err)
  return json({ error: { code: 'INTERNAL', message: 'Something went wrong. Try again.' } }, 500)
}

/** Parses a JSON object body. Throws HttpError(400, 'BAD_REQUEST') for anything else. */
export async function readJson<T>(req: Request): Promise<T> {
  let body: unknown
  try {
    body = await req.json()
  } catch {
    throw new HttpError(400, 'BAD_REQUEST', 'The request body must be JSON.')
  }
  if (typeof body !== 'object' || body === null || Array.isArray(body)) {
    throw new HttpError(400, 'BAD_REQUEST', 'The request body must be a JSON object.')
  }
  return body as T
}

/** String equality whose running time does not depend on where the strings first differ. */
export function constantTimeEqual(a: string, b: string): boolean {
  if (a.length !== b.length) return false
  let diff = 0
  for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) diff |= a.charCodeAt(i) ^ b.charCodeAt(i)
  return diff === 0
}
```

`supabase/functions/_shared/env.ts`:
```ts
// Environment access and the "today" rule shared by every function.

/** Returns the variable's value, or throws (→ 500 INTERNAL) when it is missing or empty. */
export function requireEnv(name: string): string {
  const value = Deno.env.get(name)
  if (!value) throw new Error(`Missing environment variable ${name}`)
  return value
}

/** Plaid API base URL for `env` (defaults to the PLAID_ENV variable). */
export function plaidBaseUrl(env: string = requireEnv('PLAID_ENV')): string {
  switch (env) {
    case 'sandbox':
      return 'https://sandbox.plaid.com'
    case 'production':
      return 'https://production.plaid.com'
    default:
      throw new Error(`Unsupported PLAID_ENV "${env}" (use sandbox or production)`)
  }
}

const NEW_YORK_DAY = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', {
  timeZone: 'America/New_York',
  year: 'numeric',
  month: '2-digit',
  day: '2-digit',
})

/** The America/New_York calendar date of `now`, as YYYY-MM-DD. */
export function todayInNewYork(now: Date = new Date()): string {
  const parts = Object.fromEntries(
    NEW_YORK_DAY.formatToParts(now).map((part) => [part.type, part.value]),
  )
  return `${parts.year}-${parts.month}-${parts.day}`
}
```

`supabase/functions/_shared/db.ts`:
```ts
// Service-role Supabase client and user-JWT check.
import { createClient, type SupabaseClient } from 'npm:@supabase/supabase-js@2.118.0'
import { requireEnv } from './env.ts'
import { HttpError } from './http.ts'

export type { SupabaseClient }

/** Service-role client (bypasses RLS). No session is stored or refreshed. */
export function adminClient(): SupabaseClient {
  return createClient(requireEnv('SUPABASE_URL'), requireEnv('SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY'), {
    auth: { persistSession: false, autoRefreshToken: false, detectSessionInUrl: false },
  })
}

/** Returns the signed-in user's id, or throws HttpError(401, 'UNAUTHORIZED'). */
export async function requireUser(req: Request, admin: SupabaseClient): Promise<string> {
  const match = /^Bearer\s+(\S+)$/i.exec(req.headers.get('Authorization') ?? '')
  if (!match) throw new HttpError(401, 'UNAUTHORIZED', 'Sign in to continue.')
  const { data, error } = await admin.auth.getUser(match[1])
  if (error || !data.user) {
    throw new HttpError(401, 'UNAUTHORIZED', 'Your session has expired. Sign in again.')
  }
  return data.user.id
}
```

`supabase/functions/_shared/roundup.ts`:
```ts
// Round-up rule and the Plaid transaction → `p_rows` mapping (contract C2).

export const EXCLUDED_CATEGORIES: ReadonlySet<string> = new Set([
  'TRANSFER_IN',
  'TRANSFER_OUT',
  'LOAN_PAYMENTS',
  'BANK_FEES',
  'INCOME',
])

/** Cents needed to reach the next whole dollar. 0 for whole dollars, zero and refunds. */
export function roundupCents(amountCents: number): number {
  if (amountCents <= 0) return 0
  return (100 - (amountCents % 100)) % 100
}

/** The subset of a Plaid `/transactions/sync` transaction that Droplet reads. */
export interface PlaidTransaction {
  transaction_id: string
  amount: number
  iso_currency_code: string | null
  date: string
  pending: boolean
  merchant_name?: string | null
  name?: string | null
  personal_finance_category?: { primary?: string | null } | null
}

/** One element of `apply_transactions(p_rows)`. */
export interface TransactionRow {
  plaid_transaction_id: string
  merchant_name: string
  amount_cents: number
  iso_currency_code: string
  date: string
  pending: boolean
  category: string | null
  roundup_cents: number
  roundup_status: 'pending' | 'skipped'
}

/**
 * Maps a Plaid transaction to a database row. Returns null for transactions dated before the
 * day the bank was linked, so history from before Droplet never produces drops.
 */
export function toTransactionRow(t: PlaidTransaction, linkedOn: string): TransactionRow | null {
  if (t.date < linkedOn) return null
  const amountCents = Math.round(t.amount * 100)
  // Plaid sends a null ISO code for unofficial currencies; 'XXX' is ISO 4217 "no currency".
  const currency = t.iso_currency_code ?? 'XXX'
  const category = t.personal_finance_category?.primary ?? null
  const eligible = !t.pending &&
    currency === 'USD' &&
    amountCents > 0 &&
    (category === null || !EXCLUDED_CATEGORIES.has(category))
  const roundup = eligible ? roundupCents(amountCents) : 0
  return {
    plaid_transaction_id: t.transaction_id,
    merchant_name: t.merchant_name || t.name || 'Purchase',
    amount_cents: amountCents,
    iso_currency_code: currency,
    date: t.date,
    pending: t.pending,
    category,
    roundup_cents: roundup,
    roundup_status: roundup > 0 ? 'pending' : 'skipped',
  }
}
```

### Task 4: True Markets price fetch

**Files:**
- Create: `supabase/functions/_shared/prices.ts`
- Test: `supabase/functions/tests/prices.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `GET https://api.truemarkets.co/v1/defi/market/prices?symbol=<SYMBOL>` (public, no auth). It returns `{"symbol":"SOL","candles":[{"interval":"30s","closePrice":"99.366035688829",…},…]}`.
- Produces: `fetchPrice(symbol: string, fetchFn?: typeof fetch): Promise<string>`, as in C3. It returns the `30s` candle's `closePrice` string unchanged, with a 5 s `AbortSignal.timeout`. It throws on a non-200 response, a missing `30s` candle, or a price that is malformed or not positive.

```ts
import { assertEquals, assertInstanceOf, assertRejects } from 'jsr:@std/assert@1.0.19'
import { fetchPrice } from '../_shared/prices.ts'

// Captured from GET https://api.truemarkets.co/v1/defi/market/prices?symbol=SOL
const SOL_RESPONSE = {
  symbol: 'SOL',
  candles: [
    {
      interval: '1h',
      openPrice: '100.36',
      closePrice: '99.36',
      highPrice: '100.39',
      lowPrice: '98.55',
    },
    {
      interval: '30s',
      openPrice: '99.39',
      closePrice: '99.366035688829',
      highPrice: '99.39',
      lowPrice: '99.33',
    },
  ],
}

type Call = { url: string; init?: RequestInit }

function fakeFetch(status: number, body: unknown, calls: Call[] = []): typeof fetch {
  return ((input: string | URL | Request, init?: RequestInit) => {
    calls.push({ url: String(input), init })
    const text = typeof body === 'string' ? body : JSON.stringify(body)
    return Promise.resolve(new Response(text, { status }))
  }) as typeof fetch
}

function withCandles(candles: unknown[]): unknown {

}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

`supabase/functions/_shared/prices.ts`:
```ts
// Live asset price from True Markets' public market-data endpoint (no auth, no key).

const PRICES_URL = 'https://api.truemarkets.co/v1/defi/market/prices'
const TIMEOUT_MS = 5000
const DECIMAL = /^\d+(\.\d+)?$/

interface Candle {
  interval?: string
  closePrice?: unknown
}

/** USD price of `symbol` as a decimal string: the closePrice of the "30s" candle. */
export async function fetchPrice(symbol: string, fetchFn: typeof fetch = fetch): Promise<string> {
  const url = `${PRICES_URL}?symbol=${encodeURIComponent(symbol)}`
  const res = await fetchFn(url, {
    headers: { Accept: 'application/json' },
    signal: AbortSignal.timeout(TIMEOUT_MS),
  })
  if (!res.ok) {
    await res.body?.cancel()
    throw new Error(`True Markets price for ${symbol}: HTTP ${res.status}`)
  }
  const body = (await res.json()) as { candles?: Candle[] }
  const candle = body.candles?.find((c) => c.interval === '30s')
  if (!candle) throw new Error(`True Markets price for ${symbol}: no 30s candle`)
  const price = candle.closePrice
  if (typeof price !== 'string' || !DECIMAL.test(price) || !(Number(price) > 0)) {
    throw new Error(`True Markets price for ${symbol}: bad closePrice ${JSON.stringify(price)}`)
  }
  return price
}
```

### Task 5: Plaid REST client

**Files:**
- Create: `supabase/functions/_shared/plaid.ts`
- Test: `supabase/functions/tests/plaid.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `requireEnv` and `plaidBaseUrl` (Task 3 `env.ts`), `HttpError` (Task 3 `http.ts`), `PlaidTransaction` (Task 3 `roundup.ts`). Env vars `PLAID_CLIENT_ID`, `PLAID_SECRET`, `PLAID_ENV`.
- Produces, exactly as in C3:
  - `PlaidApiError(errorCode, errorType, message, status)`
  - `PlaidClient` with `constructor({clientId, secret, baseUrl, fetchFn?})`, `static fromEnv(fetchFn?)`, `linkTokenCreate({userId, webhook?})`, `exchangePublicToken(publicToken)`, `transactionsSync(accessToken, cursor)`, `sandboxTransactionsCreate(accessToken, txns)` and `webhookVerificationKey(keyId)`
- Also produces (additive):
  - `plaidHttpError(err: PlaidApiError): HttpError`: `ITEM_LOGIN_REQUIRED` → `409 ITEM_LOGIN_REQUIRED`, anything else → `502 PLAID_ERROR` with Plaid's `error_message`.
  - `rethrowAsHttp(err: unknown): never`: use it as `.catch(rethrowAsHttp)` on direct Plaid calls in entrypoints, so `errorResponse()` returns the C1 codes.
  - The types `TransactionsSyncPage` and `SandboxTransaction`.
- Every request is `POST <base>/<path>` with JSON body `{client_id, secret, …}`. Each call logs `plaid <path> status=<n> request_id=<id>` and never logs the body.

`supabase/functions/tests/plaid.test.ts`:
```ts
import { assertEquals, assertInstanceOf, assertRejects, assertThrows } from 'jsr:@std/assert@1.0.19'
import { HttpError } from '../_shared/http.ts'
import { PlaidApiError, PlaidClient, plaidHttpError, rethrowAsHttp } from '../_shared/plaid.ts'

type Call = { url: string; method?: string; contentType: string | null; body: unknown }
type Reply = { status?: number; body: unknown }

function fakePlaid(replies: Reply[]): { client: PlaidClient; calls: Call[] } {
  const calls: Call[] = []
  const fetchFn = ((input: string | URL | Request, init?: RequestInit) => {
    calls.push({
      url: String(input),
      method: init?.method,
      contentType: new Headers(init?.headers).get('Content-Type'),
      body: JSON.parse(String(init?.body)),
    })
    const reply = replies.shift() ?? { body: {} }
    const text = typeof reply.body === 'string' ? reply.body : JSON.stringify(reply.body)
    return Promise.resolve(new Response(text, { status: reply.status ?? 200 }))
  }) as typeof fetch
  const client = new PlaidClient({
    clientId: 'client-123',
    secret: 'secret-abc',
    baseUrl: 'https://sandbox.plaid.com',
    fetchFn,
  })
  return { client, calls }
}

const CREDENTIALS = { client_id: 'client-123', secret: 'secret-abc' }

  assertEquals(calls.length, 1)

})

Deno.test('plaidHttpError maps login-required to 409 and the rest to 502', () => {
  const login = plaidHttpError(new PlaidApiError('ITEM_LOGIN_REQUIRED', 'ITEM_ERROR', 'm', 400))
  assertEquals([login.status, login.code], [409, 'ITEM_LOGIN_REQUIRED'])
  const other = plaidHttpError(new PlaidApiError('RATE_LIMIT_EXCEEDED', 'RATE_LIMIT', 'slow', 429))
  assertEquals([other.status, other.code, other.message], [502, 'PLAID_ERROR', 'slow'])
})

Deno.test('rethrowAsHttp converts PlaidApiError and passes other errors through', () => {
  const plaid = new PlaidApiError('INVALID_FIELD', 'INVALID_REQUEST', 'bad field', 400)
  const err = assertThrows(() => rethrowAsHttp(plaid), HttpError)
  assertEquals([err.status, err.code], [502, 'PLAID_ERROR'])
  const plain = new TypeError('boom')
  assertInstanceOf(assertThrows(() => rethrowAsHttp(plain)), TypeError)
})

Deno.test('fromEnv reads PLAID_CLIENT_ID, PLAID_SECRET and PLAID_ENV', async () => {
  const names = ['PLAID_CLIENT_ID', 'PLAID_SECRET', 'PLAID_ENV']
  const saved = names.map((name) => Deno.env.get(name))
  try {
    for (const name of names) Deno.env.delete(name)
    assertThrows(() => PlaidClient.fromEnv(), Error, 'PLAID_CLIENT_ID')
    Deno.env.set('PLAID_CLIENT_ID', 'env-client')
    Deno.env.set('PLAID_SECRET', 'env-secret')
    Deno.env.set('PLAID_ENV', 'sandbox')
    const urls: string[] = []
    let sent: unknown
    const fetchFn = ((input: string | URL | Request, init?: RequestInit) => {
      urls.push(String(input))
      sent = JSON.parse(String(init?.body))
      return Promise.resolve(Response.json({ access_token: 'a', item_id: 'i' }))
    }) as typeof fetch
    await PlaidClient.fromEnv(fetchFn).exchangePublicToken('p')
    assertEquals(urls, ['https://sandbox.plaid.com/item/public_token/exchange'])
    assertEquals(sent, { client_id: 'env-client', secret: 'env-secret', public_token: 'p' })
  } finally {
    names.forEach((name, i) => {
      const value = saved[i]
      if (value === undefined) Deno.env.delete(name)
      else Deno.env.set(name, value)
    })
  }
})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

`supabase/functions/_shared/plaid.ts`:
```ts
// Minimal Plaid REST client: only the five endpoints Droplet uses (spec §5).
import { plaidBaseUrl, requireEnv } from './env.ts'
import { HttpError } from './http.ts'
import type { PlaidTransaction } from './roundup.ts'

export class PlaidApiError extends Error {
  constructor(
    public errorCode: string,
    public errorType: string,
    message: string,
    public status: number,
  ) {
    super(message)
    this.name = 'PlaidApiError'
  }
}

/** Contract C1 mapping: ITEM_LOGIN_REQUIRED → 409, any other Plaid error → 502 PLAID_ERROR. */
export function plaidHttpError(err: PlaidApiError): HttpError {
  if (err.errorCode === 'ITEM_LOGIN_REQUIRED') {
    return new HttpError(409, 'ITEM_LOGIN_REQUIRED', 'Your bank needs you to sign in again.')
  }
  return new HttpError(502, 'PLAID_ERROR', err.message)
}

/** Use as `.catch(rethrowAsHttp)` on a direct Plaid call inside an Edge Function. */
export function rethrowAsHttp(err: unknown): never {
  throw err instanceof PlaidApiError ? plaidHttpError(err) : err
}

export interface TransactionsSyncPage {
  added: PlaidTransaction[]
  modified: PlaidTransaction[]
  removed: { transaction_id: string }[]
  next_cursor: string
  has_more: boolean
}

export interface SandboxTransaction {
  date_transacted: string
  date_posted: string
  amount: number
  description: string
}

export class PlaidClient {
  readonly #clientId: string
  readonly #secret: string
  readonly #baseUrl: string
  readonly #fetchFn: typeof fetch

  constructor(opts: { clientId: string; secret: string; baseUrl: string; fetchFn?: typeof fetch }) {
    this.#clientId = opts.clientId
    this.#secret = opts.secret
    this.#baseUrl = opts.baseUrl
    this.#fetchFn = opts.fetchFn ?? fetch
  }

  static fromEnv(fetchFn?: typeof fetch): PlaidClient {
    return new PlaidClient({
      clientId: requireEnv('PLAID_CLIENT_ID'),
      secret: requireEnv('PLAID_SECRET'),
      baseUrl: plaidBaseUrl(),
      fetchFn,
    })
  }

  async linkTokenCreate(
    args: { userId: string; webhook?: string },
  ): Promise<{ link_token: string; expiration: string }> {
    const data = await this.#post<{ link_token: string; expiration: string }>(
      '/link/token/create',
      {
        client_name: 'Droplet',
        language: 'en',
        country_codes: ['US'],
        user: { client_user_id: args.userId },
        products: ['transactions'],
        transactions: { days_requested: 30 },
        ...(args.webhook ? { webhook: args.webhook } : {}),
      },
    )
    return { link_token: data.link_token, expiration: data.expiration }
  }

  async exchangePublicToken(
    publicToken: string,
  ): Promise<{ access_token: string; item_id: string }> {
    const data = await this.#post<{ access_token: string; item_id: string }>(
      '/item/public_token/exchange',
      { public_token: publicToken },
    )
    return { access_token: data.access_token, item_id: data.item_id }
  }

  async transactionsSync(
    accessToken: string,
    cursor: string | null,
  ): Promise<TransactionsSyncPage> {
    const data = await this.#post<TransactionsSyncPage>('/transactions/sync', {
      access_token: accessToken,
      count: 500,
      ...(cursor ? { cursor } : {}),
    })
    return {
      added: data.added,
      modified: data.modified,
      removed: data.removed,
      next_cursor: data.next_cursor,
      has_more: data.has_more,
    }
  }

  async sandboxTransactionsCreate(accessToken: string, txns: SandboxTransaction[]): Promise<void> {
    await this.#post('/sandbox/transactions/create', {
      access_token: accessToken,
      transactions: txns,
    })
  }

  async webhookVerificationKey(keyId: string): Promise<JsonWebKey> {
    const data = await this.#post<{ key: JsonWebKey }>('/webhook_verification_key/get', {
      key_id: keyId,
    })
    return data.key
  }

  async #post<T>(path: string, body: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<T> {
    const res = await this.#fetchFn(`${this.#baseUrl}${path}`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
      body: JSON.stringify({ client_id: this.#clientId, secret: this.#secret, ...body }),
    })
    const data = (await res.json().catch(() => null)) as Record<string, unknown> | null
    // Log the request id for support, never the request body (it carries the secret and tokens).
    console.log(`plaid ${path} status=${res.status} request_id=${data?.request_id ?? 'none'}`)
    if (!res.ok || data === null) {
      throw new PlaidApiError(
        String(data?.error_code ?? 'UNKNOWN'),
        String(data?.error_type ?? 'API_ERROR'),
        String(data?.error_message ?? `Plaid ${path} failed with HTTP ${res.status}`),
        res.status,
      )
    }
    return data as T
  }
}
```
