- [ ] **Step 3: Create `ios/Droplet/Core/Models.swift`**

```swift
import Foundation

// Wire models. Keys are snake_case on the wire; JSONDecoder.droplet / JSONEncoder.droplet convert them.

struct Asset: Codable, Sendable, Hashable, Identifiable {
    let symbol: String
    let name: String
    let chain: String
    let iconUrl: URL?
    let sortOrder: Int

    var id: String { symbol }
}

struct Profile: Codable, Sendable, Equatable {
    let id: UUID
    var displayName: String
    var thresholdCents: Int
    var targetAsset: String
    var roundupsPaused: Bool
}

/// PATCH body for `profiles`. Nil fields are omitted from the JSON.
struct ProfilePatch: Encodable, Sendable, Equatable {
    var displayName: String? = nil
    var thresholdCents: Int? = nil
    var targetAsset: String? = nil
    var roundupsPaused: Bool? = nil
}

enum BankStatus: String, Codable, Sendable {
    case active
    case loginRequired = "login_required"
    case error
}

struct BankConnection: Codable, Sendable, Equatable {
    let institutionName: String?
    let status: BankStatus
    let lastSyncedAt: Date?
}

enum RoundupStatus: String, Codable, Sendable {
    case pending, invested, skipped, reversed
}

struct Transaction: Codable, Sendable, Identifiable, Equatable {
    let id: UUID
    let merchantName: String
    let amountCents: Int
    /// Postgres `date`, formatted `yyyy-MM-dd`.
    let date: String
    let pending: Bool
    let roundupCents: Int
    let roundupStatus: RoundupStatus
    let createdAt: Date

    /// `date` as local midnight in `Calendar.current`; falls back to `createdAt` if `date` is malformed.
    var day: Date { DropletDates.localMidnight(fromDay: date) ?? createdAt }
}

struct DropSummary: Codable, Sendable, Equatable {
    let pendingCents: Int
    let thresholdCents: Int
    let lifetimeRoundupCents: Int
    let investedCents: Int

    var progress: Double { min(1, Double(pendingCents) / Double(max(thresholdCents, 1))) }
    var remainingCents: Int { max(0, thresholdCents - pendingCents) }

    static let empty = DropSummary(pendingCents: 0, thresholdCents: 500, lifetimeRoundupCents: 0, investedCents: 0)
}

struct Holding: Codable, Sendable, Identifiable, Equatable {
    let assetSymbol: String
    let assetName: String
    let iconUrl: URL?
    let investedCents: Int
    /// Decimal string, e.g. "0.053137".
    let quantity: String
    let lastInvestedAt: Date?

    var id: String { assetSymbol }
}

struct Investment: Codable, Sendable, Equatable, Identifiable {
    let id: UUID
    let assetSymbol: String
    let amountCents: Int
    /// Decimal string, e.g. "99.366035688829".
    let priceUsd: String
    /// Decimal string, e.g. "0.053137".
    let quantity: String
    let mode: String
    let createdAt: Date
}

struct SyncResult: Codable, Sendable, Equatable {
    let added: Int
    let modified: Int
    let removed: Int
    let investment: Investment?
}

// MARK: - Edge Function bodies (contract C1)

/// `{}` for functions that take no input.
struct EmptyBody: Encodable, Sendable {}

/// `plaid-exchange` request. `institution_name` is sent as `null` when unknown.
struct ExchangeBody: Encodable, Sendable {
    let publicToken: String
    let institutionName: String?

    private enum CodingKeys: String, CodingKey { case publicToken, institutionName }

    func encode(to encoder: any Encoder) throws {
        var container = encoder.container(keyedBy: CodingKeys.self)
        try container.encode(publicToken, forKey: .publicToken)
        try container.encode(institutionName, forKey: .institutionName)
    }
}

/// `simulate-purchase` request.
struct SimulatePurchaseBody: Encodable, Sendable {
    let merchant: String
    let amountCents: Int
}

/// `plaid-link-token` response.
struct LinkTokenResponse: Decodable, Sendable {
    let linkToken: String
    let expiration: String
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Create `ios/Droplet/Core/Coding.swift`**

- `Date.ISO8601FormatStyle` parses both `2026-10-24T16:03:11.482123+00:00` (PostgREST `timestamptz`) and `2026-10-24T16:03:11.482Z` (JavaScript `toISOString`).
- A bare Postgres `date` becomes local midnight.
```swift
import Foundation

extension JSONDecoder {
    /// snake_case keys; dates as ISO-8601 with or without fractional seconds, or Postgres `yyyy-MM-dd`.
    static var droplet: JSONDecoder {
        let decoder = JSONDecoder()
        decoder.keyDecodingStrategy = .convertFromSnakeCase
        decoder.dateDecodingStrategy = .custom { decoder in
            let container = try decoder.singleValueContainer()
            let string = try container.decode(String.self)
            guard let date = DropletDates.parse(string) else {
                throw DecodingError.dataCorruptedError(
                    in: container, debugDescription: "Unrecognized date: \(string)"
                )
            }
            return date
        }
        return decoder
    }
}

extension JSONEncoder {
    /// snake_case keys; ISO-8601 dates; sorted keys so output is deterministic.
    static var droplet: JSONEncoder {
        let encoder = JSONEncoder()
        encoder.keyEncodingStrategy = .convertToSnakeCase
        encoder.dateEncodingStrategy = .iso8601
        encoder.outputFormatting = [.sortedKeys]
        return encoder
    }
}

enum DropletDates {
    private static let withFraction = Date.ISO8601FormatStyle(includingFractionalSeconds: true)
    private static let withoutFraction = Date.ISO8601FormatStyle()

    /// Parses `2026-10-24T16:03:11.482123+00:00`, `2026-10-24T16:03:11Z` or `2026-10-24`.
    static func parse(_ string: String) -> Date? {
        if let date = try? Date(string, strategy: withFraction) { return date }
        if let date = try? Date(string, strategy: withoutFraction) { return date }
        return localMidnight(fromDay: string)
    }

    /// `yyyy-MM-dd` as local midnight in `Calendar.current`.
    static func localMidnight(fromDay string: String) -> Date? {
        let parts = string.split(separator: "-", omittingEmptySubsequences: false)
        guard parts.count == 3, parts[0].count == 4, parts[1].count == 2, parts[2].count == 2,
              let year = Int(parts[0]), let month = Int(parts[1]), let day = Int(parts[2])
        else { return nil }
        return Calendar.current.date(from: DateComponents(year: year, month: month, day: day))
    }

    /// Local calendar day of `date`, formatted `yyyy-MM-dd`.
    static func dayString(_ date: Date) -> String {
        let parts = Calendar.current.dateComponents([.year, .month, .day], from: date)
        return String(format: "%04d-%02d-%02d", parts.year ?? 0, parts.month ?? 0, parts.day ?? 0)
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Create `ios/Droplet/Core/Money.swift`**

Formatting is pinned to the `en_US` locale, so the output never depends on the device's region.
```swift
import Foundation

enum Money {
    private static let usd = Decimal.FormatStyle.Currency(code: "USD", locale: Locale(identifier: "en_US"))
    private static let posix = Locale(identifier: "en_US_POSIX")
    private static let display = Locale(identifier: "en_US")

    /// 567 -> "$5.67", 0 -> "$0.00", -567 -> "-$5.67".
    static func format(cents: Int) -> String {
        (Decimal(cents) / 100).formatted(usd)
    }

    /// 33 -> "+$0.33", 0 -> "$0.00", -33 -> "-$0.33".
    static func formatSigned(cents: Int) -> String {
        cents > 0 ? "+" + format(cents: cents) : format(cents: cents)
    }

    /// Mirrors the server rule: next whole dollar minus the amount; 0 for refunds and whole dollars.
    static func roundupCents(forAmountCents amountCents: Int) -> Int {
        amountCents <= 0 ? 0 : (100 - amountCents % 100) % 100
    }

    /// "0.053137000000000000" -> "0.053137". Returns the input unchanged when it is not a number.
    static func formatQuantity(_ decimal: String, maxFractionDigits: Int = 6) -> String {
        guard let value = Decimal(string: decimal, locale: posix) else { return decimal }
        return value.formatted(
            .number.locale(display).precision(.fractionLength(0...max(0, maxFractionDigits)))
        )
    }
}
```

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - From Task 10: every model, `JSONDecoder.droplet` and `JSONEncoder.droplet`, `Money.roundupCents(forAmountCents:)`, `DropletDates.dayString(_:)`, `EmptyBody`, `ExchangeBody`, `SimulatePurchaseBody` and `LinkTokenResponse`.
  - From C1: the function names `plaid-link-token`, `plaid-exchange`, `plaid-sync` and `simulate-purchase`, and the error envelope `{"error":{"code","message"}}`.
  - From C2: the tables and views `profiles`, `assets`, `bank_connections`, `transactions`, `drop_summary` and `portfolio_by_asset`. RLS already limits every row to the signed-in user.
  - From supabase-swift 2.55.4, each call checked against its source:
    - Client setup:
      - `SupabaseClient(supabaseURL:supabaseKey:options:)`
      - `SupabaseClientOptions(db:auth:)`
      - `SupabaseClientOptions.DatabaseOptions(schema:encoder:decoder:)`
      - `SupabaseClientOptions.AuthOptions(emitLocalSessionAsInitialSession:)`
    - Auth:
      - `auth.authStateChanges`, which yields `(event: AuthChangeEvent, session: Session?)`
      - `Session.isExpired`
      - `auth.currentUser`
      - `auth.session`
      - `auth.signUp(email:password:data:)`
      - `auth.signIn(email:password:)`
      - `auth.signOut()`
    - PostgREST: `from(_:)`, `select(_:)`, `eq(_:value:)`, `order(_:ascending:)`, `limit(_:)`, `single()`, `update(_:)` (a throwing call) and `execute().value`.
    - Functions:
      - `functions.invoke(_:options:decoder:)`
      - `FunctionInvokeOptions(body:encoder:)`
      - `FunctionsError.httpError(code:data:)` and `.relayError`
- Produces:
  - **Everything listed under C4** for `UserFacingError`, `SessionState`, `DropletAPI`, `LiveDropletAPI`, `PreviewDropletAPI`, `PreviewSeed` and `AppConfig`, with the exact names and signatures given there.
  - **Additional members, used by tests and previews:**
    - `UserFacingError.offlineMessage`: `"You're offline. Check your connection and try again."`
    - `AppConfig.from(infoDictionary: [String: Any]) -> AppConfig?`. It returns nil when a value is missing, or when the URL has no host.
    - `PreviewDropletAPI.setBankConnection(_ connection: BankConnection?)`, for previewing the `login_required` state.
    - `PreviewDropletAPI.prices`: made-up fill prices for the preview sweep.
    - `PreviewSeed.userID` and `PreviewSeed.curatedAssets`.
  - **`PreviewSeed.demo`:**
    - $3.21 pending of a $5.00 threshold.
    - Three holdings, largest first: SOL $10.96, ETH $5.27 and CBBTC $5.03.
    - 17 transactions over the last 7 days, covering every round-up status.
  - **`PreviewSeed.empty`:** a new user with default settings and no activity.
  - **`PreviewDropletAPI` behavior:**
    - It throws `UserFacingError` with C1 codes: `NO_BANK`, `ITEM_LOGIN_REQUIRED` and `BAD_REQUEST`.
    - `signUp` clears the bank, because a new account has no bank linked.
    - `simulatePurchase`, `sync` and `exchange` mirror `sweep_drops`: once pending drops reach the threshold, and round-ups aren't paused, they invest everything pending into `profile.targetAsset`.

`ios/DropletTests/UserFacingErrorTests.swift`:
```swift
import Foundation
import Supabase
import Testing
@testable import Droplet

struct UserFacingErrorTests {
    @Test func decodesEdgeFunctionErrorBody() {
        let body = Data(#"{"error":{"code":"ITEM_LOGIN_REQUIRED","message":"Reconnect your bank to keep rounding up."}}"#.utf8)
        let error = UserFacingError.from(FunctionsError.httpError(code: 409, data: body))
        #expect(error.code == "ITEM_LOGIN_REQUIRED")
        #expect(error.message == "Reconnect your bank to keep rounding up.")
    }

    @Test func undecodableFunctionErrorFallsBackToStatus() {
        let error = UserFacingError.from(FunctionsError.httpError(code: 502, data: Data("Bad Gateway".utf8)))
        #expect(error.code == nil)
        #expect(error.message == "Something went wrong (HTTP 502). Please try again.")
    }

    @Test func relayErrorHasFriendlyMessage() {
        #expect(UserFacingError.from(FunctionsError.relayError).message == "Couldn't reach Droplet's servers. Please try again.")
    }

    @Test(arguments: [URLError.Code.notConnectedToInternet, .networkConnectionLost, .timedOut])
    func networkLossReadsAsOffline(code: URLError.Code) {
        let error = UserFacingError.from(URLError(code))
        #expect(error.message == "You're offline. Check your connection and try again.")
        #expect(error.code == nil)
    }

    @Test func otherErrorsUseLocalizedDescription() {
        let postgrest = PostgrestError(code: "42501", message: "permission denied for table profiles")
        #expect(UserFacingError.from(postgrest).message == "permission denied for table profiles")
        let other = URLError(.badServerResponse)
        #expect(UserFacingError.from(other).message == other.localizedDescription)
    }

    @Test func passesUserFacingErrorsThrough() {
        let original = UserFacingError(message: "Connect a bank first.", code: "NO_BANK")
        #expect(UserFacingError.from(original) == original)
    }

    @Test func eachErrorHasItsOwnIdentity() {
        let first = UserFacingError(message: "Same text")
        let second = UserFacingError(message: "Same text")
        #expect(first.id != second.id)
        #expect(first != second)
    }
}
```

`ios/DropletTests/AppConfigTests.swift`. Hosted tests run inside the app, so `Bundle.main` is the app bundle, and `hostAppBundleIsConfigured` checks the real `Config.xcconfig` → `Info.plist` path:
```swift
import Foundation
import Testing
@testable import Droplet

struct AppConfigTests {
    @Test func readsValuesFromInfoDictionary() throws {
        let config = try #require(AppConfig.from(infoDictionary: [
            "SupabaseURL": "http://127.0.0.1:54321",
            "SupabasePublishableKey": "sb_publishable_dummy",
        ]))
        #expect(config.supabaseURL.absoluteString == "http://127.0.0.1:54321")
        #expect(config.supabaseKey == "sb_publishable_dummy")
    }

    @Test func rejectsURLTruncatedByXcconfigComment() {
        #expect(AppConfig.from(infoDictionary: [
            "SupabaseURL": "http:",
            "SupabasePublishableKey": "sb_publishable_dummy",
        ]) == nil)
    }

    @Test func rejectsMissingValues() {
        #expect(AppConfig.from(infoDictionary: ["SupabaseURL": "http://127.0.0.1:54321", "SupabasePublishableKey": ""]) == nil)
        #expect(AppConfig.from(infoDictionary: [:]) == nil)
    }

    @Test func hostAppBundleIsConfigured() {
        let config = AppConfig.fromBundle()
        #expect(config.supabaseURL.host() != nil)
        #expect(!config.supabaseKey.isEmpty)
    }
}
```

```swift
import Foundation
import Testing
@testable import Droplet

struct PreviewDropletAPITests {
    private func seed(thresholdCents: Int = 500, paused: Bool = false) -> PreviewSeed {
        var seed = PreviewSeed.empty
        seed.profile.thresholdCents = thresholdCents
        seed.profile.roundupsPaused = paused
        seed.summary = DropSummary(pendingCents: 0, thresholdCents: thresholdCents, lifetimeRoundupCents: 0, investedCents: 0)
        return seed
    }

    @Test func demoSeedIsInternallyConsistent() {
        let demo = PreviewSeed.demo
        let pending = demo.transactions.filter { $0.roundupStatus == .pending }.reduce(0) { $0 + $1.roundupCents }
        let invested = demo.holdings.reduce(0) { $0 + $1.investedCents }
        #expect(demo.summary.pendingCents == 321)
        #expect(demo.summary.pendingCents == pending)
        #expect(demo.summary.investedCents == invested)
        #expect(demo.summary.lifetimeRoundupCents == pending + invested)
        #expect(demo.summary.thresholdCents == demo.profile.thresholdCents)
        #expect(demo.assets.map(\.symbol) == ["SOL", "ETH", "CBBTC", "AAVE", "JUP", "RENDER"])
        for transaction in demo.transactions where transaction.roundupStatus == .pending || transaction.roundupStatus == .invested {
            #expect(transaction.roundupCents == Money.roundupCents(forAmountCents: transaction.amountCents))

    @Test func crossingThresholdSweepsEveryPendingDrop() async throws {

        _ = try await api.simulatePurchase(merchant: "Corner Store", amountCents: 101)
        let second = try await api.simulatePurchase(merchant: "Corner Store", amountCents: 101)

    @Test func failNextThrowsOnceThenClears() async throws {

    @Test func recordsCallsInOrder() async throws {

    @Test func sessionStatesYieldCurrentStateThenChanges() async throws {

        try await api.signUp(email: "sam@example.com", password: "hunter22", name: "Sam")
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Create `ios/Droplet/Core/UserFacingError.swift`**

```swift
import Foundation
import Supabase

/// An error ready to show in an ErrorBanner.
struct UserFacingError: Error, Equatable, Identifiable, Sendable {
    let id: UUID
    let message: String
    /// The contract C1 error code (e.g. "ITEM_LOGIN_REQUIRED"), when the server sent one.
    let code: String?

    init(message: String, code: String? = nil) {
        self.id = UUID()
        self.message = message
        self.code = code
    }

    static let offlineMessage = "You're offline. Check your connection and try again."

    static func from(_ error: any Error) -> UserFacingError {
        if let error = error as? UserFacingError {
            return error
        }
        if let error = error as? FunctionsError {
            switch error {
            case let .httpError(status, data):
                if let envelope = try? JSONDecoder.droplet.decode(ErrorEnvelope.self, from: data) {
                    return UserFacingError(message: envelope.error.message, code: envelope.error.code)
                }
                return UserFacingError(message: "Something went wrong (HTTP \(status)). Please try again.")
            case .relayError:
                return UserFacingError(message: "Couldn't reach Droplet's servers. Please try again.")
            }
        }
        if let error = error as? URLError,
           [.notConnectedToInternet, .networkConnectionLost, .timedOut].contains(error.code) {
            return UserFacingError(message: offlineMessage)
        }
        return UserFacingError(message: error.localizedDescription)
    }
}

/// `{"error":{"code":"<CODE>","message":"<human text>"}}` (contract C1).
private struct ErrorEnvelope: Decodable {
    struct Body: Decodable {
        let code: String
        let message: String
    }

    let error: Body
}
```

`ios/Droplet/Core/DropletAPI.swift`:
```swift
import Foundation

enum SessionState: Sendable, Equatable {
    case signedOut
    case signedIn(UUID)
}

/// Everything the UI needs from the backend. `LiveDropletAPI` talks to Supabase;
/// `PreviewDropletAPI` is an in-memory fake for previews and tests.
protocol DropletAPI: Sendable {
    /// Yields the current state first, then every change.
    func sessionStates() -> AsyncStream<SessionState>
    func currentEmail() async -> String?
    func signUp(email: String, password: String, name: String) async throws
    func signIn(email: String, password: String) async throws
    func signOut() async throws

    func profile() async throws -> Profile
    func updateProfile(_ patch: ProfilePatch) async throws -> Profile
    func assets() async throws -> [Asset]
    func bankConnection() async throws -> BankConnection?

    func createLinkToken() async throws -> String
    func exchange(publicToken: String, institutionName: String?) async throws -> SyncResult
    func sync() async throws -> SyncResult
    func simulatePurchase(merchant: String, amountCents: Int) async throws -> SyncResult

    func transactions(limit: Int) async throws -> [Transaction]
    func dropSummary() async throws -> DropSummary
    func portfolio() async throws -> [Holding]
}
```

`ios/Droplet/Core/AppConfig.swift`:
```swift
import Foundation

/// Supabase connection settings, injected through Config.xcconfig -> Info.plist.
struct AppConfig: Sendable {
    let supabaseURL: URL
    let supabaseKey: String

    static func fromBundle(_ bundle: Bundle = .main) -> AppConfig {
        guard let config = from(infoDictionary: bundle.infoDictionary ?? [:]) else {
            preconditionFailure(
                "SupabaseURL / SupabasePublishableKey are missing from Info.plist. "
                    + "Run scripts/make-ios-config.sh, then rebuild."
            )
        }
        return config
    }

    /// Nil when either value is missing, or when the URL has no host
    /// (for example "http:" because an xcconfig `//` started a comment).
    static func from(infoDictionary info: [String: Any]) -> AppConfig? {
        guard let rawURL = info["SupabaseURL"] as? String,
              let url = URL(string: rawURL), url.host() != nil,
              let key = info["SupabasePublishableKey"] as? String, !key.isEmpty
        else { return nil }
        return AppConfig(supabaseURL: url, supabaseKey: key)
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Create `ios/Droplet/Core/PreviewDropletAPI.swift`**

- Seed timestamps count back from "now", so the demo data never holds a row dated in the future, whatever the local time.
- `sessionStates()` is `nonisolated`, because the protocol requirement is synchronous. It registers the stream's continuation on the actor and yields the current state first.
```swift
import Foundation

/// Fixture data for `PreviewDropletAPI`.
struct PreviewSeed: Sendable {
    var profile: Profile
    var assets: [Asset]
    var transactions: [Transaction]
    var holdings: [Holding]
    var summary: DropSummary

    static let userID = UUID(uuidString: "6F1C3A52-9D4B-4E8A-B7C1-2D5E8F9A0B13")!

    static let curatedAssets: [Asset] = [
        Asset(symbol: "SOL", name: "Solana", chain: "solana",
              iconUrl: URL(string: "https://coin-images.coingecko.com/coins/images/4128/large/solana.png?1718769756"), sortOrder: 1),
        Asset(symbol: "ETH", name: "Ethereum", chain: "base",
              iconUrl: URL(string: "https://coin-images.coingecko.com/coins/images/279/large/ethereum.png?1696501628"), sortOrder: 2),
        Asset(symbol: "CBBTC", name: "Bitcoin (cbBTC)", chain: "base",
              iconUrl: URL(string: "https://coin-images.coingecko.com/coins/images/40143/large/cbbtc.webp?1726136727"), sortOrder: 3),
        Asset(symbol: "AAVE", name: "Aave", chain: "base",
              iconUrl: URL(string: "https://coin-images.coingecko.com/coins/images/12645/large/aave-token-round.png?1720472354"), sortOrder: 4),
        Asset(symbol: "JUP", name: "Jupiter", chain: "solana",
              iconUrl: URL(string: "https://coin-images.coingecko.com/coins/images/34188/large/jup.png?1704266489"), sortOrder: 5),
        Asset(symbol: "RENDER", name: "Render", chain: "solana",
              iconUrl: URL(string: "https://coin-images.coingecko.com/coins/images/11636/large/rndr.png?1696511529"), sortOrder: 6),
    ]

    /// A linked user part-way to their next drop: $3.21 of $5.00 pending, three holdings.
    static let demo: PreviewSeed = {
        let transactions: [Transaction] = [
            purchase("Shake Shack", 1875, hoursAgo: 0.5, .skipped, pending: true),
            purchase("Blue Bottle Coffee", 567, hoursAgo: 3, .pending),
            purchase("Sweetgreen", 1342, hoursAgo: 26, .pending),
            purchase("Uber", 2318, hoursAgo: 30, .pending),
            purchase("Joe's Pizza", 1251, hoursAgo: 46, .pending),
            purchase("Whole Foods Market", 3401, hoursAgo: 49, .pending),
            purchase("Shell", 4000, hoursAgo: 58, .skipped),
            purchase("Duane Reade", 1311, hoursAgo: 70, .invested),
            purchase("Lyft", 1312, hoursAgo: 73, .reversed),
            purchase("Uber", 1823, hoursAgo: 80, .invested),
            purchase("CVS Pharmacy", 2836, hoursAgo: 95, .invested),
            purchase("Pret A Manger", 1207, hoursAgo: 99, .invested),
            purchase("Trader Joe's", 3247, hoursAgo: 118, .invested),
            purchase("Chipotle", 1089, hoursAgo: 123, .invested),
            purchase("Starbucks", 615, hoursAgo: 140, .invested),
            purchase("Target", 2675, hoursAgo: 145, .invested),
            purchase("Netflix", 1549, hoursAgo: 150, .invested),
        ]
        let holdings: [Holding] = [
            holding("SOL", investedCents: 1096, quantity: "0.060074545", daysAgo: 3),
            holding("ETH", investedCents: 527, quantity: "0.001344388", daysAgo: 9),
            holding("CBBTC", investedCents: 503, quantity: "0.000046041", daysAgo: 16),
        ]
        let pending = transactions.filter { $0.roundupStatus == .pending }.reduce(0) { $0 + $1.roundupCents }
        let invested = holdings.reduce(0) { $0 + $1.investedCents }
        return PreviewSeed(
            profile: Profile(id: userID, displayName: "Alex Rivera", thresholdCents: 500, targetAsset: "SOL", roundupsPaused: false),
            assets: curatedAssets,
            transactions: transactions,
            holdings: holdings,
            summary: DropSummary(
                pendingCents: pending, thresholdCents: 500,
                lifetimeRoundupCents: pending + invested, investedCents: invested
            )
        )
    }()

    /// A brand-new user: default settings, no activity.
    static let empty = PreviewSeed(
        profile: Profile(id: userID, displayName: "", thresholdCents: 500, targetAsset: "SOL", roundupsPaused: false),
        assets: curatedAssets,
        transactions: [],
        holdings: [],
        summary: .empty
    )

    /// Timestamps count back from now so the seed never contains future rows.
    private static func purchase(
        _ merchant: String, _ amountCents: Int, hoursAgo: Double, _ status: RoundupStatus, pending: Bool = false
    ) -> Transaction {
        let createdAt = Date.now.addingTimeInterval(-hoursAgo * 3600)
        let roundup = (status == .pending || status == .invested) ? Money.roundupCents(forAmountCents: amountCents) : 0
        return Transaction(
            id: UUID(), merchantName: merchant, amountCents: amountCents, date: DropletDates.dayString(createdAt),
            pending: pending, roundupCents: roundup, roundupStatus: status, createdAt: createdAt
        )
    }

    private static func holding(_ symbol: String, investedCents: Int, quantity: String, daysAgo: Int) -> Holding {
        let asset = curatedAssets.first { $0.symbol == symbol }!
        return Holding(
            assetSymbol: symbol, assetName: asset.name, iconUrl: asset.iconUrl, investedCents: investedCents,
            quantity: quantity, lastInvestedAt: Calendar.current.date(byAdding: .day, value: -daysAgo, to: .now)
        )
    }
}

/// In-memory `DropletAPI` for SwiftUI previews and unit tests. Mirrors the server rules:
/// round-ups accumulate as pending drops and sweep into the target asset at the threshold.
actor PreviewDropletAPI: DropletAPI {
    /// Made-up fill prices for previews only.
    static let prices: [String: String] = [
        "SOL": "182.44", "ETH": "3920.17", "CBBTC": "109250.5", "AAVE": "268.9", "JUP": "0.5128", "RENDER": "3.62",
    ]

    private static let posix = Locale(identifier: "en_US_POSIX")

    private var session: SessionState
    private var email: String?
    private var profileValue: Profile
    private var assetList: [Asset]
    private var bank: BankConnection?
    private var transactionList: [Transaction]
    private var holdingList: [Holding]
    private var summary: DropSummary
    private var nextError: (any Error)?
    private var observers: [UUID: AsyncStream<SessionState>.Continuation] = [:]

    /// Method names in call order, for tests.
    private(set) var calls: [String] = []

    init(signedIn: Bool = true, hasBank: Bool = true, seed: PreviewSeed = .demo) {
        session = signedIn ? .signedIn(seed.profile.id) : .signedOut
        email = signedIn ? "alex@example.com" : nil
        profileValue = seed.profile
        assetList = seed.assets
        bank = hasBank ? BankConnection(institutionName: "First Platypus Bank", status: .active, lastSyncedAt: .now) : nil
        transactionList = seed.transactions
        holdingList = seed.holdings
        summary = seed.summary
    }

    // MARK: Test and preview controls

    /// The next throwing call throws `error`, then the failure clears.
    func fail(next error: (any Error)?) {
        nextError = error
    }

    /// Replaces the bank connection, e.g. to preview the `login_required` state.
    func setBankConnection(_ connection: BankConnection?) {
        bank = connection
    }

    // MARK: Auth

    nonisolated func sessionStates() -> AsyncStream<SessionState> {
        let (stream, continuation) = AsyncStream<SessionState>.makeStream()
        let id = UUID()
        continuation.onTermination = { _ in
            Task { await self.removeObserver(id) }
        }
        Task { await self.addObserver(continuation, id: id) }
        return stream
    }

    func currentEmail() async -> String? {
        calls.append("currentEmail")
        return session == .signedOut ? nil : email
    }

    func signUp(email: String, password: String, name: String) async throws {
        try record("signUp")
        try validate(email: email, password: password)
        self.email = email
        profileValue.displayName = name
        bank = nil
        setSession(.signedIn(profileValue.id))
    }

    func signIn(email: String, password: String) async throws {
        try record("signIn")
        try validate(email: email, password: password)
        self.email = email
        setSession(.signedIn(profileValue.id))
    }

    func signOut() async throws {
        try record("signOut")
        email = nil
        setSession(.signedOut)
    }

    // MARK: Reads and profile

    func profile() async throws -> Profile {
        try record("profile")
        return profileValue
    }

    func updateProfile(_ patch: ProfilePatch) async throws -> Profile {
        try record("updateProfile")
        if let threshold = patch.thresholdCents, ![200, 500, 1000].contains(threshold) {
            throw UserFacingError(message: "Choose a $2, $5 or $10 threshold.", code: "BAD_REQUEST")
        }
        if let symbol = patch.targetAsset, !assetList.contains(where: { $0.symbol == symbol }) {
            throw UserFacingError(message: "\(symbol) isn't available.", code: "BAD_REQUEST")
        }
        if let displayName = patch.displayName { profileValue.displayName = displayName }
        if let threshold = patch.thresholdCents { profileValue.thresholdCents = threshold }
        if let symbol = patch.targetAsset { profileValue.targetAsset = symbol }
        if let paused = patch.roundupsPaused { profileValue.roundupsPaused = paused }
        summary = DropSummary(
            pendingCents: summary.pendingCents, thresholdCents: profileValue.thresholdCents,
            lifetimeRoundupCents: summary.lifetimeRoundupCents, investedCents: summary.investedCents
        )
        return profileValue
    }

    func assets() async throws -> [Asset] {
        try record("assets")
        return assetList.sorted { $0.sortOrder < $1.sortOrder }
    }

    func bankConnection() async throws -> BankConnection? {
        try record("bankConnection")
        return bank
    }

    func transactions(limit: Int) async throws -> [Transaction] {
        try record("transactions")
        let newestFirst = transactionList.sorted { ($0.date, $0.createdAt) > ($1.date, $1.createdAt) }
        return Array(newestFirst.prefix(max(0, limit)))
    }

    func dropSummary() async throws -> DropSummary {
        try record("dropSummary")
        return summary
    }

    func portfolio() async throws -> [Holding] {
        try record("portfolio")
        return holdingList.sorted { $0.investedCents > $1.investedCents }
    }

    // MARK: Edge Functions

    func createLinkToken() async throws -> String {
        try record("createLinkToken")
        return "link-sandbox-" + UUID().uuidString.lowercased()
    }

    func exchange(publicToken: String, institutionName: String?) async throws -> SyncResult {
        try record("exchange")
        bank = BankConnection(institutionName: institutionName, status: .active, lastSyncedAt: .now)
        return SyncResult(added: transactionList.count, modified: 0, removed: 0, investment: sweepIfReady())
    }

    func sync() async throws -> SyncResult {
        try record("sync")
        let linked = try requireBank()
        bank = BankConnection(institutionName: linked.institutionName, status: .active, lastSyncedAt: .now)
        return SyncResult(added: 0, modified: 0, removed: 0, investment: sweepIfReady())
    }

    func simulatePurchase(merchant: String, amountCents: Int) async throws -> SyncResult {
        try record("simulatePurchase")
        let name = merchant.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
        guard (1...60).contains(name.count), (1...100_000).contains(amountCents) else {
            throw UserFacingError(
                message: "Enter a merchant and an amount from $0.01 to $1,000.00.", code: "BAD_REQUEST"
            )
        }
        let linked = try requireBank()
        let now = Date.now
        let roundup = Money.roundupCents(forAmountCents: amountCents)
        transactionList.insert(
            Transaction(
                id: UUID(), merchantName: name, amountCents: amountCents, date: DropletDates.dayString(now),
                pending: false, roundupCents: roundup, roundupStatus: roundup > 0 ? .pending : .skipped, createdAt: now
            ),
            at: 0
        )
        summary = DropSummary(
            pendingCents: summary.pendingCents + roundup, thresholdCents: summary.thresholdCents,
            lifetimeRoundupCents: summary.lifetimeRoundupCents + roundup, investedCents: summary.investedCents
        )
        bank = BankConnection(institutionName: linked.institutionName, status: .active, lastSyncedAt: now)
        return SyncResult(added: 1, modified: 0, removed: 0, investment: sweepIfReady())
    }

    // MARK: Helpers

    private func record(_ name: String) throws {
        calls.append(name)
        if let error = nextError {
            nextError = nil
            throw error
        }
    }

    private func validate(email: String, password: String) throws {
        guard email.contains("@") else {
            throw UserFacingError(message: "Unable to validate email address: invalid format")
        }
        guard password.count >= 6 else {
            throw UserFacingError(message: "Password should be at least 6 characters.")
        }
    }

    private func requireBank() throws -> BankConnection {
        guard let bank else {
            throw UserFacingError(message: "Connect a bank first.", code: "NO_BANK")
        }
        if bank.status == .loginRequired {
            throw UserFacingError(message: "Reconnect your bank to keep rounding up.", code: "ITEM_LOGIN_REQUIRED")
        }
        return bank
    }

    private func addObserver(_ continuation: AsyncStream<SessionState>.Continuation, id: UUID) {
        calls.append("sessionStates")
        observers[id] = continuation
        continuation.yield(session)
    }

    private func removeObserver(_ id: UUID) {
        observers[id] = nil
    }

    private func setSession(_ state: SessionState) {
        session = state
        for continuation in observers.values {
            continuation.yield(state)
        }
    }

    /// Mirrors `sweep_drops`: invest every pending drop once the total reaches the threshold.
    private func sweepIfReady() -> Investment? {
        let amount = summary.pendingCents
        let symbol = profileValue.targetAsset
        guard !profileValue.roundupsPaused, amount > 0, amount >= profileValue.thresholdCents,
              let priceText = Self.prices[symbol],
              let price = Decimal(string: priceText, locale: Self.posix)
        else { return nil }

        let quantity = Self.rounded(Decimal(amount) / 100 / price, scale: 18)
        let investment = Investment(
            id: UUID(), assetSymbol: symbol, amountCents: amount, priceUsd: priceText,
            quantity: "\(quantity)", mode: "simulated", createdAt: .now
        )

        transactionList = transactionList.map { transaction in
            guard transaction.roundupStatus == .pending else { return transaction }
            return Transaction(
                id: transaction.id, merchantName: transaction.merchantName, amountCents: transaction.amountCents,
                date: transaction.date, pending: transaction.pending, roundupCents: transaction.roundupCents,
                roundupStatus: .invested, createdAt: transaction.createdAt
            )
        }

        if let index = holdingList.firstIndex(where: { $0.assetSymbol == symbol }) {
            let current = holdingList[index]
            let total = (Decimal(string: current.quantity, locale: Self.posix) ?? 0) + quantity
            holdingList[index] = Holding(
                assetSymbol: symbol, assetName: current.assetName, iconUrl: current.iconUrl,
                investedCents: current.investedCents + amount, quantity: "\(total)", lastInvestedAt: investment.createdAt
            )
        } else {
            let asset = assetList.first { $0.symbol == symbol }
            holdingList.append(Holding(
                assetSymbol: symbol, assetName: asset?.name ?? symbol, iconUrl: asset?.iconUrl,
                investedCents: amount, quantity: "\(quantity)", lastInvestedAt: investment.createdAt
            ))
        }
        holdingList.sort { $0.investedCents > $1.investedCents }

        summary = DropSummary(
            pendingCents: 0, thresholdCents: summary.thresholdCents,
            lifetimeRoundupCents: summary.lifetimeRoundupCents, investedCents: summary.investedCents + amount
        )
        return investment
    }

    private static func rounded(_ value: Decimal, scale: Int) -> Decimal {
        var input = value
        var result = Decimal()
        NSDecimalRound(&result, &input, scale, .plain)
        return result
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Create `ios/Droplet/Core/LiveDropletAPI.swift`**

This file has no unit test, because it needs a live backend. It must compile against supabase-swift 2.55.4, and Task 18 exercises it end to end.
- `emitLocalSessionAsInitialSession: true` emits the stored session immediately.
  - A stored session that has expired maps to `.signedOut`.
  - The SDK then refreshes the session and emits `tokenRefreshed`, which maps to `.signedIn`.
  - `AppModel` ignores consecutive duplicate states.
- Calls with no input send `EmptyBody()`, which encodes `{}` (C1).
```swift
import Foundation
import Supabase

/// `DropletAPI` backed by Supabase. RLS scopes every read to the signed-in user.
final class LiveDropletAPI: DropletAPI {
    private let client: SupabaseClient

    init(client: SupabaseClient) {
        self.client = client
    }

    convenience init(config: AppConfig) {
        self.init(client: SupabaseClient(
            supabaseURL: config.supabaseURL,
            supabaseKey: config.supabaseKey,
            options: SupabaseClientOptions(
                db: .init(encoder: .droplet, decoder: .droplet),
                auth: .init(emitLocalSessionAsInitialSession: true)
            )
        ))
    }

    // MARK: Auth

    func sessionStates() -> AsyncStream<SessionState> {
        let changes = client.auth.authStateChanges
        return AsyncStream { continuation in
            let task = Task {
                for await change in changes {
                    if let session = change.session, !session.isExpired {
                        continuation.yield(.signedIn(session.user.id))
                    } else {
                        continuation.yield(.signedOut)
                    }
                }
                continuation.finish()
            }
            continuation.onTermination = { _ in task.cancel() }
        }
    }

    func currentEmail() async -> String? {
        client.auth.currentUser?.email
    }

    func signUp(email: String, password: String, name: String) async throws {
        try await client.auth.signUp(email: email, password: password, data: ["display_name": .string(name)])
    }

    func signIn(email: String, password: String) async throws {
        try await client.auth.signIn(email: email, password: password)
    }

    func signOut() async throws {
        try await client.auth.signOut()
    }

    // MARK: Reads and profile

    func profile() async throws -> Profile {
        try await client.from("profiles").select().single().execute().value
    }

    func updateProfile(_ patch: ProfilePatch) async throws -> Profile {
        let uid = try await client.auth.session.user.id
        return try await client.from("profiles")
            .update(patch)
            .eq("id", value: uid)
            .select()
            .single()
            .execute()
            .value
    }

    func assets() async throws -> [Asset] {
        try await client.from("assets")
            .select()
            .eq("enabled", value: true)
            .order("sort_order")
            .execute()
            .value
    }

    func bankConnection() async throws -> BankConnection? {
        let rows: [BankConnection] = try await client.from("bank_connections")
            .select()
            .limit(1)
            .execute()
            .value
        return rows.first
    }

    func transactions(limit: Int) async throws -> [Transaction] {
        try await client.from("transactions")
            .select("id,merchant_name,amount_cents,date,pending,roundup_cents,roundup_status,created_at")
            .order("date", ascending: false)
            .order("created_at", ascending: false)
            .limit(limit)
            .execute()
            .value
    }

    func dropSummary() async throws -> DropSummary {
        try await client.from("drop_summary").select().single().execute().value
    }

    func portfolio() async throws -> [Holding] {
        try await client.from("portfolio_by_asset")
            .select()
            .order("invested_cents", ascending: false)
            .execute()
            .value
    }

    // MARK: Edge Functions (contract C1)

    func createLinkToken() async throws -> String {
        let response: LinkTokenResponse = try await invoke("plaid-link-token", body: EmptyBody())
        return response.linkToken
    }

    func exchange(publicToken: String, institutionName: String?) async throws -> SyncResult {
        try await invoke("plaid-exchange", body: ExchangeBody(publicToken: publicToken, institutionName: institutionName))
    }

    func sync() async throws -> SyncResult {
        try await invoke("plaid-sync", body: EmptyBody())
    }

    func simulatePurchase(merchant: String, amountCents: Int) async throws -> SyncResult {
        try await invoke("simulate-purchase", body: SimulatePurchaseBody(merchant: merchant, amountCents: amountCents))
    }

    private func invoke<Response: Decodable>(_ name: String, body: some Encodable & Sendable) async throws -> Response {
        try await client.functions.invoke(
            name,
            options: FunctionInvokeOptions(body: body, encoder: .droplet),
            decoder: .droplet
        )
    }
}
```

### Task 12: App routing, shared store, auth and connect-bank models

**Files:**
- Create: `ios/Droplet/App/AppModel.swift`
- Create: `ios/Droplet/App/DropletStore.swift`
- Create: `ios/Droplet/Features/Auth/AuthModel.swift`
- Create: `ios/Droplet/Features/Onboarding/ConnectBankModel.swift`
- Test: `ios/DropletTests/AppModelTests.swift`
- Test: `ios/DropletTests/DropletStoreTests.swift`
- Test: `ios/DropletTests/AuthModelTests.swift`
- Test: `ios/DropletTests/ConnectBankModelTests.swift`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - From Task 11: `DropletAPI`, `SessionState`, `PreviewDropletAPI` (including `fail(next:)`, `calls` and `setBankConnection(_:)`), `PreviewSeed` and `UserFacingError.from(_:)`.
  - From Task 10: `Transaction.day` and `DropletDates`.
- Produces:
  - **Everything listed under C5**, with the exact names and signatures given there: `AppModel`, `DropletStore`, `DaySection`, `AuthModel` and `ConnectBankModel`.
  - **Behavior the UI relies on:**
    - `AppModel.start()` runs for the app's lifetime; call it from `.task`. It skips consecutive duplicate states. If `bankConnection()` throws, it routes to `.main`, and `DropletStore` then shows the error.
    - `DropletStore.load()`:
      - It sets `error = nil` on success.
      - On failure it keeps the data already loaded.
      - It ignores `CancellationError` and `URLError(.cancelled)`, for example when a `.refreshable` gets cancelled.
    - `DropletStore.sync()`: on failure it sets `error`, then quietly reloads, so that a `login_required` bank status shows up.
    - The setters (`setThreshold`, `setTargetAsset`, `setPaused`):
      - They update optimistically.
      - On failure they revert and set `error`.
      - On success they refresh `summary`. `setThreshold` also updates `summary.thresholdCents` straight away.
    - `DropletStore.signOut()` always clears every loaded value and `error`, even when the server sign-out call fails.

```swift
import Foundation
import Testing
@testable import Droplet

/// Polls `condition` on the main actor until it holds or `timeout` passes.
@MainActor
private func eventually(timeout: Duration = .seconds(2), _ condition: () -> Bool) async -> Bool {
    let clock = ContinuousClock()
    let deadline = clock.now.advanced(by: timeout)
    while clock.now < deadline {
        if condition() { return true }
        try? await Task.sleep(for: .milliseconds(10))
    }
    return condition()
}

@MainActor
struct AppModelTests {
    @Test func startsLaunching() {
        #expect(AppModel(api: PreviewDropletAPI()).route == .launching)
    }

    @Test func signedOutRoutesToAuth() async {
        let model = AppModel(api: PreviewDropletAPI(signedIn: false))
        let task = Task { await model.start() }
        defer { task.cancel() }
        #expect(await eventually { model.route == .auth })
    }

    @Test func signedInWithBankRoutesToMain() async {
        let model = AppModel(api: PreviewDropletAPI(signedIn: true, hasBank: true))
        let task = Task { await model.start() }
        defer { task.cancel() }
        #expect(await eventually { model.route == .main })
    }

    @Test func signedInWithoutBankRoutesToConnectBank() async {
        let model = AppModel(api: PreviewDropletAPI(signedIn: true, hasBank: false))
        let task = Task { await model.start() }
        defer { task.cancel() }
        #expect(await eventually { model.route == .connectBank })
    }

    @Test func signUpLinkBankThenSignOut() async throws {
        let api = PreviewDropletAPI(signedIn: false)
        let model = AppModel(api: api)
        let task = Task { await model.start() }
        defer { task.cancel() }
        #expect(await eventually { model.route == .auth })

        try await api.signUp(email: "sam@example.com", password: "hunter22", name: "Sam")
        #expect(await eventually { model.route == .connectBank })

        model.bankLinked()
        #expect(model.route == .main)

        try await api.signOut()
        #expect(await eventually { model.route == .auth })
    }

    @Test func signInWithExistingBankRoutesToMain() async throws {
        let api = PreviewDropletAPI(signedIn: false, hasBank: true)
        let model = AppModel(api: api)
        let task = Task { await model.start() }
        defer { task.cancel() }
        #expect(await eventually { model.route == .auth })

        try await api.signIn(email: "alex@example.com", password: "hunter22")
        #expect(await eventually { model.route == .main })
    }

    @Test func bankLookupFailureFallsBackToMain() async {
        let api = PreviewDropletAPI(signedIn: true, hasBank: false)
        await api.fail(next: URLError(.notConnectedToInternet))
        let model = AppModel(api: api)
        let task = Task { await model.start() }
        defer { task.cancel() }
        #expect(await eventually { model.route == .main })
    }
}
```

```swift
import Foundation

@MainActor
struct DropletStoreTests {
    private func transaction(_ merchant: String, _ cents: Int, date: String, hour: Int) -> Transaction {
        let midnight = DropletDates.localMidnight(fromDay: date)!
        let roundup = Money.roundupCents(forAmountCents: cents)
        return Transaction(
            id: UUID(), merchantName: merchant, amountCents: cents, date: date, pending: false,
            roundupCents: roundup, roundupStatus: roundup > 0 ? .pending : .skipped,
            createdAt: midnight.addingTimeInterval(TimeInterval(hour * 3600))
        )
    }

    @Test func signOutClearsStateEvenWhenTheServerCallFails() async {

        let store = DropletStore(api: api)
        await store.load()
        await api.fail(next: URLError(.notConnectedToInternet))
        await store.signOut()
        #expect(await api.calls.last == "signOut")
        #expect(store.profile == nil)
        #expect(store.email == nil)
        #expect(store.bank == nil)
        #expect(store.transactions.isEmpty)
        #expect(store.holdings.isEmpty)
        #expect(store.summary == .empty)
        #expect(store.error == nil)
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Create `ios/Droplet/App/AppModel.swift`**

```swift
import Foundation
import Observation

/// Top-level routing: launching -> auth -> connectBank -> main, driven by the auth session.
@MainActor @Observable final class AppModel {
    enum Route: Equatable {
        case launching, auth, connectBank, main
    }

    private(set) var route: Route = .launching
    let api: any DropletAPI

    init(api: any DropletAPI) {
        self.api = api
    }

    /// Runs for the app's lifetime (call from `.task`). Re-routes on every session change.
    func start() async {
        var last: SessionState?
        for await state in api.sessionStates() {
            guard state != last else { continue }
            last = state
            switch state {
            case .signedOut:
                route = .auth
            case .signedIn:
                route = await routeForSignedInUser()
            }
        }
    }

    /// Called by the onboarding flow once Plaid Link and the exchange succeed.
    func bankLinked() {
        route = .main
    }

    private func routeForSignedInUser() async -> Route {
        do {
            return try await api.bankConnection() == nil ? .connectBank : .main
        } catch {
            // Offline or a transient failure: show the tabs; DropletStore surfaces the error.
            return .main
        }
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Create `ios/Droplet/App/DropletStore.swift`**

```swift
import Foundation
import Observation

struct DaySection: Identifiable, Equatable, Sendable {
    let day: Date
    let items: [Transaction]

    var id: Date { day }
}

/// Shared data for the three tabs. Views read it; mutations go through its async methods.
@MainActor @Observable final class DropletStore {
    private(set) var profile: Profile?
    private(set) var email: String?
    private(set) var assets: [Asset] = []
    private(set) var bank: BankConnection?
    private(set) var summary: DropSummary = .empty
    private(set) var transactions: [Transaction] = []
    private(set) var holdings: [Holding] = []
    private(set) var isLoading = false
    private(set) var isSyncing = false
    private(set) var lastInvestment: Investment?
    var error: UserFacingError?

    private let api: any DropletAPI

    init(api: any DropletAPI) {
        self.api = api
    }

    var totalInvestedCents: Int {
        holdings.reduce(0) { $0 + $1.investedCents }
    }

    var targetAsset: Asset? {
        assets.first { $0.symbol == profile?.targetAsset }
    }

    /// Transactions grouped by calendar day, newest day first; rows keep newest-first order.
    var transactionsByDay: [DaySection] {
        let calendar = Calendar.current
        let groups = Dictionary(grouping: transactions) { calendar.startOfDay(for: $0.day) }
        return groups.keys.sorted(by: >).map { DaySection(day: $0, items: groups[$0] ?? []) }
    }

    func load() async {
        isLoading = true
        defer { isLoading = false }
        do {
            try await fetchAll()
            error = nil
        } catch {
            capture(error)
        }
    }

    func sync() async {
        isSyncing = true
        defer { isSyncing = false }
        do {
            let result = try await api.sync()
            if let investment = result.investment { lastInvestment = investment }
        } catch {
            capture(error)
            try? await fetchAll()
            return
        }
        await load()
    }

    /// Returns true when the purchase was recorded.
    func simulatePurchase(merchant: String, amountCents: Int) async -> Bool {
        isSyncing = true
        defer { isSyncing = false }
        do {
            let result = try await api.simulatePurchase(merchant: merchant, amountCents: amountCents)
            if let investment = result.investment { lastInvestment = investment }
        } catch {
            capture(error)
            return false
        }
        await load()
        return true
    }

    func setThreshold(_ cents: Int) async {
        let previousSummary = summary
        summary = DropSummary(
            pendingCents: summary.pendingCents, thresholdCents: cents,
            lifetimeRoundupCents: summary.lifetimeRoundupCents, investedCents: summary.investedCents
        )
        let saved = await saveProfile(ProfilePatch(thresholdCents: cents)) { $0.thresholdCents = cents }
        if !saved { summary = previousSummary }
    }

    func setTargetAsset(_ symbol: String) async {
        await saveProfile(ProfilePatch(targetAsset: symbol)) { $0.targetAsset = symbol }
    }

    func setPaused(_ paused: Bool) async {
        await saveProfile(ProfilePatch(roundupsPaused: paused)) { $0.roundupsPaused = paused }
    }

    /// Always clears local state, even when the server call fails, so no stale data outlives the session.
    func signOut() async {
        defer { reset() }
        do {
            try await api.signOut()
        } catch {
            // Ignored: the deferred reset() runs either way.
        }
    }

    // MARK: Private

    private func fetchAll() async throws {
        let api = self.api
        async let profileValue = api.profile()
        async let emailValue = api.currentEmail()
        async let assetList = api.assets()
        async let bankValue = api.bankConnection()
        async let summaryValue = api.dropSummary()
        async let transactionList = api.transactions(limit: 200)
        async let holdingList = api.portfolio()

        let loaded = try await (
            profileValue, emailValue, assetList, bankValue, summaryValue, transactionList, holdingList
        )
        profile = loaded.0
        email = loaded.1
        assets = loaded.2.sorted { $0.sortOrder < $1.sortOrder }
        bank = loaded.3
        summary = loaded.4
        transactions = loaded.5.sorted { ($0.date, $0.createdAt) > ($1.date, $1.createdAt) }
        holdings = loaded.6.sorted { $0.investedCents > $1.investedCents }
    }

    /// Applies `change` to the local profile immediately, then persists `patch`.
    /// Reverts and sets `error` on failure; refreshes the summary on success.
    @discardableResult
    private func saveProfile(_ patch: ProfilePatch, optimistic change: (inout Profile) -> Void) async -> Bool {
        let previous = profile
        if var draft = profile {
            change(&draft)
            profile = draft
        }
        do {
            profile = try await api.updateProfile(patch)
        } catch {
            profile = previous
            capture(error)
            return false
        }
        do {
            summary = try await api.dropSummary()
        } catch {
            capture(error)
        }
        return true
    }

    private func capture(_ error: any Error) {
        if error is CancellationError { return }
        if let urlError = error as? URLError, urlError.code == .cancelled { return }
        self.error = UserFacingError.from(error)
    }

    private func reset() {
        profile = nil
        email = nil
        assets = []
        bank = nil
        summary = .empty
        transactions = []
        holdings = []
        lastInvestment = nil
        error = nil
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Create `ios/Droplet/Features/Auth/AuthModel.swift` and `ios/Droplet/Features/Onboarding/ConnectBankModel.swift`**

`ios/Droplet/Features/Auth/AuthModel.swift`:
```swift
import Foundation
import Observation

/// Sign-in / sign-up form state. On success AppModel re-routes via `sessionStates()`.
@MainActor @Observable final class AuthModel {
    enum Mode: Equatable {
        case signIn, signUp
    }

    var mode: Mode = .signUp
    var name = ""
    var email = ""
    var password = ""
    private(set) var isSubmitting = false
    var error: UserFacingError?

    private let api: any DropletAPI

    init(api: any DropletAPI) {
        self.api = api
    }

    var canSubmit: Bool {
        email.contains("@")
            && password.count >= 6
            && (mode == .signIn || !name.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces).isEmpty)
            && !isSubmitting
    }

    func submit() async {
        guard canSubmit else { return }
        isSubmitting = true
        error = nil
        defer { isSubmitting = false }
        let email = email.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines)
        do {
            switch mode {
            case .signUp:
                try await api.signUp(
                    email: email, password: password, name: name.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces)
                )
            case .signIn:
                try await api.signIn(email: email, password: password)
            }
        } catch {
            self.error = UserFacingError.from(error)
        }
    }
}
```

`ios/Droplet/Features/Onboarding/ConnectBankModel.swift`:
```swift
import Foundation
import Observation

/// Plaid Link flow: fetch a link token, present Link, exchange the public token.
@MainActor @Observable final class ConnectBankModel {
    enum Phase: Equatable {
        case idle
        case fetchingToken
        case ready(linkToken: String)
        case exchanging
        case done
    }

    private(set) var phase: Phase = .idle
    var error: UserFacingError?

    private let api: any DropletAPI

    init(api: any DropletAPI) {
        self.api = api
    }

    func prepare() async {
        if phase == .fetchingToken || phase == .exchanging { return }
        error = nil
        phase = .fetchingToken
        do {
            phase = .ready(linkToken: try await api.createLinkToken())
        } catch {
            phase = .idle
            self.error = UserFacingError.from(error)
        }
    }

    func handleSuccess(publicToken: String, institutionName: String?) async {
        error = nil
        phase = .exchanging
        do {
            _ = try await api.exchange(publicToken: publicToken, institutionName: institutionName)
            phase = .done
        } catch {
            phase = .idle
            self.error = UserFacingError.from(error)
        }
    }

    func handleExit(errorMessage: String?) {
        phase = .idle
        error = errorMessage.map { UserFacingError(message: $0) }
    }
}
```

## Part 3 — iOS UI (Tasks 13–17)

These tasks add the design system, every screen, and the app wiring. They use only contracts C4/C5. The code below was compiled together with the Core from Tasks 9–12, with no compiler warnings, and the full Task 12 test suite passes with this `DropletApp` as its host.

How every UI task is verified:
- **No unit tests.** The behavior under these views lives in the C5 models, which Task 12 already tests. Each task's gate is a clean `xcodebuild build`, plus the `#Preview` canvases rendering from `PreviewDropletAPI`. Task 17 adds a unit-test regression run and a Simulator launch screenshot.
- **Regenerate the project after adding files.** XcodeGen globs `ios/Droplet/**` only at generate time, so every build command below starts with `xcodegen generate --spec ios/project.yml`.
- **Build command, used by every task** (run from the repo root):
  `xcodegen generate --spec ios/project.yml && xcodebuild -project ios/Droplet.xcodeproj -scheme Droplet -destination 'platform=iOS Simulator,name=iPhone 17' build 2>&1 | grep -E 'error:|\*\* BUILD'`
  - Expected output on success: the single line `** BUILD SUCCEEDED **`.
  - On failure, each compiler `error:` line prints above `** BUILD FAILED **`.
  - The grep is deliberate: `xcodebuild` ends its log with a blank line, so `| tail -1` would print nothing.

Shared conventions:
- **Environment:**
  - `DropletApp` injects both `AppModel` and `DropletStore` with `.environment(_:)`.
  - Tab screens read `@Environment(DropletStore.self)`. `ProfileView` and `RootView` also read `@Environment(AppModel.self)`, because the Reconnect sheet needs `appModel.api`.
  - Bindings to `@Observable` models go through `@Bindable var x = x` inside `body`.
- **Design rules:**
  - Glass appears only on chrome:
    - the system tab bar and bottom accessory;
    - the `ErrorBanner` capsule;
    - `.glassProminent` primary buttons.
  - Content cards use `Card`, which is `.background(.background.secondary, in: .rect(cornerRadius: 24))`.
  - Every animation is `Theme.spring`. Under Reduce Motion:
    - the press scale becomes a dim;
    - the gauge wave stops;
    - the banner slide becomes a fade.
- **Allocation colors:** the chart uses six categorical colors keyed by asset symbol, so a color always means the same asset. Light and dark variants were validated for color-vision-deficiency separation against the card surfaces (`#F2F2F7` / `#1C1C1E`). The legend and the Holdings list carry the same identity, so identity never depends on color alone.

---

### Task 13: Design system — Theme, DropShape, DropGauge

**Files:**
- Create: `ios/Droplet/Design/Theme.swift`
- Create: `ios/Droplet/Design/DropShape.swift`
- Create: `ios/Droplet/Design/DropGauge.swift`
- Test: none (UI). Verification is the build plus the `#Preview("Theme")`, `#Preview("DropShape")` and `#Preview("DropGauge")` canvases.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - `UserFacingError(message:code:)` and its `id` / `message` (C4);
  - `Money.format(cents:)` (C4);
  - the asset `AccentColor` in `ios/Droplet/Resources/Assets.xcassets` (Task 9).
- Produces:
  - `enum Theme`:
    - `static let accent: Color` (asset "AccentColor")
    - `static let spring: Animation`, `.spring(response: 0.4, dampingFraction: 1.0)`
    - `static let sheetSpring: Animation`, `.spring(response: 0.3, dampingFraction: 0.8)`
    - `static let heroFont: Font` (56 pt semibold)
    - `static let heroTracking: CGFloat = -1.1`
    - `static let cardCornerRadius: CGFloat = 24`
    - `enum Spacing { xxs 2, xs 4, s 8, m 12, l 16, xl 24, xxl 32 }`
  - `extension View { func heroNumber() -> some View }`: 56 pt semibold, tracking −1.1, `.monospacedDigit()`, scaled with Dynamic Type.
  - `struct PressableButtonStyle: ButtonStyle`, also available as `.buttonStyle(.pressable)`.
  - `struct Card<Content: View>: View { init(padding: CGFloat = Theme.Spacing.xl, @ViewBuilder content: () -> Content) }`
  - `struct ErrorBanner: View { init(error: UserFacingError, retry: (() -> Void)?, dismiss: (() -> Void)? = nil) }`
  - `extension View { func errorBanner(_ error: Binding<UserFacingError?>, retry: (() -> Void)? = nil) -> some View }`: floats the banner at the top. Retry and close both clear the binding.
  - `struct DropShape: Shape`
  - `struct DropGauge: View { init(progress: Double, pendingCents: Int, thresholdCents: Int) }`, with:
    - `static func accessibilityLabel(pendingCents: Int, thresholdCents: Int) -> String`, which returns "3 dollars 21 cents of 5 dollars until your next drop";
    - `static func spokenAmount(cents: Int) -> String`;
    - `static func clamp(_ value: Double) -> Double`.
  - `struct WaterShape: Shape { var level: Double; var phase: Double; var amplitude: CGFloat }`

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the design tokens and shared components**

`ios/Droplet/Design/Theme.swift`:
```swift
import SwiftUI

/// Design tokens. Every value here is deliberate; views use these instead of literals.
enum Theme {
    /// Droplet blue: #2F7BF6 light, #5A9BFF dark (Resources/Assets.xcassets/AccentColor).
    static let accent = Color("AccentColor")

    /// Default motion: critically damped, no bounce.
    static let spring = Animation.spring(response: 0.4, dampingFraction: 1.0)
    /// Sheets and drawers.
    static let sheetSpring = Animation.spring(response: 0.3, dampingFraction: 0.8)

    /// Hero numbers: 56 pt semibold. Pair with `.heroNumber()` for tracking, digits and Dynamic Type.
    static let heroFont = Font.system(size: 56, weight: .semibold)
    /// −0.02 em at 56 pt.
    static let heroTracking: CGFloat = -1.1

    static let cardCornerRadius: CGFloat = 24

    enum Spacing {
        static let xxs: CGFloat = 2
        static let xs: CGFloat = 4
        static let s: CGFloat = 8
        static let m: CGFloat = 12
        static let l: CGFloat = 16
        static let xl: CGFloat = 24
        static let xxl: CGFloat = 32
    }
}

// MARK: - Hero number

/// 56 pt semibold, tracking −1.1, tabular digits, scaled with Dynamic Type.
struct HeroNumberModifier: ViewModifier {
    @ScaledMetric(relativeTo: .largeTitle) private var size: CGFloat = 56

    func body(content: Content) -> some View {
        content
            .font(.system(size: size, weight: .semibold))
            .tracking(Theme.heroTracking)
            .monospacedDigit()
            .lineLimit(1)
            .minimumScaleFactor(0.5)
    }
}

extension View {
    func heroNumber() -> some View { modifier(HeroNumberModifier()) }
}

// MARK: - Buttons

/// Scales to 0.97 on press-down with `Theme.spring`. Under Reduce Motion it dims instead of scaling.
struct PressableButtonStyle: ButtonStyle {
    @Environment(\.accessibilityReduceMotion) private var reduceMotion

    func makeBody(configuration: Configuration) -> some View {
        configuration.label
            .contentShape(.rect)
            .scaleEffect(configuration.isPressed && !reduceMotion ? 0.97 : 1)
            .opacity(configuration.isPressed && reduceMotion ? 0.6 : 1)
            .animation(Theme.spring, value: configuration.isPressed)
    }
}

extension ButtonStyle where Self == PressableButtonStyle {
    static var pressable: PressableButtonStyle { PressableButtonStyle() }
}

// MARK: - Card

/// Content container. Never glass: cards sit in the content layer.
struct Card<Content: View>: View {
    private let padding: CGFloat
    private let content: Content

    init(padding: CGFloat = Theme.Spacing.xl, @ViewBuilder content: () -> Content) {
        self.padding = padding
        self.content = content()
    }

    var body: some View {
        content
            .padding(padding)
            .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, alignment: .leading)
            .background(.background.secondary, in: .rect(cornerRadius: Theme.cardCornerRadius))
    }
}

// MARK: - Error banner

/// Floating glass capsule with the error message, an optional Retry and an optional close button.
struct ErrorBanner: View {
    let error: UserFacingError
    let retry: (() -> Void)?
    var dismiss: (() -> Void)? = nil

    var body: some View {
        HStack(spacing: Theme.Spacing.m) {
            Image(systemName: "exclamationmark.triangle.fill")
                .foregroundStyle(.red)
                .accessibilityHidden(true)
            Text(error.message)
                .font(.subheadline.weight(.medium))
                .foregroundStyle(.primary)
                .lineLimit(3)
                .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, alignment: .leading)
            if let retry {
                Button("Retry", action: retry)
                    .font(.subheadline.weight(.semibold))
                    .foregroundStyle(Theme.accent)
                    .buttonStyle(.pressable)
            }
            if let dismiss {
                Button(action: dismiss) {
                    Image(systemName: "xmark")
                        .font(.footnote.weight(.semibold))
                        .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
                        .frame(width: 28, height: 28)
                }
                .buttonStyle(.pressable)
                .accessibilityLabel("Dismiss")
            }
        }
        .padding(.leading, Theme.Spacing.l)
        .padding(.trailing, Theme.Spacing.s)
        .padding(.vertical, Theme.Spacing.m)
        .glassEffect(.regular, in: .capsule)
        .accessibilityElement(children: .contain)
    }
}

/// Shows `error` as an `ErrorBanner` floating at the top; clearing the binding hides it.
struct ErrorBannerModifier: ViewModifier {
    @Binding var error: UserFacingError?
    let retry: (() -> Void)?
    @Environment(\.accessibilityReduceMotion) private var reduceMotion

    func body(content: Content) -> some View {
        content
            .overlay(alignment: .top) {
                if let current = error {
                    ErrorBanner(error: current, retry: retry.map(wrapRetry), dismiss: { error = nil })
                        .padding(.horizontal, Theme.Spacing.l)
                        .padding(.top, Theme.Spacing.s)
                        .transition(reduceMotion ? .opacity : .move(edge: .top).combined(with: .opacity))
                        .id(current.id)
                }
            }
            .animation(Theme.spring, value: error)
    }

    private func wrapRetry(_ action: @escaping () -> Void) -> () -> Void {
        { error = nil; action() }
    }
}

extension View {
    func errorBanner(_ error: Binding<UserFacingError?>, retry: (() -> Void)? = nil) -> some View {
        modifier(ErrorBannerModifier(error: error, retry: retry))
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the drop outline**

`ios/Droplet/Design/DropShape.swift`:
```swift
import SwiftUI

/// A teardrop: pointed tip at the top, round bulb at the bottom.
/// The bulb is a circle of radius `min(width, height) / 2` sitting on the bottom edge;
/// the sides are cubic curves that leave the tip at an angle and meet the bulb tangentially.
struct DropShape: Shape {
    func path(in rect: CGRect) -> Path {
        let radius = min(rect.width, rect.height) / 2
        let centerX = rect.midX
        let centerY = rect.maxY - radius
        let tipY = rect.minY
        let shoulder = (centerY - tipY) * 0.35

        var path = Path()
        path.move(to: CGPoint(x: centerX, y: tipY))
        path.addCurve(
            to: CGPoint(x: centerX + radius, y: centerY),
            control1: CGPoint(x: centerX + radius * 0.35, y: tipY + shoulder),
            control2: CGPoint(x: centerX + radius, y: centerY - radius * 0.9)
        )
        path.addArc(
            center: CGPoint(x: centerX, y: centerY),
            radius: radius,
            startAngle: .degrees(0),
            endAngle: .degrees(180),
            clockwise: false
        )
        path.addCurve(
            to: CGPoint(x: centerX, y: tipY),
            control1: CGPoint(x: centerX - radius, y: centerY - radius * 0.9),
            control2: CGPoint(x: centerX - radius * 0.35, y: tipY + shoulder)
        )
        path.closeSubpath()
        return path
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the filling drop gauge**

`ios/Droplet/Design/DropGauge.swift`:
```swift
import SwiftUI

/// Spending hero: a drop that fills as pending round-ups approach the threshold.
struct DropGauge: View {
    let progress: Double
    let pendingCents: Int
    let thresholdCents: Int

    @Environment(\.accessibilityReduceMotion) private var reduceMotion
    @Environment(\.dynamicTypeSize) private var dynamicTypeSize
    @State private var level: Double = 0

    var body: some View {
        let layout = dynamicTypeSize.isAccessibilitySize
            ? AnyLayout(VStackLayout(alignment: .leading, spacing: Theme.Spacing.l))
            : AnyLayout(HStackLayout(alignment: .center, spacing: Theme.Spacing.xl))
        layout {
            drop
                .frame(width: 104, height: 136)
            VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: Theme.Spacing.xs) {
                Text(Money.format(cents: pendingCents))
                    .heroNumber()
                    .contentTransition(.numericText(value: Double(pendingCents)))
                Text("of \(Money.format(cents: thresholdCents))")
                    .font(.title3.weight(.medium))
                    .monospacedDigit()
                    .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
                Text("until your next drop")
                    .font(.subheadline)
                    .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
            }
        }
        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, alignment: .leading)
        .animation(Theme.spring, value: pendingCents)
        .onAppear { setLevel(progress) }
        .onChange(of: progress) { _, newValue in setLevel(newValue) }
        .accessibilityElement(children: .ignore)
        .accessibilityLabel(Self.accessibilityLabel(pendingCents: pendingCents, thresholdCents: thresholdCents))
        .accessibilityValue("\(Int((Self.clamp(progress) * 100).rounded())) percent")
    }

    private var drop: some View {
        ZStack {
            DropShape()
                .fill(Theme.accent.opacity(0.12))
            water
                .clipShape(DropShape())
            DropShape()
                .stroke(Theme.accent.opacity(0.45), lineWidth: 1.5)
        }
        .accessibilityHidden(true)
    }

    @ViewBuilder private var water: some View {
        if reduceMotion {
            WaterShape(level: level, phase: 0, amplitude: 0)
                .fill(Theme.accent.gradient)
        } else {
            TimelineView(.animation(minimumInterval: 1.0 / 30.0, paused: level <= 0)) { context in
                let seconds = context.date.timeIntervalSinceReferenceDate
                WaterShape(level: level, phase: seconds * 2 * .pi / 3.2, amplitude: 2.5)
                    .fill(Theme.accent.gradient)
            }
        }
    }

    private func setLevel(_ value: Double) {
        let target = Self.clamp(value)
        if reduceMotion {
            level = target
        } else {
            withAnimation(Theme.spring) { level = target }
        }
    }

    static func clamp(_ value: Double) -> Double { min(max(value, 0), 1) }

    /// "3 dollars 21 cents of 5 dollars until your next drop"
    static func accessibilityLabel(pendingCents: Int, thresholdCents: Int) -> String {
        "\(spokenAmount(cents: pendingCents)) of \(spokenAmount(cents: thresholdCents)) until your next drop"
    }

    /// 321 → "3 dollars 21 cents", 500 → "5 dollars", 21 → "21 cents", 0 → "0 dollars".
    static func spokenAmount(cents: Int) -> String {
        let dollars = abs(cents) / 100
        let remainder = abs(cents) % 100
        var parts: [String] = []
        if dollars > 0 || remainder == 0 { parts.append("\(dollars) \(dollars == 1 ? "dollar" : "dollars")") }
        if remainder > 0 { parts.append("\(remainder) \(remainder == 1 ? "cent" : "cents")") }
        return (cents < 0 ? "minus " : "") + parts.joined(separator: " ")
    }
}

/// The water body: filled from the bottom up to `level` (0...1) with a sine-wave surface.
struct WaterShape: Shape {
    var level: Double
    var phase: Double
    var amplitude: CGFloat

    var animatableData: Double {
        get { level }
        set { level = newValue }
    }

    func path(in rect: CGRect) -> Path {
        let clamped = min(max(level, 0), 1)
        guard clamped > 0 else { return Path() }
        let surfaceY = rect.maxY - rect.height * CGFloat(clamped)
        let steps = 32
        var path = Path()
        path.move(to: CGPoint(x: rect.minX, y: rect.maxY))
        for step in 0...steps {
            let fraction = Double(step) / Double(steps)
            let x = rect.minX + rect.width * CGFloat(fraction)
            let y = surfaceY + amplitude * CGFloat(sin(fraction * 2 * .pi + phase))
            path.addLine(to: CGPoint(x: x, y: y))
        }
        path.addLine(to: CGPoint(x: rect.maxX, y: rect.maxY))
        path.closeSubpath()
        return path
    }
}

#Preview("DropGauge") {
    @Previewable @State var pending = 321
    VStack(spacing: Theme.Spacing.xl) {
        Card {
            DropGauge(progress: Double(pending) / 500, pendingCents: pending, thresholdCents: 500)
        }
        Button("Add a round-up") { pending = min(500, pending + 33) }
            .buttonStyle(.glassProminent)
        Card {
            DropGauge(progress: 0, pendingCents: 0, thresholdCents: 1_000)
        }
    }
    .padding(Theme.Spacing.l)
}
```

### Task 14: Auth and Connect Bank screens

**Files:**
- Create: `ios/Droplet/Features/Auth/AuthView.swift`
- Create: `ios/Droplet/Features/Onboarding/ConnectBankView.swift`
- Test: none (UI). Verification is the build plus the `#Preview("Sign up")` and `#Preview("Connect bank")` canvases.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - `AuthModel(api:)`, with `mode`, `name`, `email`, `password`, `isSubmitting`, `error`, `canSubmit` and `submit()` (C5);
  - `ConnectBankModel(api:)`, with `phase: Phase` (`.idle`, `.fetchingToken`, `.ready(linkToken:)`, `.exchanging`, `.done`), `error`, `prepare()`, `handleSuccess(publicToken:institutionName:)` and `handleExit(errorMessage:)` (C5);
  - `any DropletAPI` and `PreviewDropletAPI(signedIn:hasBank:)` (C4);
  - from Task 13: `Theme`, `Card`, `DropShape`, `.pressable`, `.errorBanner(_:retry:)`;
  - LinkKit 7.2: `LinkTokenConfiguration(token:onSuccess:onExit:onEvent:onLoad:)`, `Plaid.createPlaidLinkSession(configuration:) throws -> PlaidLinkSession`, `PlaidLinkSession.sheet()`, `LinkSuccess.publicToken`, `LinkSuccess.metadata.institution.name`, `LinkExit.error?.displayMessage` and `LinkExit.error?.errorMessage`.
- Produces:
  - `struct AuthView: View { init(api: any DropletAPI) }`
  - `struct ConnectBankView: View { init(api: any DropletAPI, onLinked: @escaping @MainActor () -> Void) }`. It calls `onLinked()` once `phase` becomes `.done`.

ConnectBankView flow:
1. It calls `prepare()` on appear.
2. When the phase becomes `.ready(token)`, it creates the `PlaidLinkSession` right away, because creation preloads Link.
3. "Connect bank" is enabled when the session exists. It is also enabled in `.idle`, where a tap fetches a fresh token and opens Link as soon as it is ready.
4. The LinkKit callbacks are `@Sendable`. They copy out plain strings and hop to the main actor, touching only the model.
5. When the phase leaves `.ready`, the view dismisses the sheet. `.idle` also drops the session, because a Link session is single-use.

Error haptics: both screens add `.sensoryFeedback(.error, trigger: model.error) { _, new in new != nil }`.
- `UserFacingError` is `Equatable` and every instance has a fresh UUID `id`, so each new error fires, even one with the same message as the last.
- Both models set `error = nil` when a new attempt starts. The condition skips that change, so clearing an error never buzzes.
