`ios/Droplet/Features/Auth/AuthView.swift`:
```swift
import SwiftUI

struct AuthView: View {
    @State private var model: AuthModel
    @FocusState private var focus: Field?

    private enum Field: Hashable { case name, email, password }

    init(api: any DropletAPI) {
        _model = State(initialValue: AuthModel(api: api))
    }

    var body: some View {
        @Bindable var model = model
        ScrollView {
            VStack(spacing: Theme.Spacing.xxl) {
                wordmark
                VStack(spacing: Theme.Spacing.m) {
                    if model.mode == .signUp {
                        field {
                            TextField("Name", text: $model.name)
                                .textContentType(.name)
                                .submitLabel(.next)
                                .focused($focus, equals: .name)
                                .onSubmit { focus = .email }
                        }
                        .transition(.opacity)
                    }
                    field {
                        TextField("Email", text: $model.email)
                            .textContentType(.emailAddress)
                            .keyboardType(.emailAddress)
                            .textInputAutocapitalization(.never)
                            .autocorrectionDisabled()
                            .submitLabel(.next)
                            .focused($focus, equals: .email)
                            .onSubmit { focus = .password }
                    }
                    field {
                        SecureField("Password", text: $model.password)
                            .textContentType(.password)
                            .submitLabel(.go)
                            .focused($focus, equals: .password)
                            .onSubmit(submit)
                    }
                    hints
                }
                VStack(spacing: Theme.Spacing.l) {
                    Button(action: submit) {
                        ZStack {
                            Text(model.mode == .signUp ? "Create account" : "Sign in")
                                .opacity(model.isSubmitting ? 0 : 1)
                            if model.isSubmitting {
                                ProgressView()
                            }
                        }
                        .font(.headline)
                        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
                        .padding(.vertical, Theme.Spacing.xs)
                    }
                    .buttonStyle(.glassProminent)
                    .controlSize(.large)
                    .disabled(!model.canSubmit)

                    Button(action: toggleMode) {
                        Text(model.mode == .signUp ? "Already have an account? Sign in" : "New to Droplet? Create an account")
                            .font(.subheadline.weight(.medium))
                            .foregroundStyle(Theme.accent)
                    }
                    .buttonStyle(.pressable)
                }
            }
            .padding(.horizontal, Theme.Spacing.xl)
            .padding(.top, 72)
            .padding(.bottom, Theme.Spacing.xxl)
            .frame(maxWidth: 440)
            .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
        }
        .scrollDismissesKeyboard(.interactively)
        .animation(Theme.spring, value: model.mode)
        .animation(Theme.spring, value: model.error)
        .sensoryFeedback(.error, trigger: model.error) { _, new in new != nil }
    }

    private var wordmark: some View {
        VStack(spacing: Theme.Spacing.m) {
            DropShape()
                .fill(Theme.accent.gradient)
                .frame(width: 44, height: 57)
                .accessibilityHidden(true)
            Text("Droplet")
                .font(.largeTitle.weight(.bold))
                .tracking(-0.7)
            Text("Spare change, invested.")
                .font(.body)
                .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
        }
        .accessibilityElement(children: .combine)
    }

    private func field<Content: View>(@ViewBuilder _ content: () -> Content) -> some View {
        content()
            .font(.body)
            .padding(.horizontal, Theme.Spacing.l)
            .frame(minHeight: 52)
            .background(.background.secondary, in: .rect(cornerRadius: 14))
    }

    @ViewBuilder private var hints: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: Theme.Spacing.xs) {
            if !model.email.isEmpty && !model.email.contains("@") {
                Text("Enter a valid email address.")
                    .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
            }
            if !model.password.isEmpty && model.password.count < 6 {
                Text("Use at least 6 characters.")
                    .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
            }
            if let error = model.error {
                Label(error.message, systemImage: "exclamationmark.circle.fill")
                    .foregroundStyle(.red)
            }
        }
        .font(.footnote)
        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, alignment: .leading)
        .padding(.horizontal, Theme.Spacing.xs)
    }

    private func submit() {
        guard model.canSubmit else { return }
        focus = nil
        Task { await model.submit() }
    }

    private func toggleMode() {
        model.mode = model.mode == .signUp ? .signIn : .signUp
        model.error = nil
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the bank-linking screen**

`ios/Droplet/Features/Onboarding/ConnectBankView.swift`:
```swift
import LinkKit
import SwiftUI

struct ConnectBankView: View {
    private let onLinked: @MainActor () -> Void
    @State private var model: ConnectBankModel
    @State private var linkSession: PlaidLinkSession?
    @State private var isPresentingLink = false
    @State private var openWhenReady = false

    init(api: any DropletAPI, onLinked: @escaping @MainActor () -> Void) {
        self.onLinked = onLinked
        _model = State(initialValue: ConnectBankModel(api: api))
    }

    var body: some View {
        @Bindable var model = model
        VStack(spacing: Theme.Spacing.xxl) {
            Spacer(minLength: Theme.Spacing.l)
            explainer
            Spacer(minLength: Theme.Spacing.l)
            VStack(spacing: Theme.Spacing.m) {
                connectButton
                Text("Sandbox login: user_good / pass_good")
                    .font(.footnote.monospaced())
                    .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
                    .textSelection(.enabled)
            }
        }
        .padding(.horizontal, Theme.Spacing.xl)
        .padding(.bottom, Theme.Spacing.l)
        .frame(maxWidth: 440)
        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
        .errorBanner($model.error, retry: connect)
        .sensoryFeedback(.error, trigger: model.error) { _, new in new != nil }
        .sheet(isPresented: $isPresentingLink) {
            if let linkSession {
                linkSession.sheet()
            }
        }
        .task {
            if model.phase == .idle { await model.prepare() }
        }
        .onChange(of: model.phase) { _, phase in handle(phase) }
        .animation(Theme.spring, value: model.phase)
    }

    private var explainer: some View {
        VStack(spacing: Theme.Spacing.xl) {
            ZStack {
                Circle()
                    .fill(Theme.accent.opacity(0.12))
                    .frame(width: 112, height: 112)
                DropShape()
                    .fill(Theme.accent.gradient)
                    .frame(width: 40, height: 52)
            }
            .accessibilityHidden(true)
            VStack(spacing: Theme.Spacing.s) {
                Text("Connect your bank")
                    .font(.largeTitle.weight(.bold))
                    .tracking(-0.7)
                Text("We round up every purchase. Your spare change becomes drops.")
                    .font(.body)
                    .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
            }
            .multilineTextAlignment(.center)
            Card(padding: Theme.Spacing.l) {
                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: Theme.Spacing.m) {
                    step("arrow.up.circle.fill", "Card purchases round up to the next dollar.")
                    step("drop.fill", "When your drops reach the threshold, they're invested.")
                    step("testtube.2", "Trades are simulated in this build.")
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private func step(_ symbol: String, _ text: String) -> some View {
        HStack(alignment: .firstTextBaseline, spacing: Theme.Spacing.m) {
            Image(systemName: symbol)
                .foregroundStyle(Theme.accent)
                .frame(width: 24)
                .accessibilityHidden(true)
            Text(text)
                .font(.subheadline)
                .foregroundStyle(.primary)
        }
    }

    private var connectButton: some View {
        Button(action: connect) {
            HStack(spacing: Theme.Spacing.s) {
                if isBusy {
                    ProgressView()
                }
                Text(buttonTitle)
            }
            .font(.headline)
            .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
            .padding(.vertical, Theme.Spacing.xs)
        }
        .buttonStyle(.glassProminent)
        .controlSize(.large)
        .disabled(!canConnect)
    }

    private var buttonTitle: String {
        switch model.phase {
        case .idle, .ready: "Connect bank"
        case .fetchingToken: "Preparing…"
        case .exchanging: "Linking your bank…"
        case .done: "Connected"
        }
    }

    private var isBusy: Bool {
        model.phase == .fetchingToken || model.phase == .exchanging
    }

    private var canConnect: Bool {
        switch model.phase {
        case .idle: true
        case .ready: linkSession != nil
        case .fetchingToken, .exchanging, .done: false
        }
    }

    private func connect() {
        switch model.phase {
        case .ready where linkSession != nil:
            isPresentingLink = true
        case .idle:
            openWhenReady = true
            Task { await model.prepare() }
        default:
            break
        }
    }

    private func handle(_ phase: ConnectBankModel.Phase) {
        switch phase {
        case .ready(let token):
            linkSession = makeSession(token: token)
            if openWhenReady, linkSession != nil {
                openWhenReady = false
                isPresentingLink = true
            }
        case .idle:
            // Link exited or a step failed. A Link session is single-use: the next attempt fetches a fresh token.
            isPresentingLink = false
            linkSession = nil
            openWhenReady = false
        case .exchanging:
            isPresentingLink = false
        case .done:
            onLinked()
        case .fetchingToken:
            break
        }
    }

    /// Creating the session preloads Link. Callbacks hop to the main actor and only touch the model.
    private func makeSession(token: String) -> PlaidLinkSession? {
        let model = model
        let configuration = LinkTokenConfiguration(
            token: token,
            onSuccess: { @Sendable success in
                let publicToken = success.publicToken
                let institutionName = success.metadata.institution.name
                Task { @MainActor in
                    await model.handleSuccess(publicToken: publicToken, institutionName: institutionName)
                }
            },
            onExit: { @Sendable exit in
                let message = exit.error?.displayMessage ?? exit.error?.errorMessage
                Task { @MainActor in
                    model.handleExit(errorMessage: message)
                }
            },
            onEvent: nil,
            onLoad: nil
        )
        do {
            return try Plaid.createPlaidLinkSession(configuration: configuration)
        } catch {
            model.handleExit(errorMessage: error.localizedDescription)
            return nil
        }
    }
}

}
```

- Consumes:
  - From `DropletStore` (C5): `summary`, `transactions`, `transactionsByDay: [DaySection]`, `bank?.status`, `profile?.roundupsPaused`, `targetAsset?.name`, `isLoading` and `sync()`.
  - `DaySection` (`day`, `items`).
  - `Transaction`: `merchantName`, `amountCents`, `pending`, `roundupCents`, `roundupStatus` and `day`.
  - `RoundupStatus`, `BankStatus.loginRequired`, `Money.format(cents:)` and `Money.formatSigned(cents:)`.
  - `PreviewDropletAPI(seed:)`, `PreviewSeed.demo` and `PreviewSeed.empty`.
  - From Task 13: `Card`, `DropGauge` and `Theme`.
- Produces:
  - `struct TransactionRow: View { let transaction: Transaction }`
  - `struct SpendingView: View`, built with `SpendingView()` and reading the store from the environment.
  - `static func SpendingView.title(for day: Date) -> String`, which returns "Today", "Yesterday" or "Wednesday, Oct 22".

How `TransactionRow` renders each status:
- **Amount:** shown as money out, `Money.format(cents: -amountCents)`, so a 567-cent purchase reads "-$5.67".
- **`pending`:** "+$0.33" in accent.
- **`invested`:** a small `drop.fill` badge plus "+$0.33" in accent.
- **`skipped`:** "—" in tertiary.
- **`reversed`:** merchant and amount struck through in tertiary, a "· Reversed" subtitle, and "—".

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the transaction row**

`ios/Droplet/Features/Spending/TransactionRow.swift`:
```swift
import SwiftUI

/// One purchase: merchant and date on the left, amount and round-up on the right.
struct TransactionRow: View {
    let transaction: Transaction

    private var isReversed: Bool { transaction.roundupStatus == .reversed }

    var body: some View {
        HStack(alignment: .firstTextBaseline, spacing: Theme.Spacing.m) {
            VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: Theme.Spacing.xxs) {
                Text(transaction.merchantName)
                    .font(.body.weight(.medium))
                    .lineLimit(1)
                    .strikethrough(isReversed)
                    .foregroundStyle(isReversed ? .tertiary : .primary)
                Text(subtitle)
                    .font(.footnote)
                    .foregroundStyle(isReversed ? .tertiary : .secondary)
            }
            Spacer(minLength: Theme.Spacing.m)
            VStack(alignment: .trailing, spacing: Theme.Spacing.xxs) {
                Text(Money.format(cents: -transaction.amountCents))
                    .font(.body)
                    .monospacedDigit()
                    .strikethrough(isReversed)
                    .foregroundStyle(isReversed ? .tertiary : .primary)
                roundup
            }
        }
        .padding(.vertical, Theme.Spacing.m)
        .accessibilityElement(children: .combine)
    }

    private var subtitle: String {
        let date = transaction.day.formatted(.dateTime.month(.abbreviated).day())
        if isReversed { return "\(date) · Reversed" }
        if transaction.pending { return "\(date) · Pending" }
        return date
    }

    @ViewBuilder private var roundup: some View {
        switch transaction.roundupStatus {
        case .pending:
            Text(Money.formatSigned(cents: transaction.roundupCents))
                .font(.subheadline.weight(.semibold))
                .monospacedDigit()
                .foregroundStyle(Theme.accent)
        case .invested:
            HStack(spacing: Theme.Spacing.xs) {
                Image(systemName: "drop.fill")
                    .font(.caption2)
                    .accessibilityLabel("Invested")
                Text(Money.formatSigned(cents: transaction.roundupCents))
                    .font(.subheadline.weight(.semibold))
                    .monospacedDigit()
            }
            .foregroundStyle(Theme.accent)
        case .skipped, .reversed:
            Text("—")
                .font(.subheadline)
                .foregroundStyle(.tertiary)
                .accessibilityLabel("No round-up")
        }
    }
}

            ForEach(PreviewSeed.demo.transactions) { transaction in
                TransactionRow(transaction: transaction)
            }
        }
        .padding(.horizontal, Theme.Spacing.l)
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the Spending screen**

`ios/Droplet/Features/Spending/SpendingView.swift`:
```swift
import SwiftUI

struct SpendingView: View {
    @Environment(DropletStore.self) private var store

    var body: some View {
        NavigationStack {
            ScrollView {
                LazyVStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: Theme.Spacing.xl) {
                    hero
                    if store.bank?.status == .loginRequired {
                        notice("exclamationmark.triangle.fill", "Your bank needs to be reconnected. Open Profile to reconnect it.")
                    }
                    if store.profile?.roundupsPaused == true {
                        notice("pause.circle.fill", "Round-ups are paused. Resume them in Profile.")
                    }
                    if store.transactions.isEmpty {
                        emptyState
                    } else {
                        ForEach(store.transactionsByDay) { section in
                            daySection(section)
                        }
                    }
                }
                .padding(.horizontal, Theme.Spacing.l)
                .padding(.bottom, Theme.Spacing.xl)
            }
            .navigationTitle("Spending")
            .refreshable { await store.sync() }
        }
    }

    private var hero: some View {
        Card {
            VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: Theme.Spacing.xl) {
                DropGauge(
                    progress: store.summary.progress,
                    pendingCents: store.summary.pendingCents,
                    thresholdCents: store.summary.thresholdCents
                )
                HStack(alignment: .top) {
                    stat("Rounded up so far", Money.format(cents: store.summary.lifetimeRoundupCents))
                    Spacer(minLength: Theme.Spacing.l)
                    stat("Next drop goes to", store.targetAsset?.name ?? "—")
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private func stat(_ label: String, _ value: String) -> some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: Theme.Spacing.xxs) {
            Text(label)
                .font(.caption)
                .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
            Text(value)
                .font(.subheadline.weight(.semibold))
                .monospacedDigit()
        }
        .accessibilityElement(children: .combine)
    }

    private func notice(_ symbol: String, _ text: String) -> some View {
        Card(padding: Theme.Spacing.l) {
            HStack(alignment: .firstTextBaseline, spacing: Theme.Spacing.m) {
                Image(systemName: symbol)
                    .foregroundStyle(Theme.accent)
                    .accessibilityHidden(true)
                Text(text)
                    .font(.subheadline)
            }
        }
    }

    @ViewBuilder private var emptyState: some View {
        if store.isLoading {
            ProgressView()
                .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
                .padding(.top, Theme.Spacing.xxl)
        } else {
            ContentUnavailableView(
                "No purchases yet",
                systemImage: "drop",
                description: Text("Add a test purchase in Profile to see your first drop.")
            )
            .padding(.top, Theme.Spacing.l)
        }
    }

    private func daySection(_ section: DaySection) -> some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: Theme.Spacing.s) {
            Text(Self.title(for: section.day))
                .font(.subheadline.weight(.semibold))
                .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
                .padding(.horizontal, Theme.Spacing.xs)
                .accessibilityAddTraits(.isHeader)
            Card(padding: 0) {
                VStack(spacing: 0) {
                    ForEach(section.items) { transaction in
                        TransactionRow(transaction: transaction)
                    }
                }
                .padding(.horizontal, Theme.Spacing.l)
            }
        }
    }

    /// "Today", "Yesterday", otherwise "Wednesday, Oct 22".
    static func title(for day: Date) -> String {
        let calendar = Calendar.current
        if calendar.isDateInToday(day) { return "Today" }
        if calendar.isDateInYesterday(day) { return "Yesterday" }
        return day.formatted(.dateTime.weekday(.wide).month(.abbreviated).day())
    }
}

#Preview("Spending") {

    SpendingView()
        .environment(store)
        .task { await store.load() }
}

#Preview("Spending, empty") {
    let store = DropletStore(api: PreviewDropletAPI(seed: .empty))
    SpendingView()
        .environment(store)
        .task { await store.load() }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the allocation donut**

`ios/Droplet/Features/Portfolio/AllocationChart.swift`:
```swift
import Charts
import SwiftUI
import UIKit

/// Donut of invested cents per asset. At most `maxSlices` sectors; the tail folds into "Other".
struct AllocationChart: View {
    let holdings: [Holding]

    struct Slice: Identifiable, Equatable {
        let id: String
        let cents: Int
        let color: Color
    }

    static let maxSlices = 6

    var body: some View {
        let slices = Self.slices(for: holdings)
        let total = slices.reduce(0) { $0 + $1.cents }
        Chart(slices) { slice in
            SectorMark(
                angle: .value("Invested", slice.cents),
                innerRadius: .ratio(0.618),
                angularInset: 1.5
            )
            .cornerRadius(4)
            .foregroundStyle(by: .value("Asset", slice.id))
            .accessibilityLabel(slice.id)
            .accessibilityValue(Money.format(cents: slice.cents))
        }
        .chartForegroundStyleScale(domain: slices.map(\.id), range: slices.map(\.color))
        .chartLegend(position: .bottom, alignment: .center, spacing: Theme.Spacing.l)
        .chartBackground { proxy in
            GeometryReader { geometry in
                if let plotFrame = proxy.plotFrame, let top = slices.first, total > 0 {
                    let frame = geometry[plotFrame]
                    VStack(spacing: 0) {
                        Text(Double(top.cents) / Double(total), format: .percent.precision(.fractionLength(0)))
                            .font(.title2.weight(.semibold))
                            .monospacedDigit()
                        Text(top.id)
                            .font(.caption)
                            .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
                    }
                    .position(x: frame.midX, y: frame.midY)
                    .accessibilityHidden(true)
                }
            }
        }
        .frame(height: 240)
    }

    static func slices(for holdings: [Holding]) -> [Slice] {
        let sorted = holdings
            .filter { $0.investedCents > 0 }
            .sorted { $0.investedCents > $1.investedCents }
        let slice = { (holding: Holding) in
            Slice(id: holding.assetSymbol, cents: holding.investedCents, color: color(for: holding.assetSymbol))
        }
        guard sorted.count > maxSlices else { return sorted.map(slice) }
        let head = sorted.prefix(maxSlices - 1).map(slice)
        let rest = sorted.dropFirst(maxSlices - 1).reduce(0) { $0 + $1.investedCents }
        return head + [Slice(id: "Other", cents: rest, color: otherColor)]
    }

    /// Fixed color per curated asset, so a color always means the same asset.
    /// Categorical slots 1–6 (light/dark), validated for CVD separation on the card surfaces.
    static func color(for symbol: String) -> Color {
        switch symbol {
        case "SOL": dynamic(light: 0x2A78D6, dark: 0x3987E5)
        case "ETH": dynamic(light: 0xEB6834, dark: 0xD95926)
        case "CBBTC": dynamic(light: 0x1BAF7A, dark: 0x199E70)
        case "AAVE": dynamic(light: 0xEDA100, dark: 0xC98500)
        case "JUP": dynamic(light: 0xE87BA4, dark: 0xD55181)
        case "RENDER": dynamic(light: 0x008300, dark: 0x008300)
        default: dynamic(light: 0x6250D6, dark: 0x9085E9)
        }
    }

    static let otherColor = dynamic(light: 0x8E8E93, dark: 0x8E8E93)

    private static func dynamic(light: UInt32, dark: UInt32) -> Color {
        Color(uiColor: UIColor { traits in
            uiColor(hex: traits.userInterfaceStyle == .dark ? dark : light)
        })
    }

    private static func uiColor(hex: UInt32) -> UIColor {
        UIColor(
            red: CGFloat((hex >> 16) & 0xFF) / 255,
            green: CGFloat((hex >> 8) & 0xFF) / 255,
            blue: CGFloat(hex & 0xFF) / 255,
            alpha: 1
        )
    }
}

    }
    .padding(Theme.Spacing.l)
}
```

```swift
import SwiftUI

/// One asset: icon, symbol and name, then invested dollars and quantity.
struct HoldingRow: View {
    let holding: Holding

    var body: some View {
        HStack(spacing: Theme.Spacing.m) {
            icon
            VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: Theme.Spacing.xxs) {
                Text(holding.assetSymbol)
                    .font(.body.weight(.semibold))
                Text(holding.assetName)
                    .font(.footnote)
                    .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
                    .lineLimit(1)
            }
            Spacer(minLength: Theme.Spacing.m)
            VStack(alignment: .trailing, spacing: Theme.Spacing.xxs) {
                Text(Money.format(cents: holding.investedCents))
                    .font(.body.weight(.medium))
                    .monospacedDigit()
                Text("\(Money.formatQuantity(holding.quantity)) \(holding.assetSymbol)")
                    .font(.footnote)
                    .monospacedDigit()
                    .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
                    .lineLimit(1)
            }
        }
        .padding(.vertical, Theme.Spacing.m)
        .accessibilityElement(children: .combine)
    }

    private var icon: some View {
        AsyncImage(url: holding.iconUrl) { phase in
            if let image = phase.image {
                image
                    .resizable()
                    .scaledToFit()
            } else {
                Circle()
                    .fill(AllocationChart.color(for: holding.assetSymbol).opacity(0.18))
                    .overlay {
                        Text(holding.assetSymbol.prefix(1))
                            .font(.footnote.weight(.semibold))
                            .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
                    }
            }
        }
        .frame(width: 36, height: 36)
        .clipShape(.circle)
        .accessibilityHidden(true)
    }
}
```

```swift
import SwiftUI

struct PortfolioView: View {
    @Environment(DropletStore.self) private var store

    var body: some View {
        NavigationStack {
            ScrollView {
                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: Theme.Spacing.xl) {
                    hero
                    if store.holdings.isEmpty {
                        emptyState
                    } else {
                        Card {
                            AllocationChart(holdings: store.holdings)
                        }
                        holdings
                    }
                }
                .padding(.horizontal, Theme.Spacing.l)
                .padding(.bottom, Theme.Spacing.xl)
            }
            .navigationTitle("Portfolio")
            .refreshable { await store.load() }
        }
    }

    private var hero: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: Theme.Spacing.xs) {
            HStack(spacing: Theme.Spacing.s) {
                Text("Invested from round-ups")
                    .font(.subheadline)
                    .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
                Spacer(minLength: Theme.Spacing.s)
                SimulatedBadge()
            }
            Text(Money.format(cents: store.totalInvestedCents))
                .heroNumber()
                .contentTransition(.numericText(value: Double(store.totalInvestedCents)))
                .animation(Theme.spring, value: store.totalInvestedCents)
        }
        .padding(.horizontal, Theme.Spacing.xs)
        .accessibilityElement(children: .combine)
    }

    private var holdings: some View {
        VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: Theme.Spacing.s) {
            Text("Holdings")
                .font(.subheadline.weight(.semibold))
                .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
                .padding(.horizontal, Theme.Spacing.xs)
                .accessibilityAddTraits(.isHeader)
            Card(padding: 0) {
                VStack(spacing: 0) {
                    ForEach(store.holdings) { holding in
                        HoldingRow(holding: holding)
                    }
                }
                .padding(.horizontal, Theme.Spacing.l)
            }
            Text("Amounts are what was invested, not current value. Fills are simulated at the live True Markets price.")
                .font(.footnote)
                .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
                .padding(.horizontal, Theme.Spacing.xs)
        }
    }

    private var emptyState: some View {
        ContentUnavailableView(
            "No drops invested yet",
            systemImage: "chart.pie",
            description: Text("Your first drop lands at \(Money.format(cents: store.summary.thresholdCents)).")
        )
        .padding(.top, Theme.Spacing.l)
    }
}

/// Small capsule that marks investments as simulated. Content layer, so no glass.

    var body: some View {
        Text("Simulated")
            .font(.caption.weight(.semibold))
            .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
            .padding(.horizontal, Theme.Spacing.s)
            .padding(.vertical, Theme.Spacing.xs)
            .background(.fill.tertiary, in: .capsule)
            .accessibilityLabel("Simulated trades")
    }
}
```

**Files:**

- Create: `ios/Droplet/Features/Profile/TestPurchaseSheet.swift`
- Create: `ios/Droplet/Features/Profile/ProfileView.swift`

- Test: the existing `DropletTests` suite (a regression check that the host app still launches) plus a Simulator launch screenshot.

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - `AppModel`: `route`, `api`, `start()` and `bankLinked()` (C5).
  - From `DropletStore` (C5):
    - data: `profile`, `email`, `assets`, `bank`, `summary`, `isSyncing`, `lastInvestment` and `error` (settable);
    - actions: `load()`, `sync()`, `simulatePurchase(merchant:amountCents:) -> Bool`, `setThreshold(_:)`, `setTargetAsset(_:)`, `setPaused(_:)` and `signOut()`.
  - From C4: `Investment`, `LiveDropletAPI(config:)`, `AppConfig.fromBundle()`, `Money.roundupCents(forAmountCents:)`, `PreviewDropletAPI` and `PreviewSeed.demo.summary`.
  - From Tasks 13–16: every view.
- Produces:
  - `struct NextDropPill: View { init(summary: DropSummary, celebrating: Investment? = nil) }`
  - `struct TestPurchaseSheet: View`, with:
    - `static let presets: [TestPurchaseSheet.Preset]`: Blue Bottle 567, Sweetgreen 1342, Uber 2318;
    - `static func cents(from text: String) -> Int?`, where "5.67" → 567 and anything outside 1…100000 cents → nil.
  - `struct ProfileView: View`, with `static let thresholdOptions = [200, 500, 1_000]`.
  - `struct MainTabs: View`, with `enum Destination { case portfolio, spending, profile }`.
  - `struct RootView: View`
  - `@main struct DropletApp: App`

What `MainTabs` sets up:
- Three `Tab`s:
  - "Portfolio" / `chart.pie.fill`;
  - "Spending" / `drop.fill`;
  - "Profile" / `person.crop.circle.fill`.
- `.tabBarMinimizeBehavior(.onScrollDown)`.
- `.tabViewBottomAccessory { NextDropPill }`, where a tap jumps to Spending.
  - In `.inline` placement the pill drops its caption.
  - For 4 s after `lastInvestment` changes, it shows "Invested $5.28 in SOL".
- `.task { await store.load() }`, plus a reload whenever `scenePhase` becomes `.active`.
- `.sensoryFeedback(.success)` and `.sensoryFeedback(.error)`, each firing only when the new value is non-nil.
- The ErrorBanner overlay, whose Retry calls `load()`.

`ProfileView` keeps local mirrors of threshold, asset and pause, so the controls respond instantly. It writes through the store and re-syncs from `store.profile` afterwards. A `.selection` haptic fires only on user-initiated picker changes.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the bottom-accessory pill**

`ios/Droplet/Features/Shared/NextDropPill.swift`:
```swift
import SwiftUI

/// Bottom-accessory content: progress ring plus "$3.21 / $5.00".
/// For a few seconds after an investment it shows "Invested $5.28 in SOL" instead.
/// The system draws the accessory's glass; this view adds none.
struct NextDropPill: View {
    let summary: DropSummary
    var celebrating: Investment? = nil

    @Environment(\.tabViewBottomAccessoryPlacement) private var placement

    var body: some View {
        HStack(spacing: Theme.Spacing.s) {
            if let investment = celebrating {
                Image(systemName: "drop.fill")
                    .foregroundStyle(Theme.accent)
                Text("Invested \(Money.format(cents: investment.amountCents)) in \(investment.assetSymbol)")
                    .font(.subheadline.weight(.semibold))
                    .monospacedDigit()
                    .lineLimit(1)
            } else {
                ProgressRing(progress: summary.progress)
                    .frame(width: 20, height: 20)
                Text("\(Money.format(cents: summary.pendingCents)) / \(Money.format(cents: summary.thresholdCents))")
                    .font(.subheadline.weight(.semibold))
                    .monospacedDigit()
                    .contentTransition(.numericText(value: Double(summary.pendingCents)))
                    .lineLimit(1)
                if placement != .inline {
                    Text("until your next drop")
                        .font(.subheadline)
                        .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
                        .lineLimit(1)
                }
            }
        }
        .padding(.horizontal, Theme.Spacing.l)
        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity)
        .contentShape(.rect)
        .animation(Theme.spring, value: summary)
        .animation(Theme.spring, value: celebrating)
        .accessibilityElement(children: .ignore)
        .accessibilityLabel(accessibilityText)
    }

    private var accessibilityText: String {
        if let investment = celebrating {
            return "Invested \(DropGauge.spokenAmount(cents: investment.amountCents)) in \(investment.assetSymbol)"
        }
        return DropGauge.accessibilityLabel(pendingCents: summary.pendingCents, thresholdCents: summary.thresholdCents)
    }
}

private struct ProgressRing: View {
    let progress: Double

    var body: some View {
        ZStack {
            Circle()
                .stroke(Theme.accent.opacity(0.2), lineWidth: 3)
            Circle()
                .trim(from: 0, to: DropGauge.clamp(progress))
                .stroke(Theme.accent, style: StrokeStyle(lineWidth: 3, lineCap: .round))
                .rotationEffect(.degrees(-90))
        }
        .accessibilityHidden(true)
    }
}

    }

    .glassEffect(.regular, in: .rect(cornerRadius: Theme.cardCornerRadius))
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the developer test-purchase sheet**

`ios/Droplet/Features/Profile/TestPurchaseSheet.swift`:
```swift
import SwiftUI

/// Developer sheet: stage a Plaid Sandbox purchase, then sync.
struct TestPurchaseSheet: View {
    @Environment(DropletStore.self) private var store
    @Environment(\.dismiss) private var dismiss
    @State private var merchant = ""
    @State private var amount = ""
    @State private var submitting: String?
    @State private var failure: UserFacingError?

    struct Preset: Identifiable {
        let merchant: String
        let amountCents: Int
        let systemImage: String
        var id: String { merchant }
    }

    static let presets = [
        Preset(merchant: "Blue Bottle", amountCents: 567, systemImage: "cup.and.saucer.fill"),
        Preset(merchant: "Sweetgreen", amountCents: 1_342, systemImage: "leaf.fill"),
        Preset(merchant: "Uber", amountCents: 2_318, systemImage: "car.fill"),
    ]

    private var customCents: Int? { Self.cents(from: amount) }
    private var trimmedMerchant: String { merchant.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespacesAndNewlines) }
    private var canAddCustom: Bool {
        customCents != nil && (1...60).contains(trimmedMerchant.count) && submitting == nil
    }

    var body: some View {
        NavigationStack {
            Form {
                Section {
                    ForEach(Self.presets) { preset in
                        Button {
                            add(merchant: preset.merchant, cents: preset.amountCents, tag: preset.id)
                        } label: {
                            presetRow(preset)
                        }
                    }
                } header: {
                    Text("Presets")
                } footer: {
                    Text("Each purchase posts to your Sandbox bank today. Droplet then syncs and rounds it up.")
                }

                Section("Custom") {
                    TextField("Merchant", text: $merchant)
                        .textInputAutocapitalization(.words)
                    LabeledContent("Amount") {
                        TextField("0.00", text: $amount)
                            .keyboardType(.decimalPad)
                            .multilineTextAlignment(.trailing)
                            .monospacedDigit()
                    }
                    if let cents = customCents {
                        LabeledContent("Round-up") {
                            Text(Money.formatSigned(cents: Money.roundupCents(forAmountCents: cents)))
                                .monospacedDigit()
                                .foregroundStyle(Theme.accent)
                        }
                    }
                    Button {
                        if let cents = customCents {
                            add(merchant: trimmedMerchant, cents: cents, tag: "custom")
                        }
                    } label: {
                        HStack {
                            Text("Add purchase")
                            Spacer()
                            if submitting == "custom" {
                                ProgressView()
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    .disabled(!canAddCustom)
                }

                if let failure {
                    Section {
                        Label(failure.message, systemImage: "exclamationmark.triangle.fill")
                            .font(.subheadline)
                            .foregroundStyle(.red)
                    }
                }
            }
            .disabled(submitting != nil)
            .animation(Theme.spring, value: customCents)
            .animation(Theme.spring, value: failure)
            .navigationTitle("Add test purchase")
            .navigationBarTitleDisplayMode(.inline)
            .toolbar {
                ToolbarItem(placement: .cancellationAction) {
                    Button("Cancel", role: .cancel) { dismiss() }
                }
            }
        }
        .presentationDetents([.medium, .large])
        .presentationDragIndicator(.visible)
    }

    private func presetRow(_ preset: Preset) -> some View {
        HStack(spacing: Theme.Spacing.m) {
            Image(systemName: preset.systemImage)
                .foregroundStyle(Theme.accent)
                .frame(width: 28)
                .accessibilityHidden(true)
            // Color.primary, not .primary: inside a Form button, .primary resolves to the tint.
            Text(preset.merchant)
                .foregroundStyle(Color.primary)
            Spacer()
            if submitting == preset.id {
                ProgressView()
            } else {
                VStack(alignment: .trailing, spacing: Theme.Spacing.xxs) {
                    Text(Money.format(cents: preset.amountCents))
                        .foregroundStyle(Color.primary)
                    Text(Money.formatSigned(cents: Money.roundupCents(forAmountCents: preset.amountCents)))
                        .font(.footnote.weight(.semibold))
                        .foregroundStyle(Theme.accent)
                }
                .monospacedDigit()
            }
        }
        .accessibilityElement(children: .combine)
    }

    private func add(merchant: String, cents: Int, tag: String) {
        submitting = tag
        failure = nil
        Task {
            let succeeded = await store.simulatePurchase(merchant: merchant, amountCents: cents)
            submitting = nil
            if succeeded {
                dismiss()
            } else {
                // Show the error here, under the sheet's own content, instead of the hidden tab banner.
                failure = store.error
                store.error = nil
            }
        }
    }

    /// "5.67" → 567, "12" → 1200, "0.5" → 50. Nil unless 1...100_000 cents with at most 2 decimals.
    static func cents(from text: String) -> Int? {
        let cleaned = text.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces).replacingOccurrences(of: ",", with: ".")
        let parts = cleaned.split(separator: ".", omittingEmptySubsequences: false).map(String.init)
        let digits = Set("0123456789")
        guard (1...2).contains(parts.count),
              (1...4).contains(parts[0].count),
              parts.count == 1 || (1...2).contains(parts[1].count),
              parts.allSatisfy({ $0.allSatisfy(digits.contains) })
        else { return nil }
        let dollars = Int(parts[0]) ?? 0
        let fraction = parts.count == 2 ? (Int(parts[1].count == 1 ? parts[1] + "0" : parts[1]) ?? 0) : 0
        let total = dollars * 100 + fraction
        return (1...100_000).contains(total) ? total : nil
    }
}

#Preview("Test purchase") {
    let store = DropletStore(api: PreviewDropletAPI())
    Color.clear
        .sheet(isPresented: .constant(true)) {
            TestPurchaseSheet()
                .environment(store)
        }
        .task { await store.load() }
}
```

```swift
import SwiftUI

struct ProfileView: View {
    @Environment(DropletStore.self) private var store
    @Environment(AppModel.self) private var appModel

    // Local mirrors keep the controls responsive while a profile update is in flight.
    @State private var threshold = 500
    @State private var targetSymbol = "SOL"
    @State private var paused = false
    @State private var selectionTick = 0
    @State private var isAddingPurchase = false
    @State private var isReconnecting = false

    static let thresholdOptions = [200, 500, 1_000]

    var body: some View {
        NavigationStack {
            List {
                account
                bank
                roundups
                developer
                Section {
                    Button("Sign out", role: .destructive) {
                        Task { await store.signOut() }
                    }
                }
            }
            .listStyle(.insetGrouped)
            .navigationTitle("Profile")
            .onAppear(perform: syncFromProfile)
            .onChange(of: store.profile) { syncFromProfile() }
            .onChange(of: threshold) { _, value in
                guard let profile = store.profile, value != profile.thresholdCents else { return }
                selectionTick += 1
                Task {
                    await store.setThreshold(value)
                    syncFromProfile()
                }
            }
            .onChange(of: targetSymbol) { _, value in
                guard let profile = store.profile, value != profile.targetAsset else { return }
                selectionTick += 1
                Task {
                    await store.setTargetAsset(value)
                    syncFromProfile()
                }
            }
            .onChange(of: paused) { _, value in
                guard let profile = store.profile, value != profile.roundupsPaused else { return }
                Task {
                    await store.setPaused(value)
                    syncFromProfile()
                }
            }
            .sensoryFeedback(.selection, trigger: selectionTick)
            .sheet(isPresented: $isAddingPurchase) {
                TestPurchaseSheet()
            }
            .sheet(isPresented: $isReconnecting) {
                ConnectBankView(api: appModel.api) {
                    isReconnecting = false
                    Task { await store.load() }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    private var account: some View {
        Section {
            HStack(spacing: Theme.Spacing.m) {
                Image(systemName: "person.crop.circle.fill")
                    .font(.system(size: 44))
                    .symbolRenderingMode(.hierarchical)
                    .foregroundStyle(Theme.accent)
                    .accessibilityHidden(true)
                VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: Theme.Spacing.xxs) {
                    Text(displayName)
                        .font(.headline)
                    if let email = store.email {
                        Text(email)
                            .font(.subheadline)
                            .foregroundStyle(.secondary)
                    }
                }
            }
            .padding(.vertical, Theme.Spacing.xs)
            .accessibilityElement(children: .combine)
        }
    }

    private var displayName: String {
        let name = store.profile?.displayName.trimmingCharacters(in: .whitespaces) ?? ""
        return name.isEmpty ? "Droplet member" : name
    }

    private var bank: some View {
        Section("Bank") {
            LabeledContent("Institution", value: store.bank?.institutionName ?? "Not connected")
            LabeledContent("Status") {
                bankStatus
            }
            if let lastSynced = store.bank?.lastSyncedAt {
                LabeledContent("Last synced") {
                    Text(lastSynced, format: .relative(presentation: .named))
                }
            }
            if store.bank == nil || store.bank?.status == .loginRequired {
                Button(store.bank == nil ? "Connect bank" : "Reconnect bank") {
                    isReconnecting = true
                }
            }
        }
    }

    @ViewBuilder private var bankStatus: some View {
        switch store.bank?.status {
        case .active:
            statusLabel("Connected", systemImage: "checkmark.circle.fill", color: .green)
        case .loginRequired:
            statusLabel("Needs reconnect", systemImage: "exclamationmark.triangle.fill", color: .orange)
        case .error:
            statusLabel("Sync error", systemImage: "xmark.octagon.fill", color: .red)
        case nil:
            statusLabel("Not connected", systemImage: "minus.circle", color: .secondary)
        }
    }

    private func statusLabel(_ title: String, systemImage: String, color: Color) -> some View {
        HStack(spacing: Theme.Spacing.xs) {
            Image(systemName: systemImage)
                .foregroundStyle(color)
                .accessibilityHidden(true)
            Text(title)
        }
    }

    private var roundups: some View {
        Section {
            VStack(alignment: .leading, spacing: Theme.Spacing.s) {
                Text("Invest when drops reach")
                Picker("Threshold", selection: $threshold) {
                    ForEach(Self.thresholdOptions, id: \.self) { cents in
                        Text("$\(cents / 100)").tag(cents)
                    }
                }
                .pickerStyle(.segmented)
                .labelsHidden()
            }
            .padding(.vertical, Theme.Spacing.xs)
            Picker("Invest in", selection: $targetSymbol) {
                ForEach(store.assets) { asset in
                    Text(asset.name).tag(asset.symbol)
                }
            }
            Toggle("Pause round-ups", isOn: $paused)
        } header: {
            Text("Round-ups")
        } footer: {
            Text("When your drops reach \(Money.format(cents: threshold)), Droplet invests them in \(store.targetAsset?.name ?? targetSymbol). Trades are simulated.")
        }
        .disabled(store.profile == nil)
    }

    private var developer: some View {
        Section {
            Button {
                isAddingPurchase = true
            } label: {
                Label("Add test purchase", systemImage: "cart.badge.plus")
            }
            Button {
                Task { await store.sync() }
            } label: {
                HStack {
                    Label("Sync now", systemImage: "arrow.triangle.2.circlepath")
                    Spacer()
                    if store.isSyncing {
                        ProgressView()
                    }
                }
            }
            .disabled(store.isSyncing)
        } header: {
            Text("Developer")
        } footer: {
            Text("Sandbox only — creates a fake card purchase in Plaid Sandbox.")
        }
    }

    private func syncFromProfile() {
        guard let profile = store.profile else { return }
        threshold = profile.thresholdCents
        targetSymbol = profile.targetAsset
        paused = profile.roundupsPaused
    }
}

        .environment(AppModel(api: api))
        .task { await store.load() }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the tab container**

`ios/Droplet/App/MainTabs.swift`:
```swift
import SwiftUI

struct MainTabs: View {
    enum Destination: Hashable { case portfolio, spending, profile }

    @Environment(DropletStore.self) private var store
    @Environment(\.scenePhase) private var scenePhase
    @State private var selection: Destination = .spending
    @State private var celebrating: Investment?

    var body: some View {
        @Bindable var store = store
        TabView(selection: $selection) {
            Tab("Portfolio", systemImage: "chart.pie.fill", value: Destination.portfolio) {
                PortfolioView()
            }
            Tab("Spending", systemImage: "drop.fill", value: Destination.spending) {
                SpendingView()
            }
            Tab("Profile", systemImage: "person.crop.circle.fill", value: Destination.profile) {
                ProfileView()
            }
        }
        .tabBarMinimizeBehavior(.onScrollDown)
        .tabViewBottomAccessory {
            Button {
                selection = .spending
            } label: {
                NextDropPill(summary: store.summary, celebrating: celebrating)
            }
            .buttonStyle(.plain)
            .accessibilityHint("Shows your spending")
        }
        .errorBanner($store.error, retry: { Task { await store.load() } })
        .task { await store.load() }
        .onChange(of: scenePhase) { _, phase in
            if phase == .active {
                Task { await store.load() }
            }
        }
        .onChange(of: store.lastInvestment) { _, investment in
            if let investment { celebrating = investment }
        }
        .task(id: celebrating?.id) {
            guard celebrating != nil else { return }
            do {
                try await Task.sleep(for: .seconds(4))
                celebrating = nil
            } catch {
                // Cancelled because a newer investment arrived; that one owns the pill now.
            }
        }
        .sensoryFeedback(.success, trigger: store.lastInvestment) { _, new in new != nil }
        .sensoryFeedback(.error, trigger: store.error) { _, new in new != nil }
    }
}

    MainTabs()
        .environment(DropletStore(api: api))
        .environment(AppModel(api: api))
}
```

```swift
import SwiftUI

/// Cross-fades between the four routes `AppModel` decides.
struct RootView: View {
    @Environment(AppModel.self) private var appModel

    var body: some View {
        ZStack {
            switch appModel.route {
            case .launching:
                LaunchView()
                    .transition(.opacity)
            case .auth:
                AuthView(api: appModel.api)
                    .transition(.opacity)
            case .connectBank:
                ConnectBankView(api: appModel.api, onLinked: appModel.bankLinked)
                    .transition(.opacity)
            case .main:
                MainTabs()
                    .transition(.opacity)
            }
        }
        .animation(Theme.spring, value: appModel.route)
    }
}

private struct LaunchView: View {
    var body: some View {
        VStack(spacing: Theme.Spacing.l) {
            DropShape()
                .fill(Theme.accent.gradient)
                .frame(width: 44, height: 57)
                .accessibilityHidden(true)
            ProgressView()
        }
        .frame(maxWidth: .infinity, maxHeight: .infinity)
        .accessibilityElement(children: .ignore)
        .accessibilityLabel("Loading")
    }
}
```

### Task 18: Local end-to-end run in the Simulator

- Create: `docs/screenshots/local-{auth,connect,spending,portfolio,profile}.png`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - Tasks 1–2: the local stack and migrations.
  - Tasks 3–8: Edge Functions served locally.
  - Tasks 9–17: the app.
  - The UI labels from Task 17: "Add test purchase", the presets "Blue Bottle" ($5.67), "Sweetgreen" ($13.42) and "Uber" ($23.18), "Sync now", the threshold segments "$2" / "$5" / "$10".
- Produces: evidence that the whole round-up → sweep → portfolio flow works against Plaid Sandbox.

In the Simulator:
1. Enter Name `Demo`, Email `demo@droplet.dev`, Password `droplet123`, and tap the sign-up button.
2. On the Connect bank screen, take `docs/screenshots/local-connect.png`, then tap **Connect bank**.
3. In Plaid Link, continue, search for **First Platypus Bank** (a non-OAuth sandbox bank), and sign in with `user_good` / `pass_good`.
4. Choose the checking account and finish.

Expected: Link closes and the app shows the tab bar.

Then verify the database. Run:
```bash
C=$(docker ps --format '{{.Names}}' | grep -m1 supabase_db_droplet)
docker exec -i "$C" psql -U postgres -d postgres -qtA -c "select institution_name, status from public.bank_connections;" -c "select count(*) from public.plaid_items where access_token like 'access-sandbox-%';"
```
Expected: `First Platypus Bank|active` and `1`.

- [ ] **Step 5: Add test purchases and watch drops accumulate**

In the Simulator:
1. Open **Profile** and set the threshold segment to **$2**.
2. Tap **Add test purchase** and choose **Blue Bottle** ($5.67).
3. Open **Spending**.

Expected: the list shows Blue Bottle `−$5.67` / `+$0.33`, and the drop gauge reads `$0.33 of $2.00`. Take `docs/screenshots/local-spending.png`.

Then add **Sweetgreen** ($13.42 → +$0.58), **Uber** ($23.18 → +$0.82) and a custom purchase "Corner Deli" $4.51 (→ +$0.49).

Expected: once pending drops reach ≥ $2.00, that sync returns an investment, the success haptic fires, and the gauge resets toward `$0.00 of $2.00`. The total is $2.22 from these four purchases alone. Any sandbox-bank purchases dated today also count, and can trigger the investment one purchase earlier.

- [ ] **Step 6: Verify the simulated investment**

Run:
```bash
docker exec -i "$C" psql -U postgres -d postgres -qtA \
  -c "select asset_symbol, amount_cents, mode, (quantity > 0) from public.investments;" \
  -c "select roundup_status, count(*) from public.transactions group by 1 order by 1;"
```
Expected:
- At least one row `SOL|<cents>|simulated|t`, where `<cents>` is the sum of the drops it consumed (`222` when only the four test purchases count).
- No `pending` rows left over from those purchases.

Then open **Portfolio**. Expected:
- The total invested equals the sum of `amount_cents` above.
- One SOL row, with a quantity of about the amount ÷ the current SOL price.
- The "Simulated" label is visible. Take `docs/screenshots/local-portfolio.png` and `docs/screenshots/local-profile.png`.

Known demo limitations:
- **Reconnect makes a new Item.** "Reconnect bank" (shown only when Plaid reports `login_required`) runs a fresh Link session, not Plaid update mode. The new Item replaces the old row with `linked_on = today` and a fresh cursor, so purchases made between the failure and the reconnect are not rounded up.
- **Late-night window.** Plaid rejects future-dated sandbox transactions, and the reference timezone it uses is undocumented. If "Add test purchase" fails with a `PLAID_ERROR` between 00:00 and 03:00 ET, retry after 03:00 ET.


### Task 19: Deploy to the hosted Supabase project and document setup

**Files:**
- Create: `README.md`
- Modify: `ios/Droplet/Resources/Config.xcconfig` (regenerated for the remote project; gitignored)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - The Supabase project ref `REF`, from the Supabase MCP `list_projects` / `create_project` (free tier) or from the Dashboard URL.
  - A logged-in Supabase CLI. The user runs `supabase login` once, or exports `SUPABASE_ACCESS_TOKEN`.
  - Everything from Tasks 1–18.
- Produces:
  - Remote Edge Functions at `https://$REF.supabase.co/functions/v1/*`.
  - Remote secrets.
  - The `droplet-sync-all` cron job, active.
  - The app pointed at the remote project.

- [ ] **Step 1: Link the project**

Run:
```bash
export REF=<project-ref from the Supabase MCP or dashboard>
supabase link --project-ref "$REF"
```
Expected: `Finished supabase link.` It prompts for the database password, which the user types themselves.

- [ ] **Step 2: Push migrations and auth config**

Run:
```bash
supabase db push && supabase config push
```
Expected: the four migrations are applied. `config push` shows the auth diff, including `enable_confirmations = false`. Confirm with `y`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Set function secrets (values never echoed)**

Run:
```bash
grep -v '^PLAID_WEBHOOK_URL=' supabase/functions/.env > /tmp/droplet-remote.env
echo "PLAID_WEBHOOK_URL=https://$REF.supabase.co/functions/v1/plaid-webhook" >> /tmp/droplet-remote.env
supabase secrets set --env-file /tmp/droplet-remote.env && rm -f /tmp/droplet-remote.env
supabase secrets list | grep -E 'PLAID_CLIENT_ID|PLAID_SECRET|PLAID_ENV|PLAID_WEBHOOK_URL|CRON_SECRET' | wc -l
```
Expected: `5`.

- [ ] **Step 4: Deploy all functions**

Run:
```bash
supabase functions deploy --project-ref "$REF"
curl -s -X POST "https://$REF.supabase.co/functions/v1/sync-all" -H 'Content-Type: application/json' -d '{}' | jq -c .
```
Expected: six functions deployed. `sync-all` without the cron header returns `{"error":{"code":"UNAUTHORIZED",...}}`, which proves `verify_jwt = false` took effect and the secret check runs.

- [ ] **Step 5: Store the cron Vault secrets so the 15-minute job fires**

Run:
```bash
CRON_SECRET=$(grep '^CRON_SECRET=' supabase/functions/.env | cut -d= -f2-)
supabase db query --linked "select vault.create_secret('https://$REF.supabase.co/functions/v1', 'droplet_functions_url'); select vault.create_secret('$CRON_SECRET', 'droplet_cron_secret');" >/dev/null && echo stored
supabase db query --linked "select jobname, schedule, active from cron.job where jobname = 'droplet-sync-all';"
```
Expected: `stored`, then a row `droplet-sync-all | */15 * * * * | t`.

- [ ] **Step 6: Point the app at the remote project and re-run the happy path**

Run:
```bash
SUPABASE_URL="https://$REF.supabase.co" SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY="$(supabase projects api-keys --project-ref "$REF" -o json | jq -r '[.[] | select(.type=="publishable" or .name=="anon")][0].api_key')" scripts/make-ios-config.sh
cd ios && xcodebuild -project Droplet.xcodeproj -scheme Droplet -destination 'platform=iOS Simulator,name=iPhone 17' -derivedDataPath build/dd build 2>&1 | grep -E 'error:|\*\* BUILD'; cd ..
xcrun simctl install booted ios/build/dd/Build/Products/Debug-iphonesimulator/Droplet.app && xcrun simctl launch booted com.droplet.app
```
If `supabase projects api-keys` prints no publishable or anon key, copy the publishable key from **Dashboard → Project Settings → API Keys** into `SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY`. It is public by design.

Expected: `** BUILD SUCCEEDED **` and the app launches. Then repeat Task 18 Steps 4–5 against the remote project: sign up, link `user_good`, and add Blue Bottle. Expected: Spending shows `+$0.33`.

Then check that webhooks arrive. Run:
```bash
supabase functions logs plaid-webhook --project-ref "$REF" 2>/dev/null | tail -5 || echo "check Dashboard → Edge Functions → plaid-webhook → Logs"
```
Expected: within a few minutes of linking, a `SYNC_UPDATES_AVAILABLE` delivery logged with status 200.

- [ ] **Step 7: Write the README**

Create `README.md`:
````markdown
# Droplet

Round up every purchase. Your spare change becomes drops, and drops become crypto.

Droplet links a bank with **Plaid** (Sandbox) and rounds every settled purchase up to the next dollar. When your drops reach your threshold ($2 / $5 / $10), it records a **simulated** investment in the asset you choose, priced from **True Markets**' live public price feed. No real trades are placed.

## Stack
- iOS 26+ SwiftUI app (`ios/`, generated with XcodeGen), using supabase-swift and Plaid LinkKit 7
- Supabase backend (`supabase/`): Postgres + RLS, Deno Edge Functions, pg_cron

## Run locally
1. `brew install supabase/tap/supabase xcodegen deno xcbeautify`, and start Docker.
2. `cp supabase/functions/.env.example supabase/functions/.env`, then fill in your Plaid **Sandbox** client id and secret and a random `CRON_SECRET`.
3. `supabase start && supabase db reset && supabase test db`
4. `supabase functions serve --env-file supabase/functions/.env`
5. `scripts/make-ios-config.sh && cd ios && xcodegen generate && open Droplet.xcodeproj`, then run on an iPhone simulator.
6. Sign up, connect **First Platypus Bank** with `user_good` / `pass_good`, then use **Profile → Add test purchase**.

## Tests
- Database: `supabase test db`
- Edge Functions: `cd supabase/functions && deno task test`
- iOS: `cd ios && xcodebuild test -project Droplet.xcodeproj -scheme Droplet -destination 'platform=iOS Simulator,name=iPhone 17' | xcbeautify`

## Deploy
See Task 19 in `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-24-droplet.md`. In short:
- `supabase link`, then `db push` and `config push`
- `secrets set --env-file`, then `functions deploy`
- store the Vault secrets `droplet_functions_url` and `droplet_cron_secret`
- `SUPABASE_URL=… SUPABASE_PUBLISHABLE_KEY=… scripts/make-ios-config.sh`

## Security
- The Plaid secret and access tokens never leave the server. `plaid_items` has RLS with no client policies.
- The app only holds the Supabase URL and the publishable key.
- The True Markets key is **not used**: trades are simulated until live trading is added.
````
